Through CPT followed by supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on Qwen3.5-4B-Base, PMC-InterCPT effectively improves medical and general multimodal performance while using fewer CPT tokens than the raw source pool. The experimental results also illustrate the complementarity between the data quality and modality for medical multimodal CPT.

</details>


### [76] [MENTIS: What Belief Changes Under Alignment? Measuring Multi-Scale Latent Torsion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01060)
*Partha Pratim Saha,Samarth Raina,Mayur Parvatikar,Amit Dhanda,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Amitava Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MENTIS框架，通过几何结构分析揭示偏好对齐（PA）在大型语言模型内部计算中留下的结构化、深度定位的几何特征。研究发现对齐变化具有选择性：规范性概念比事实性概念扭转更大，扭转与上下文熵负相关，且峰值效应集中在架构特定的中后层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐虽改善了LLM的行为表现，但内部计算如何改变尚不明确。行为级评估无法揭示模型在越狱攻击、提示注入等场景下的脆弱性根源，因此需要从内部计算几何结构角度理解对齐效应。

Method: 提出MENTIS框架，包含三种测量工具：基于协方差的层间扭转范数T1、谱扭转诊断T2、以及能量-辐射-激活深度定位度量ERA。在四个7-8B模型对上使用LITMUS数据集进行分析。

Result: 对齐引发的内部变化具有选择性：规范性概念比事实性概念的扭转变化更大；扭转与上下文熵呈负相关；峰值效应集中在架构特定的中后层。词级、提示级和模型级分析均呈现一致模式。

Conclusion: 偏好对齐在内部计算中留下了结构化、深度定位的几何特征，这些特征超出了行为级评估所能揭示的范围。

Abstract: Preference alignment has substantially improved the observable behavior of large language models, yet it remains unclear what alignment changes internally. Aligned systems still fail under jailbreaks, prompt injection, and retrieval-time corruption, suggesting behavior-level evaluation alone is incomplete. Post-training should leave measurable traces in internal computation. We ask: when an instruction-tuned (IT) model becomes a preference-aligned (PA) model, what geometric structure changes, where do those changes concentrate, and how selectively do they vary across concepts, prompts, and model families?
  We introduce MENTIS, a geometry-first framework for measuring alignment-induced internal reorganization in paired checkpoints. MENTIS compares IT and PA models using a primary layerwise covariance-based torsion norm (T1), a secondary spectral torsion diagnostic (T2), and an Energy-Radiance-Activation measure (ERA) for depth localization. Across four 7-8B model pairs on LITMUS, our study reveals that alignment-induced change is selective rather than uniform: normative concepts exhibit larger torsion shifts than factual concepts on average; torsion is negatively correlated with contextual entropy; and peak effects localize to architecture-specific mid-to-late layers. The same pattern appears across word-level, prompt-level, and model-level analyses. These results suggest preference alignment leaves structured, depth-localized geometric signatures in internal computation beyond what behavior-level evaluation alone can reveal.

</details>


### [77] [When Is 0.1% Enough? Analyzing the Combined Effects of Dimensionality Reduction and Quantization on Text Embedding Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01074)
*Riku Kisako,Hayato Tsukagoshi,Ryohei Sasano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了将降维和量化相结合压缩文本嵌入向量的有效性，发现联合方法比单独使用任何一种方法效果更好，某些情况下可将嵌入压缩至原始大小的0.1%而几乎不损失性能，且最优压缩策略因任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能文本嵌入模型输出高维实值向量，存储和计算成本高昂；虽然已有基于降维或量化的压缩方法，但两者结合的效果尚未被充分研究。

Method: 使用四种MTEB任务族和四种预训练嵌入模型，系统对比了降维与量化单独使用及联合使用时的压缩效果。

Result: 联合降维与量化可实现比单独使用任一方法更强的压缩效果；在某些设置下，嵌入可压缩至原始大小的0.1%且性能几乎无下降；最优压缩策略取决于具体任务。

Conclusion: 降维与量化的组合是一种更有效的文本嵌入压缩策略，但需要根据任务特性选择最优的压缩方案。

Abstract: Recent high-performing text embedding models often output high-dimensional real-valued vectors, resulting in substantial storage and computational costs. To address this issue, compression methods based on dimensionality reduction or quantization have been proposed; however, the effects of combining dimensionality reduction and quantization have not been sufficiently investigated. In this paper, we systematically examine the effectiveness of compressing text embeddings by combining dimensionality reduction and quantization, using four MTEB task families and four pretrained embedding models. The experimental results demonstrate that combining dimensionality reduction and quantization enables substantially stronger compression than using either method alone, that in some settings embeddings can be reduced to as little as 0.1% of their original size with almost no performance degradation, and that the optimal compression strategy depends on the task.

</details>


### [78] [On the Generalization Gap in Self-Evolving Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01075)
*Zhenting Qi,Susanna Maria Baby,Stefanie Anna Baby,Kan Yuan,Andrew Tomkins,Tu Vu,Da-Cheng Juan,Cyrus Rashtchian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在严格闭环设置下，大语言模型通过自我进化（仅使用未标注提示集和基础模型）所能达到的性能上限。在Knights and Knaves逻辑推理任务上的实验表明，自我进化虽能持续提升基础模型性能，但最终会陷入平台期，与Oracle监督训练之间存在显著差距。多轮批评-修订机制在大模型上表现接近Oracle监督，但整体上内部生成的监督信号仍不足以替代外部监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在严格闭环设置下（仅使用未标注提示集和基础模型），大语言模型通过自我进化（即利用模型自身生成的监督信号进行训练）能否接近甚至达到Oracle监督训练的效果，从而明确自我进化的能力边界。

Method: 在统一的离线自我进化框架下，分析四种代表性策略：单轮验证、带反馈的多轮修订、迭代训练和课程学习。主要使用Knights and Knaves逻辑推理任务作为测试平台，该任务具有确定性解、可控难度和易到难泛化的特性。

Result: 自我进化能持续提升基础模型性能，但投入过多训练计算后会进入平台期，与Oracle监督之间存在显著差距。多轮批评-修订机制在大模型上表现最优，Gemma 12B几乎匹配Oracle监督训练效果。在真实推理基准上，自我进化的提升较为有限。

Conclusion: 闭环自我进化在一定条件下能帮助提升模型性能，但内部生成的监督信号在最小化设置下仍然不足以替代外部Oracle监督，存在根本性局限。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) can improve through self-evolution (SE), using supervision signals generated by the model itself. In this work, we ask: under a strict closed-loop setup, where the self-evolution algorithm has access only to an unlabeled prompt set and a base model, how close can internally generated supervision come to oracle-supervised training? We analyze four representative strategies in a unified offline self-evolution framework: single-round verification, multi-turn revision with feedback, iterative training, and curriculum learning. Our primary experiments use Knights and Knaves (KK) logical reasoning tasks, which provide deterministic solutions, controlled difficulty levels, and a clean testbed for easy-to-hard generalization. We first show that self-evolution consistently improves over the base model, but plateaus after excessive training compute is invested, and eventually still leaves a non-trivial gap to oracle supervision. We find that multi-turn critic-revision with large models can reach strong self-evolution performance, with Gemma 12B nearly matching oracle-supervised training. Beyond Knights and Knaves, we also evaluate self-evolution on real-world reasoning benchmarks, where gains are also modest. Overall, our results characterize when closed-loop self-evolution can help and show how internally generated supervision remains insufficient under this minimal formulation.

</details>


### [79] [Deep Research as Rubric for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01091)
*Wangyi Mei,Zhouhong Gu,Zhenhan Bai,Yin Cai,Lefan Zhang,Zhenxin Ding,Bo Chen,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DR-rubric提出两阶段框架，通过多轮智能体搜索构建原子化可验证规则，用于GRPO策略优化。实验表明，自举生成的规则经三次迭代后达到最优性能，证明将规则构建从静态模板重构为证据驱动的研究过程，能为开放式任务提供更可扩展的细粒度奖励信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式推理和长文本生成任务缺乏可靠的自动验证信号用于基于奖励的策略优化。现有评分标准方法将规则视为给定工件（手工或提示生成），忽略了任务特定的知识密集型维度，导致奖励信号失真。

Method: 两阶段框架：Stage I通过迭代多轮智能体搜索提取领域事实、结构约束和失败模式；Stage II将证据蒸馏为原子化、可独立验证的约束，用于GRPO策略优化。支持自举生成（训练模型自身作为规则生成器），无需前沿模型辅助。

Result: 在6个基准上，仅用1K-3K训练实例即达强竞争性能。GPT-5生成规则在智能体任务广度覆盖上最优，Gemini生成规则在智能体与专家推理任务间最平衡，自举规则经三次迭代后实现专业化到再平衡的演化，达到整体最佳性能。

Conclusion: 将规则构建从静态评估模板重构为证据驱动的研究过程，能够为开放式任务产生更可扩展、更细粒度的奖励信号。

Abstract: Open-ended reasoning and long-form generation tasks lack reliable automatic verification signals for reward-based policy optimization. Rubrics offer a promising alternative, but existing approaches treat them as given artifacts -- either hand-crafted or prompt-generated -- and often miss the task-specific, knowledge-intensive dimensions that matter most, distorting the reward signal. Our key observation is that rubric construction is itself a research problem: identifying what makes a response correct or insightful requires discovering and synthesizing external knowledge. We propose Deep Research as Rubric (DR-rubric), a two-stage framework for constructing such rubrics. Stage I elicits domain facts, structural constraints, and failure modes through iterative multi-turn agentic search; Stage II distills this evidence into atomic, independently verifiable constraints for GRPO-based policy optimization. Because the model under training can serve as its own rubric generator, DR-rubric-8B supports bootstrap rubric generation without frontier-model assistance. We evaluate on 6 benchmarks spanning agentic research and expert reasoning. Experiments show that DR-Rubric achieves strong competitive performance with only 1K -- 3K training instances, where GPT-5-generated rubrics particularly benefit breadth coverage on agentic tasks, Gemini-generated rubrics yield the most balanced performance across agentic and expert reasoning tasks, and bootstrap rubrics exhibit a specialization-to-rebalancing evolution achieving the best overall performance at the third iteration. Results demonstrate that reframing rubric construction from static evaluation templates into an evidence-driven research process yields more scalable, fine-grained reward signals for open-ended tasks.

</details>


### [80] [MiCU: End-to-End Smart Home Command Understanding with Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01099)
*Haowei Han,Kexin Hu,Weiwei Cai,Debiao Zhang,Bin Qin,Yuxiang Wang,Jiawei Jiang,Xiao Yan,Bo Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MiCU，一种面向智能家居命令理解的领域特定大语言模型。通过自动数据合成、课程学习、冷启动强化学习及令牌压缩技术，MiCU在设备控制任务上平均准确率提升20.01%，已在小米Home APP部署，每日约170万页面浏览量，用户纠正率降低1.57%，人工审核准确率提升32.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有命令理解系统在精确指令上表现良好，但在模糊或错位命令（如"让卧室更温馨"）上效果不佳。大语言模型虽有泛化优势，但受限于领域数据稀缺、任务适配不足和高计算成本。

Method: (1) 利用用户日志和大语言模型自动合成训练数据；(2) 采用课程学习注入领域知识；(3) 结合冷启动训练与领域特定思维规则引导的强化学习增强推理能力；(4) 引入令牌压缩技术将设备描述压缩为单个特殊令牌，降低推理开销，提出高效变体model-fast。

Result: 在全部设备类别上平均准确率提升20.01%；每日约170万页面浏览量；用户纠正率降低1.57%；人工审核准确率提升32.05%。

Conclusion: MiUC通过数据合成、课程学习、强化学习和令牌压缩等技术，有效提升了智能家居中模糊命令的理解能力，并在实际部署中取得了显著效果。

Abstract: Command understanding systems in smart home ecosystems can automate device control and substantially improve user experience. However, while they perform well on precise utterances (e.g., "turn on the bedroom light"), they struggle with ambiguous or misaligned commands (e.g., "make the bedroom cozy"). Large language models (LLMs) generalize well across various domains and can outperform traditional rule-based systems on such tasks, but their effectiveness is often constrained by scarce domain-specific data, insufficient task-specific adaptation, and high computational costs. In this paper, we propose an automated training data synthesis workflow using user logs and LLMs; then we build MiCU, a domain-specific LLM that excels at command understanding. Specifically, we employ curriculum learning to inject domain knowledge into the base LLM, then we enhance its reasoning ability via cold-start training combined with reinforcement learning (RL) guided by domain-specific thinking rules. Additionally, we introduce a token compression technique that condenses device description into a single special token, substantially reducing inference overhead and enabling \model-fast, an efficient variant optimized for long inputs. Extensive experiments show that MiCU significantly outperforms baselines, with an average accuracy gain of 20.01% across all device categories. We have deployed MiCU in the Xiaomi Home app, receiving approximately 1.7 million page views per day. Production evaluations show that MiCU reduces user correction rate by 1.57% and increases human audited accuracy by 32.05%. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/xiaomi-research/iot_spec_llm

</details>


### [81] [From Outliers to Errors: Auditing Pali-to-English LLM Translations with Multi-Reference Adjudication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01136)
*Máté Metzger,Nadnapang Phophichit,Hansa Dhammahaso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对古典语言翻译质量审计的方法，通过多个人类译文构建参考包络面，利用嵌入漂移作为分流信号而非错误标签，对四个主流LLM的巴利-英译输出进行审计。结果显示漂移值预测错误严重性而非错误本身，GPT-5.5在尾部错误率最低，Grok 4.3错误率最高，主要错误类型为遗漏、截断和教义术语错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单分数翻译指标会将合理变体与错误混为一谈，这一问题在古典语言翻译中尤为严重，因为同一段落存在多种合理的英文译法。现有评估方法缺少处理多参考译文共存场景的审计机制。

Method: 使用三位资深译者（Bhikkhu Sujato, Thanissaro Bhikkhu, Bhikkhu Bodhi）的译文构建参考包络面，计算候选译文的归一化嵌入漂移值；对超过1.5阈值的1203个候选译文，采用盲法三模型LLM评审团进行裁定，并用300实例的作者裁定的验证集进行校准。

Result: 漂移值预测严重性而非错误：1.5-2.0区间的重大错误率仅7.9%，约80%为有效变体；大于3.0的区间重大错误率达51.6%。GPT-5.5高漂移区间错误率最低，Grok 4.3异常值最多且尾部错误率最高（总体27.6%，大于3.0时达74.4%）。主要错误类型为遗漏/截断和教义术语错误。

Conclusion: 贡献了一种可复用的古典-现代翻译审计设计：从多个人类译者定义参考包络面，用嵌入漂移优先排序审查，对标记的尾部进行裁定而非将异常值视为错误。该方法可推广至其他古典语言翻译质量评估场景。

Abstract: Single-score translation metrics can conflate legitimate variation with error, a problem especially acute for classical languages where multiple defensible English renderings of the same passage coexist. We audit Pali-to-English output from four flagship large language models (LLMs): GPT-5.5, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Grok 4.3, on 1,700 passages from the Pali Canon, using three established human translations by Bhikkhu Sujato, Thanissaro Bhikkhu, and Bhikkhu Bodhi as a local reference envelope rather than a single gold standard. Each candidate's normalized embedding drift from the reference centroid serves as a triage signal, not an error label; the 1,203 candidates above a 1.5 drift threshold are then adjudicated by a blinded three-model LLM judge panel, calibrated against a 300-instance author-adjudicated validation set. Two results stand out. First, drift predicts severity rather than error per se: the major-error rate among adjudicated high-drift candidates rose monotonically from 7.9% in the 1.5-2.0 band to 51.6% above 3.0, while approximately 80% of 1.5-2.0 outliers were judged valid translation variations. Second, model differences were clearest in the high-drift tail: GPT-5.5 had the lowest adjudicated high-drift major-error rate, with confidence intervals overlapping those of Claude Sonnet 4.6 and Gemini 3.1 Pro; Grok 4.3 had both the largest outlier volume and the highest tail major-error rate (27.6% overall, 74.4% above drift 3.0). The dominant major-error categories (e.g. omission or truncation, doctrinal term errors) are precisely the failures most likely to mislead readers of doctrinal text. The contribution is a reusable audit design for classical-to-modern translation: define a local reference envelope from multiple human translators, use embedding drift to prioritize review, and adjudicate the flagged tail rather than treating outlier status as error.

</details>


### [82] [Not All Explanations Simulate Equally: Comparing Verbalized Feature Attributions and Self-Generated Rationales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01148)
*Pingjun Hong,Benjamin Roth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种自然语言解释（特征归因解释与自生成理由）在反事实模拟中预测模型行为的效果，发现解释格式和粒度对可模拟性有显著影响，且效果因模型和格式而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言解释常被视为理解模型行为的统一接口，但不同来源的解释可能以不同方式支持对模型的模拟（simulation）。现有研究缺乏对多种解释类型在统一框架下的系统性比较。

Method: 采用反事实模拟（counterfactual simulation）设定，以LLM作为预测器（judge），将特征归因解释（verbalized feature attributions）与自生成理由（self-generated rationales）作为输入，测量LLM能否据此预测模型对后续问题的答案。在多个指令微调模型上，分析解释来源、口头化策略（verbalization strategy）和特征粒度（feature granularity）对可模拟性的影响。

Result: 解释格式和粒度显著影响可模拟性：基于归因的解释与自生成理由在提升反事实预测能力上存在差异，且效果在不同模型和格式间发生变化。

Conclusion: 不同的解释类型对模型行为的可模拟性影响不同，选择合适的解释格式和粒度对于有效理解和预测模型行为至关重要。

Abstract: Natural-language explanations are often treated as a unified interface for understanding model behavior, but different explanation sources may support simulation in different ways. This paper compares two families of explanations for question answering models: verbalized feature attributions and self-generated rationales. We evaluate them under a shared counterfactual simulation setting, using an LLM judge as predictor and measuring whether it can better predict a model's answers to follow-up questions when given its explanation. Across multiple instruction-tuned models, we analyze how explanation source, verbalization strategy, and feature granularity affect the simulatability of explanations. Our results show that explanation format and granularity affect simulatability: attribution-based explanations and self-generated rationales differ in how much they improve counterfactual prediction, with effects that vary across models and formats.

</details>


### [83] [Thinking Economically: A Hierarchical Framework for Adaptive-Complexity Reasoning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01168)
*Yubo Gao,Haotian Wu,Hong Chen,Junquan Huang,Yibo Yan,Jungang Li,Zihao Dongfang,Sicheng Tao,Puay Siew Tan,Jie Zhang,Xuming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HAB是一个分层自适应预算框架，通过粗粒度（问题级）和细粒度（步骤级）的推理预算分配，在保持甚至提升准确率的同时显著降低CoT推理的token消耗，实现了更优的性能-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CoT推理存在"过度思考"问题，即生成过长的推理链却未带来相应的精度提升。现有效率方法通常采用统一的压缩策略，忽视了推理复杂度在不同问题间和同一问题不同步骤间存在异质性这一关键观察。

Method: 提出分层自适应预算器(HAB)训练框架：层间层面预测每个问题的最优推理深度；层内层面通过PPL导出的步骤比较学习步骤特定的token预算信号，结合自适应Pareto优化目标捕获局部质量-效率权衡，并使用Fisher信息剪枝器提供细粒度训练指导，促使生成器内化更经济的推理模式。

Result: 在GSM8K和MATH500数据集上，HAB不仅超越了标准CoT的准确率，还减少了token使用量，实现了比对比基线更强的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: "经济思考"原则是有效的：根据任务和步骤的内在需求智能分配计算资源，而非追求统一的简洁性，能够在提升效率的同时不牺牲甚至提升推理质量。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) has significantly enhanced LLM reasoning, yet often incurs substantial computational overhead due to "overthinking": generating excessively long rationales without commensurate accuracy gains. Existing efficiency methods typically apply uniform compression, which overlooks a critical observation that reasoning complexity is heterogeneous at two distinct granularity: across different problems and within individual reasoning steps. This motivates our principle of Thinking Economically: intelligently allocating computational resources based on intrinsic task and step demands rather than pursuing uniform brevity. We propose Hierarchical Adaptive Budgeter (HAB), a training framework that operationalizes this principle through coarse-to-fine budgeting. At the inter-step level, HAB predicts the optimal reasoning depth for each problem. At the intra-step level, HAB learns step-specific token budgeting signals from PPL-derived step comparisons and an adaptive Pareto optimization objective that captures the local quality-efficiency trade-off, while a Fisher Information-based pruner further provides fine-grained training-time guidance, thereby encouraging the generator to internalize more economical reasoning patterns. Experiments on GSM8K and MATH500 show that HAB not only surpasses standard CoT in accuracy but also reduces token usage, achieving a stronger performance-efficiency trade-off than the compared baselines.

</details>


### [84] [CA-BED: Conversation-Aware Bayesian Experimental Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01182)
*Daniel Arnould,Rashad Aziz,Zixuan Kang,Tanav Changal,Kevin Zhu,Sunishchal Dev,Gabriel Grand,Shreyas Sunil Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CA-BED框架，将贝叶斯实验设计与LLM似然估计结合，在推理时通过模拟对话树优化问题选择，在实体推断任务中成功率提升21.8%，仅增加1.8轮对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在静态推理任务中表现优异，但在需要通过提问主动获取信息的交互场景中性能下降，关键在于如何在考虑模糊或不完整回答的情况下选择能降低不确定性的问题。

Method: 提出CA-BED（对话感知贝叶斯实验设计）框架，在推理时进行概率性对话规划：维护假设上的信念分布，通过LLM估计似然，模拟可能回答并传播期望信息增益，构建对话树来优化多轮问题选择。

Result: 在两个结构化实体推断基准上，CA-BED相比直接提示的成功率平均提升21.8%，相比其他信息寻求方法也有可比收益，仅增加平均1.8轮对话。

Conclusion: CA-BED通过贝叶斯实验设计有效提升了LLM在交互式信息获取场景中的表现，以极小的对话轮次代价实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at static reasoning tasks, yet their performance often degrades in interactive scenarios where information must be actively acquired through questioning. A key challenge lies in selecting questions that reduce uncertainty while incorporating responses that may be ambiguous or only partially informative. To address this, we propose Conversation-Aware Bayesian Experimental Design (CA-BED), an inference-time probabilistic dialog planning framework that integrates Bayesian Experimental Design with LLM-based likelihood estimation to optimize question selection over multiple conversational turns. CA-BED maintains a belief distribution over hypotheses, anticipates possible answers, and propagates expected information gain through a simulated conversation tree. Across two structured entity-deduction benchmarks, CA-BED yields an average 21.8% improvement in success rates over direct prompting, with comparable gains relative to alternative information-seeking methods. It achieves these gains with an average increase of only 1.8 conversational turns compared to direct prompting.

</details>


### [85] [Low-Resource Safety Failures Are Action Failures, Not Representation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01196)
*Rashad Aziz,Ikhlasul Akmal Hanif,Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 安全对齐在高资源语言中有效，但跨语言迁移到低资源语言时失败，原因是决策校准而非表征缺失；通过仅用1-4个目标语言样例重校准逻辑门，可将拒绝选择性从33.6提升至54.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐在高资源语言中表现良好，但在低资源语言中大幅失效，现有自适应方法（如AdaSteer、CAST）也继承了这一跨语言失败。需要诊断失效的根本原因并设计高效修复方法。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3.1-8B上覆盖23种语言，提取高资源语言的harmfulness方向表征，测试其在低资源语言上的线性可分性；设计一个低秩逻辑读出门（logistic readout），仅用1-4个目标语言样例重置决策阈值，在拒绝导向和harmfulness方向消融之间路由。

Result: 高资源语言的harmfulness方向在低资源语言上几乎同样能线性分离有害/无害提示，但有害拒绝率从87.9%骤降至43.9%；重校准方法将平均拒绝选择性从33.6提升至54.5，同时保持MMLU效用。

Conclusion: 低资源安全失败的根源是决策校准问题而非表征缺失，通过重校准现有表征而非学习新表征即可有效修复安全对齐的跨语言迁移失败。

Abstract: Safety alignment learned in high-resource languages transfers poorly to low-resource languages. Models refuse harmful prompts in English but fail to refuse when the same prompts are translated into Swahili or Burmese. Adaptive steering methods like AdaSteer and CAST inherit this failure cross-lingually. We diagnose where transfer breaks down. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B, and Llama-3.1-8B on 23 languages, the harmfulness direction extracted from high-resource activations linearly separates harmful from harmless low-resource prompts nearly as well as high-resource ones. The relevant representation is present. Yet harmful refusal drops from 87.9% to 43.9%. The model fails to convert the representation into refusal. What fails to transfer is calibration of the safety decision, not the underlying representation. We exploit this by recalibrating, rather than retraining, a high-resource gate: a low-rank logistic readout with its decision threshold reset using as few as 1 to 4 target-language examples per class. The gate routes between refusal steering and harmfulness-direction ablation, substantially raising mean refusal selectivity ($Δ$ = harmful $-$ harmless refusal) from 33.6 for the strongest adapted baseline to 54.5 while preserving MMLU utility. These results suggest that some low-resource safety failures can be repaired by recalibrating existing representations rather than learning new ones. Our code is released: https://github.com/rashadaziz/low-resource-safety.

</details>


### [86] [Implicit Geographic Inference in LLM Medical Triage: Language-Driven Disparities in Emergency Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01204)
*Qi Han Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型（Gemini 3.5 Flash）在处理相同症状但不同语言的患者描述时，会因语言本身隐含的地理位置推理而产生显著不同的医疗分诊建议（急诊推荐率0%-30%），且这种现象不是由翻译质量导致，而是模型从输入语言中推断患者所在地并据此调整建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在医疗分诊场景中是否仅因患者输入语言的不同，就对相同症状产生不一致的推荐结果，从而揭示模型中潜在的隐性语言偏见。

Method: 使用Gemini 3.5 Flash，针对一个神经症状描述（持续性头痛、视力模糊、恶心），在六种语言（英语、西班牙语、中文、印地语、日语、阿拉伯语）下各运行30次（共450次API调用），同时通过添加患者所在地信息、反向锚定（英文提示+东京地点）和回译控制（日语→英语）来隔离语言与地理位置因素的影响。

Result: 不同语言下的急诊推荐率差异巨大（日语、印地语为0%，英语、阿拉伯语为30%），但严重程度评分高度一致（7.7-8.0/10）。添加美国地点信息后，非英语提示的急诊推荐率最高提升76.7个百分点；英文提示+东京地点则使急诊率从30%降至6.7%。回译控制显示差异并非翻译质量问题。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的医疗分诊建议存在显著的基于输入语言的地理隐含偏见，模型会从语言中推断用户所在地并据此调整分诊建议，这种偏差在实际应用中可能导致严重的医疗公平性问题。

Abstract: We investigate whether large language models produce different medical triage recommendations for identical symptoms based solely on the language of the patient prompt. Using Gemini 3.5 Flash, we evaluate a neurological symptom profile (persistent headache, blurred vision, nausea) across six languages (English, Spanish, Chinese, Hindi, Japanese, Arabic) with 30 runs per condition (n=450 total API calls). We find that the model recommends emergency room visits at rates ranging from 0% (Japanese, Hindi) to 30% (English, Arabic), despite assigning nearly identical severity scores (7.7-8.0/10) across all languages. Adding a single sentence specifying the patient's US location increases ER recommendations by up to 76.7 percentage points for non-English prompts, while the reverse anchor (English prompt with a Tokyo location) reduces the ER rate from 30% to 6.7%. A back-translation control (Japanese to English) produces ER rates comparable to the English baseline, confirming that the disparity is not caused by translation quality but by implicit geographic inference from the input language. We release the complete dataset, experiment code, and results.

</details>


### [87] [Connecting the Dots: Benchmarking Reflective Memory in Long-Horizon Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01223)
*Jingjie Lin,Bingbing Wang,Zihan Wang,Zhengda Jin,Weiming Qiao,Jing Li,Ruifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RefMem-Bench基准测试集（26K问答实例，8个反思记忆维度，3种任务格式）用于评估长程对话中的反思记忆能力，并设计了REMIND层级框架（问题条件证据检索、显著性感知定位、抽象级别监督、渐进式反思对齐），实验证明该框架能有效提升模型在碎片化多模态线索中的高层语义推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文基准仅测量事实性记忆（显性回忆），无法评估模型从碎片化多模态线索中合成高层理解所需的反思记忆能力。

Method: REMIND框架采用层级化渐进式意义建构：1) 问题条件证据检索；2) 显著性感知定位；3) 抽象级别监督；4) 渐进式反思对齐（将高层反思推理蒸馏到事实推理路径中）。

Result: RefMem-Bench对现有模型构成显著挑战；REMIND通过渐进式证据感知、定位和抽象，一致提升了答案准确率和记忆召回率。

Conclusion: 反思记忆是长上下文理解中一个尚未充分研究的关键能力，REMIND框架通过层次化渐进式意义建构有效弥补了当前模型在这一维度的不足。

Abstract: Despite substantial progress in long-context modeling, existing benchmarks remain confined to factual memory for explicit recall, failing to measure the reflective memory required to synthesize fragmented, multimodal cues into high-level interpretations. To address this gap, we introduce RefMem-Bench, a benchmark for reflective memory in long-horizon dialogue. RefMem-Bench contains 26K annotated QA instances with eight reflective-memory dimensions and three task formats, requiring models to move beyond surface-level retrieval and infer latent meanings from evidence distributed across interaction histories. To enhance reflective memory capability, we propose REflective Memory INDuction (REMIND), a hierarchical framework that treats reflective memory as progressive meaning construction. REMIND couples question-conditioned evidence retrieval, salience-aware grounding, and abstraction-level supervision, and uses Progressive Reflective Alignment to distill high-level reflective reasoning into the factual inference pathway. Experiments show RefMem-Bench poses a substantial challenge to current models, while REMIND consistently improves both answer accuracy and memory recall through progressive evidence perception, grounding, and abstraction.

</details>


### [88] [Efficient RAG with Intent-Aware Retrieval and Semantics-Preserving Chunking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01240)
*Fachrina Dewi Puspitasari,Chaoning Zhang,Jiaquan Zhang,Zhicheng Wang,Hafiz Shakeel Ahmad Awan,Rizwan Qureshi,Jewon Lee,Tae-Ho Kim,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为InSemRAG的检索增强生成框架，通过意图感知检索器（IAR）和语义保护分块（SPC）两个模块，结合迭代检索-检查机制和SLM加速，解决了传统RAG系统中意图无关检索和信息碎片化导致的信息不足问题，在多项基准测试中取得了领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统由于意图无关检索（intent-agnostic retrieval）和信息碎片化（information fragmentation）两个因素导致信息不足，限制了LLM的指令遵循和推理能力。

Method: 提出InSemRAG框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 意图感知检索器（IAR），采用动态混合检索方法，基于查询意图自适应加权不同检索通道；(2) 语义保护分块（SPC），检测并修复受损的证据块以保持语义完整性。框架采用迭代检索-检查机制，并使用小语言模型（SLM）缓解计算延迟。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上取得竞争力结果：HotPotQA上F1提升2.65点，FEVER上准确率提升1.5点；使用SLM后，相比Multi-Hop RAG延迟降低4.32倍。

Conclusion: InSemRAG通过意图感知检索和语义完整性保护有效解决了传统RAG的信息不足问题，在多跳推理和证据敏感型任务上表现尤其突出，且SLM的引入在保持性能的同时显著降低了延迟。

Abstract: The demand for powerful instruction following and reasoning capability of large language models (LLMs) has promoted rapid development of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The RAG system assists LLM generation by retrieving chunks of query-fit supplementary knowledge from an external database. Conventional RAG systems, however, suffer from information insufficiency due to two factors, which are intent-agnostic retrieval and information fragmentation. Our work proposes a RAG framework, termed InSemRAG, that addresses these challenges via an iterative retrieve-and-check mechanism with two supporting modules, an intention-aware retriever (IAR) and semantics-preserving chunking (SPC). IAR implements a dynamic hybrid retrieval method that adaptively weights the retrieval channels based on the query intent, while SPC performs detection and reparation to the damaged evidence chunks to preserve the semantic integrity. To alleviate the computational latency brought by our iterative mechanism, we leverage small language models (SLMs). Extensive experiments across several benchmark datasets consistently demonstrate the competitiveness of our method against recent state-of-the-art RAG mechanisms. Particularly, our method achieves significant gains on multi-hop and evidence-sensitive tasks, with a 2.65-point improvement in F1 on HotPotQA and a 1.5-point increase in accuracy on FEVER. Our method also achieves competitive performance to Multi-Hop RAG with 4.32$\times$ lower latency with the utilization of SLM.

</details>


### [89] [Unlocking the Black Box of Latent Reasoning: An Interpretability-Guided Approach to Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01243)
*Shuochen Chang,Tong Bai,Xiaofeng Zhang,Qianli Ma,Qingyang Liu,Zhaohe Liao,Yibo Miao,Li Niu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过结构、因果和几何探针系统分析了LLM潜在推理的隐含状态，揭示了潜在向量编码了压缩且保真的推理步骤表征，并基于这些可解释性洞察提出了无需训练的解码时干预方法，通过施加几何和语义先验来优化潜在推理过程，实验表明该方法能在不更新参数的情况下持续提升推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理（Latent Reasoning）在连续隐藏状态中进行多步推理虽比显式CoT更高效，但其思维向量的不透明性阻碍了可靠性和可控性，现有方法缺乏对潜在推理过程的系统可解释性理解以及基于此的有效控制手段。

Method: 首先使用结构探针、因果探针和几何探针对潜在推理向量进行系统分析，揭示早期向量作为关键因果枢纽；然后基于这些发现设计一系列无需训练、解码时即时的干预方法，通过施加几何和语义先验来优化潜在推理过程。

Result: 在多种模型规模和多个任务领域的实验中，所提出的可解释性引导的干预方法在不更新任何参数的情况下持续提升了推理准确性。

Conclusion: 可解释性洞察可以有效指导对LLM潜在推理过程的干预控制，无需参数更新即可提升推理性能，弥合了机制可解释性与可操作控制之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Latent reasoning enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to perform multi-step inference within continuous hidden states, offering efficiency gains over explicit Chain-of-Thought (CoT). However, the opacity of these continuous thought vectors hinders their reliability and controllability. This paper bridges the gap between mechanistic interpretability and actionable control. We first present a systematic analysis using structural, causal, and geometric probes, revealing that latent vectors encode compressed, faithful representations of reasoning steps, with early vectors acting as critical causal hubs. Building on this, we operationalize these interpretability insights into a suite of training-free, decode-time interventions that refine the latent reasoning process by imposing the identified geometric and semantic priors. Extensive experiments across multiple model scales and diverse task domains demonstrate that our approaches consistently improve reasoning accuracy. Our interpretability-guided interventions consistently unlock latent capabilities and improve reasoning accuracy without any parameter updates.

</details>


### [90] [Understanding LLM Behavior in Multi-Target Cross-Lingual Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01252)
*Sangwon Ryu,Yihong Liu,Mingyang Wang,Yunsu Kim,Jungseul Ok,Gary Geunbae Lee,Hinrich Schuetze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多目标跨语言文本摘要（MTXLS）基准MEA，涵盖24种目标语言，并通过层分析揭示LLM中翻译与摘要行为在深层联合涌现，进而提出基于激活引导的推理时干预方法以提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标跨语言文本摘要（MTXLS）在用户多语言消费需求下日益重要，但该方向研究不足，缺乏标准基准和理解LLM内部机制的分析。

Method: 构建MEA基准（24种目标语言），采用端到端与流水线方法对比多种LLM；提出逐层分析框架探究LLM内部机制；设计推理时激活引导方法，利用英文摘要的隐层表示指导MTXLS生成。

Result: MTXLS性能显著落后于英文单语摘要；翻译与摘要行为在深层联合涌现而非分阶段；任务相关处理及错误均集中在相似深度；提出的激活引导方法一致提升多目标语言摘要质量。

Conclusion: MTXLS仍具挑战，LLM内部翻译与摘要并非独立阶段，而可在深层通过激活引导加以联合优化，从而提升多语言摘要效果。

Abstract: Multi-target cross-lingual text summarization (MTXLS), which summarizes a source document into multiple target languages, is increasingly important as users consume content in diverse languages, but remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce multi-target cross-lingual element-aware (MEA), a new MTXLS benchmark covering 24 target languages. We benchmark end-to-end and pipeline approaches across various LLMs and show that MTXLS performance still substantially lags behind English monolingual summarization. To better understand MTXLS in LLMs, we propose a layer-wise analysis framework for investigating how LLMs internally perform MTXLS. Our analyses suggest that translation and summarization behaviors emerge jointly within later layers rather than as distinctly decomposed stages. Most task-relevant processing occurs within these layers, and errors also tend to arise at similar depths. Motivated by these findings, we introduce an inference-time activation steering method that leverages hidden representations from English summarization to guide MTXLS generation. Experiments show that our method consistently improves MTXLS quality across target languages.

</details>


### [91] [Agentic Clustering: Controllable Text Taxonomies via Multi-Agent Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01255)
*Simon Löwe,Emily Silcock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于智能体的文本聚类方法，通过编排多个专门智能体（提议者、合成者、审计者、调查者、评论者）动态调整聚类流程，在7个公开基准上达到SOTA，ARI最高提升32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的文本聚类管线采用程序化固定流程，难以适配不同结构的语料库，也无法灵活融入用户约束（如目标簇数、聚类意图）。

Method: 采用编排器LLM在每一步检查聚类发现状态，动态调度五个专门智能体（proposer、synthesizer、auditor、investigator、critic），根据语料自适应调整管线而非执行固定流程。

Result: 在7个公开文本聚类基准上达到最先进性能，在ARI指标上比最强LLM基线提升高达32%。

Conclusion: 基于智能体的自适应聚类方法相比固定程序化管线，能更好地泛化到不同结构的语料，并支持用户约束的灵活融入。

Abstract: Recent text-clustering methods use large language models to propose a cluster taxonomy from a corpus and then assign each text to it. These pipelines are fundamentally programmatic: the sequence of LLM calls and the rules for stopping, merging, and splitting clusters are fixed in code in advance, so they generalise poorly across corpora of different structure and cannot easily incorporate user-supplied constraints such as a target cluster count or a clustering intent. We propose an agentic alternative in which an orchestrator LLM inspects the state of the discovery process at each step and dispatches one of a small set of specialised agents - proposer, synthesizer, auditor, investigator, and critic - adapting the pipeline to the corpus rather than executing a fixed one. On seven public text-clustering benchmarks the method achieves state-of-the-art performance, beating the strongest prior LLM baseline by up to 32% in ARI.

</details>


### [92] [IndoBias: A Dual Track Culturally Grounded Benchmark for LLMs Bias Evaluation in Indonesian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01260)
*Ikhlasul Akmal Hanif,Muhammad Falensi Azmi,Filbert Aurelian Tjiaranata,Eryawan Presma Yulianrifat,Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IndoBias基准，评估LLM在印尼及三种地方语言（爪哇语、巽他语、马卡萨语）中的偏见，发现现有模型在印尼语中偏向原型句，地方语言在意识形态和宗教类别中存在更高偏见，且预训练数据来源（Common Crawl vs. 人工审核文本）及引入地方语言均会影响偏见程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印尼拥有1300多个民族和700种本土语言，但LLM在该区域的偏见研究严重缺失，现有工作未能覆盖其独特的多元文化、多语言社会语境，存在评估代表性公平性和本地化刻板印象的关键空白。

Method: 构建IndoBias基准，涵盖印尼语及爪哇语、巽他语、马卡萨语三种地方语言；设计双视角评估：深度视角（对比对）和广度视角（基于生成），后者基于社会学框架（SPI、O*NET、WGI）。

Result: 1）现有LLM（尤其解码器模型）在印尼语中对原型句存在强偏见；2）地方语言在意识形态和宗教类别中偏见更高；3）LLM对地方实体的刻板印象极性非均匀；4）Common Crawl文本比人工审核文本（如Wikipedia、News）引入更多偏见，引入地方语言到预训练普遍增加偏见。

Conclusion: 强调在文化特定语境中研究偏见的重要性，IndoBias为评估印尼多元文化背景下的LLM公平性提供了系统化基准。

Abstract: Despite being home to more than 1300 ethnic groups and 700 indigenous languages, bias in Large Language Models has not been fully studied in Indonesia, thus leaving a critical gap in evaluating representational fairness and localized stereotypes within its uniquely vast, multilingual, and diverse sociocultural landscape. To address this, we introduce IndoBias as a culturally-grounded bias benchmark to assess LLMs bias in Indonesian and three local languages: Javanese, Sundanese, and Makasar. IndoBias features dual perspective evaluation tracks: depth-oriented (with contrastive-pairs) and breadth-oriented (with generation-based), where the latter is grounded in social science frameworks (SPI, O*NET, and WGI). Our results show that existing LLMs -- particularly decoder models -- exhibit strong bias towards prototypical sentences in Indonesian, while local languages suffer higher bias under Ideology and Religion category. We also find that LLMs responses exhibit a non-uniform Stereotype Polarity when prompted with various local entities. Finally, we discover that, in Indonesian, Common Crawl texts introduce more bias during pretraining, compared to human-reviewed article texts (e.g., Wikipedia, News), whereas introducing local languages to pretraining generally increases bias. This work highlights the importance of studying bias in culture-specific context. Warning: This paper contains example data that may be offensive, harmful, or biased.

</details>


### [93] [Worlds Within Words: Translating Culture in Ancient Chinese Texts with Multi-Agent Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01276)
*Xiaoqi He,Kaixin Lan,Mu You,Tao Fang,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MACAT多智能体文化感知翻译框架，将古汉语文化负载词翻译建模为选择性显化任务，通过动态识别文化关键短语并注入简洁解释性知识，在中医经典和《论语》翻译中优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型机器翻译在处理古汉语文化负载词时面临两难困境：直译保留表面形式但丢失深层文化概念，过度显化则损害简洁性和可读性。现有方法缺乏何时以及如何注入文化知识的动态决策机制。

Method: 提出MACAT框架：(1) 将文化负载词翻译建模为选择性显化任务；(2) 多智能体协作：动态识别文化显著短语并注入简洁解释；(3) 质量感知重排序模块进行候选选择；(4) 多轮评估智能体从术语精确性、可读性、忠实度、文化保留和文化显化五个维度评估翻译。

Result: 在100份中医文献和《论语》20章子集上，统一GPT-5.4评估设置下，MACAT在术语精确性、可读性、忠实度、文化保留和文化显化五个维度上一致优于骨干模型和通用机器翻译基线。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作框架能有效平衡文化负载词翻译中文化知识显化与文本简洁性之间的矛盾，为古汉语文化翻译提供了一种动态、可控的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based machine translation has advanced cross-cultural communication, yet it still struggles with culture-loaded words (CLWs) in ancient Chinese texts. The challenge extends beyond lexical alignment to deciding when and how culture-dependent knowledge should be explicated for readers lacking relevant background. Literal translation often preserves surface forms while missing underlying concepts, whereas over-explicitation harms conciseness and readability. To address this problem, we formulate CLW translation as a selective explicitation task and propose \textbf{MACAT}, a \textbf{M}ulti-\textbf{A}gent \textbf{C}ulture-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{T}ranslation framework that dynamically identifies culturally salient phrases and injects concise explanatory knowledge when necessary. MACAT further incorporates a quality-aware reranking module for candidate selection and a multi-round evaluation agent that assesses translations across terminological precision, readability, fidelity, cultural preservation, and cultural explicitation. Experiments on traditional Chinese medicine (TCM) classics and the \textit{Analects} show that, under a unified GPT-5.4 evaluation setting, MACAT consistently outperforms both the backbone model and general-purpose MT baselines on 100 TCM documents and a 20-chapter subset of the \textit{Analects}.

</details>


### [94] [Don't Read Everything: A Curvature-Conditioned Query for Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01294)
*Dong Le,Thong Nguyen,Cong-Duy Nguyen,Anh Tuan Luu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Curvature-Conditioned Query (CCQ)机制，通过对查询向量进行基于记忆协方差矩阵的曲率条件化压缩，在不改变线性注意力写操作的情况下改进读操作，显著提升检索和长上下文任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力通过循环快速权重状态降低了softmax注意力的二次复杂度，但在上下文检索和长上下文任务上持续落后于softmax注意力。现有方法通过门控、delta更新或核特征图改进写操作，但读操作保持不变——每个过去的键都加性地贡献到输出，导致有用目标被大量存储向量稀释。

Method: 借鉴softmax几何特性，在softmax对数配分函数的各向同性注意力点进行二阶泰勒展开，得到局部二次模型，其曲率恰好等于运行中的键协方差矩阵。该协方差矩阵可用与线性注意力状态相同的循环/分块机制维护。相关联的线性算子在查询读取状态之前，沿记忆的高密度方向将其压缩。该机制称为曲率条件化查询(CCQ)，仅修改读步骤，可与任何线性注意力骨干组合。

Result: 将CCQ附加到GLA和Gated DeltaNet上，在困惑度、零样本下游准确率、S-NIAH检索（训练上下文内及超越）、从4K到20K的长度外推困惑度以及LongBench准确率上均有提升，且额外开销很小。

Conclusion: CCQ是一种轻量级、即插即用的读端改进机制，通过曲率条件化查询有效解决了线性注意力中记忆稀释问题，弥合了其与softmax注意力在检索和长上下文任务上的差距。

Abstract: Linear attention reduces the quadratic cost of softmax attention by maintaining a recurrent fast-weight state, but it consistently lags on in-context retrieval and long-context tasks. Existing remedies act on the write side of memory through gating, delta updates, or kernel feature maps, but the read step is left unchanged: every past key contributes additively to the output, so useful targets are diluted by the bulk of stored vectors. We borrow one specific piece of softmax's geometry to construct a cheap read-time contraction of the query. A second-order Taylor expansion of the softmax log-partition at the isotropic-attention point gives a local quadratic model whose curvature coincides with the running key covariance, a quantity that can be maintained with the same recurrent/chunkwise mechanism as the linear-attention state. The associated linear operator contracts the query along the high-density directions of memory before it reads the state. We call this mechanism Curvature-Conditioned Query (CCQ). CCQ modifies only the read step and is composable with any linear-attention backbone. Attached to GLA and Gated DeltaNet, it improves perplexity, zero-shot downstream accuracy, S-NIAH retrieval at and beyond the training context, length-extrapolation perplexity from 4K to 20K, and LongBench accuracy, at small extra cost.

</details>


### [95] [Challenger at MultiPRIDE: Is It Hate Speech or Reclaimed?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01298)
*Hadi Bayrami Asl Tekanlou,Mahdi Bakhtiyarzadeh,Jafar Razmara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单、可解释的方法，通过生成稠密语义文本嵌入、结合Cleanlab标签噪声过滤（逻辑回归）和MLP神经网络，在MultiPride共享任务中区分仇恨言论与回收性语言，在低计算资源下实现了稳健性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数字环境中仇恨言论的传播日益严重，自动检测虽取得进展，但区分真正的仇恨言论与回收性语言（被边缘群体重新赋权的语言）仍是一个关键挑战，因为这类表达具有细微且上下文依赖的特性。

Method: 生成稠密语义文本嵌入，引入基于Cleanlab（使用逻辑回归）的标签噪声过滤阶段，然后采用多层感知机（MLP）神经网络进行最终分类。系统设计注重低计算资源消耗和可解释性。

Result: 尽管数据集存在极端类别不平衡问题，实验结果表明该方法表现稳健。评估指标包括精确率、召回率和F1分数（含宏平均指标）。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了在保持可解释性的前提下，通过更大规模的嵌入模型和更先进的预处理技术仍有进一步改进的潜力。

Abstract: The spread of hate speech has become increasingly harmful in modern digital environments, particularly on social networking platforms. While recent advances have shown promising results in automatic hate speech detection, a key challenge remains: distinguishing genuine hate speech from reclaimed language. Accurate labeling is difficult due to the nuanced and context-dependent nature of reclaimed expressions. In this paper, we present a simple and interpretable approach for distinguishing hate speech from reclaimed language, developed for the MultiPride Shared Task. Our method generates dense semantic text embeddings and incorporates a label-noise filtering stage using Cleanlab with logistic regression, followed by a Multi-layer Perceptron (MLP) neural network for final classification. The system is designed to operate under limited computational resources while maintaining strong performance. We evaluate our approach using precision, recall, and F1-score, including macro-averaged metrics. Experimental results demonstrate robust performance despite extreme class imbalance in the dataset. Overall, the findings highlight the potential for further improvements through larger embedding models and more advanced preprocessing techniques while preserving interpretability.

</details>


### [96] [Med-HEAL: Analyzing and Mitigating Hallucinations in Medical LLMs with Hallucination-Aware In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01301)
*Yiming Liao,Zeno Franco,Jose Eduardo Lizarraga Mazaba,Keke Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Med-HEAL是一个系统框架，用于识别、分析和减轻医疗大语言模型中的幻觉问题。该框架基于EHRNoteQA/MIMIC-IV构建幻觉数据集，通过LLM-as-a-Judge与人工审核双重标注，并评估了自我批判流水线和检索增强上下文学习两种缓解策略。实验表明自我批判策略可在无需参数更新的情况下显著提升三个模型的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗大语言模型中的幻觉对基于电子健康记录的临床决策支持构成严重风险，而现有基准缺乏真实的临床上下文，且对如何在实际中缓解幻觉的指导有限。

Method: 基于EHRNoteQA（MIMIC-IV出院小结）构建数据集，使用BioMistral-7B在开放式临床问答上生成输出，通过GPT-4o评估与医学生人工审核的双重标注流水线进行正确性判断和推理错误标注。在此基础上测试两种缓解策略：自我批判流水线（模型自我审查并重新生成）和检索增强上下文学习（提供幻觉与纠正示例）。

Result: 在BioMistral、Llama-3.1、DeepSeek、Qwen2.5和Qwen3五个开源模型上，自我批判策略显著提升了其中三个模型的准确率（p < 0.05），且无需更新模型参数。

Conclusion: Med-HEAL提供了一个可复用的幻觉数据集和实用框架，用于研究和缓解医疗LLM中的幻觉，支持AI系统在临床环境中的安全部署。

Abstract: Hallucinations in medical large language models (LLMs) pose serious risks for clinical decision support, particularly when models must reason over complex electronic health records (EHRs). However, existing benchmarks often lack a realistic clinical context and provide limited insight into how hallucinations can be mitigated in practice. We introduce Med-HEAL, a framework for systematically identifying, analyzing, and mitigating hallucinations in medical LLMs using clinically grounded data. Building on the EHRNoteQA benchmark derived from MIMIC-IV discharge summaries, we construct a hallucination dataset by evaluating BioMistral-7B on open-ended clinical question answering tasks. Model outputs are labeled through a dual evaluation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge assessment (GPT-4o) with human auditing by medical student reviewers, producing correctness judgments and annotations of reasoning errors via a custom web-based evaluation system.
  We then leverage this dataset to investigate mitigation strategies: a self-critique pipeline, in which the test model reviews its own answers to detect potential errors and regenerates responses for flagged cases, and retrieval-augmented in-context learning (RA-ICL), which exposes the model to hallucinated and corrected examples. Experiments across five open-source LLMs-BioMistral, Llama-3.1, DeepSeek, Qwen2.5, and Qwen3, show that the self-critique strategy improves accuracy for three of five models (p < 0.05) without requiring parameter updates.
  Med-HEAL provides both a reusable hallucination dataset and a practical framework for studying and mitigating hallucinations in medical LLMs, supporting safer deployment of AI systems in clinical environments. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/yimingliao-blad/med-heal.git.

</details>


### [97] [SkillAdaptor: Self-Adapting Skills for LLM Agents from Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01311)
*Zhuoyun Yu,Xin Xie,Wuguannan Yao,Chenxi Wang,Lei Liang,Xiang Qi,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillAdaptor是一种无需训练的步级技能自适应框架，通过显式失败归因识别首个可操作的故障步骤，定位责任技能并执行定向更新，显著提升了LLM代理在长程交互任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关的技能自适应方法通常基于完整轨迹或会话级反馈进行技能更新，导致失败归因粗糙、修订不稳定或过于宽泛，亟需更细粒度的失败定位与定向技能修正机制。

Method: SkillAdaptor框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 识别失败轨迹中首个可操作的故障步骤；(2) 将该步骤与候选技能建立责任关联；(3) 在显式接受检查约束下对目标技能进行定向更新，同时保持骨干模型参数冻结。

Result: 在WebShop、PinchBench和Claw-Eval三个评测集上，SkillAdaptor均优于无技能和技能自适应基线，PinchBench Avg Score%提升+1.5，Claw-Eval Avg Score提升+1.8，WebShop成功率提升+1.7。

Conclusion: 步级归因机制能够支持更稳定、更可审计的无需训练的技能维护，SkillAdaptor可即插即用于OpenClaw类代理框架。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on reusable external skills to solve long-horizon interactive tasks. Existing training-free skill adaptation pipelines usually update skills from full trajectories or session-level feedback, which makes failure attribution coarse and often produces unstable or overly broad revisions. We propose SkillAdaptor, a training-free step-level skill adaptation framework with explicit failure attribution, and it can plug into OpenClaw-class agent harnesses. Given a failed trajectory, SkillAdaptor identifies a first actionable fault step, links responsibility to candidate skills, and applies targeted updates under explicit acceptance checks while keeping the backbone frozen. We evaluate on WebShop, PinchBench, and Claw-Eval with Kimi-K2.5, GLM-5, and GPT-5.2. SkillAdaptor improves over no-skill and skill-adaptation baselines on all three suites, with the largest single-metric improvements of +1.5 points on PinchBench Avg Score%, +1.8 on Claw-Eval Avg Score, and +1.7 on WebShop success rate. These results indicate that step-level attribution supports more stable and auditable training-free skill maintenance\footnote{The code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/SkillAdaptor.}.

</details>


### [98] [TukaBench: A Culturally Grounded Jailbreak Benchmark for African Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01322)
*Victor Akinode,Senyu Li,Wassim Hamidouche,Waqas Zamir,Inbal Becker-Reshef,David Ifeoluwa Adelani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TUKABENCH是一个针对七种非洲低资源语言的越狱基准测试，通过四种设置（直译、文化适应、人工策划、语码切换）评估LLM安全性。研究发现：非洲语言提示比英语更少被拒绝，文化适应提示拒绝率最低；同时暴露了模型理解失败和LLM-as-a-judge在低资源语言中可靠性下降两大结构性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM安全评估高度英语中心化，低资源语言（尤其是非洲语言）的安全评估严重缺失。

Method: 基于JailbreakBench构建七种非洲语言的越狱基准TUKABENCH，通过四种设置（直译、英语语境适应后翻译、人工策划、语码切换）系统评估语言、文化基础和提示规避性对模型安全的影响。

Result: 非洲语言提示相比英语降低拒绝率，文化适应提示拒绝率最低；发现模型理解失败和低资源语言中LLM-as-a-judge可靠性下降两大问题。

Conclusion: 低资源语言的安全评估存在结构性问题：模型在LRL中理解能力不足，且自动化评估工具可靠性下降，需引入Deflection等新指标和人工标注来弥补。

Abstract: Safety evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) remains heavily English-centric, leaving Low-Resource Languages (LRLs), particularly African ones, critically underexplored. We introduce TUKABENCH, a jailbreak benchmark for seven African languages that extends JailbreakBench (JBB) beyond direct translation through four settings: human translation of JBB prompts, English adaptation to African contexts followed by human translation, human-curated prompts validated through interactions with GPT-5.2, and code-switched prompts combining English and African languages, isolating the effect of language, cultural grounding, and prompt evasiveness on model safety. Across closed and open models, prompting in African languages reduces refusal relative to English, with culturally adapted prompts leading to least refusal. The evaluation also surfaces two structural limitations: model comprehension failures and reduced LLM-as-a-judge reliability in LRLs. To capture the first, we introduce Deflection alongside Refused and Jailbroken; to assess the second, we validate outputs with human annotations, showing that judge-human agreement drops in lower-resource languages and less commonly supported scripts.

</details>


### [99] [DiffuSent: Towards a Unified Diffusion Framework for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01323)
*Shu Long,Yanglei Gan,Xuchuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiffuSent是一个非自回归的扩散框架，将ABSAs所有子任务统一建模为边界去噪扩散过程，通过对比去噪训练策略解决重复预测问题，在28个实验设置中取得一致改进，多词三元组F1平均提升+2.48，推理速度可达自回归基线模型的181倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式模型在统一方面情感分析中采用自回归逐token生成方式，未能充分捕捉方面词和观点词的整体信息，导致对多词方面词和观点词的边界不敏感，影响提取准确性。

Method: 提出DiffuSent扩散框架，将所有ABSA子任务统一建模为边界去噪扩散过程，通过逐步精化噪声状态下的边界进行预测；引入对比去噪训练策略，有效处理扩散过程带来的细微重复预测问题。

Result: 在7个子任务×4个数据集共28个实验设置中，DiffuSent一致优于最强的生成式和基于跨度的系统；多词三元组上F1平均提升+2.48；非自回归解码实现高达181倍的推理加速。

Conclusion: DiffuSent通过边界去噪扩散框架有效解决了多词边界不敏感和重复预测问题，在精度和效率上均显著优于现有方法，为非自回归范式在情感分析中的应用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (ABSA) encompasses seven distinct subtasks, each focusing on different extracted elements. Despite the proven success of generative models in unified aspect sentiment analysis, existing approaches often rely on auto-regressive token-by-token generation without grasping the whole information of the aspect and opinion terms, resulting in boundary insensitivity, particularly in context of multi-word aspect and opinion terms. To address these issues, we present DiffuSent, a non-auto-regressive diffusion framework that systematically formulates all ABSA subtasks as boundary denoising diffusion processes, progressively refining boundaries over noisy states. Furthermore, we introduce a contrastive denoising training strategy which effectively address duplicate predictions with subtle variations introduced by diffusion process. Extensive experiments across 28 settings (7 subtasks x 4 datasets) demonstrate that DiffuSent achieves delivers consistent improvements over the strongest generative and span-based systems. DiffuSent exhibits notable gains on multi-word triplets, achieving an average improvement of +2.48 F1, and maintains robust extraction accuracy in sentences containing multiple sentiment triplets. Moreover, the non-auto-regressive decoding enables substantial efficiency benefits, reaching up to 181 times faster inference than auto-regressive generative baselines

</details>


### [100] [LongAttnComp: Cross-Family Context Compression for Long-Context Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01336)
*Mengmeng Ji,Ravi Shanker Raju,Jonathan Lingjie Li,Chen Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LongAttnComp 是一种长上下文注意力压缩方法，通过微调轻量级交叉注意力评分层，结合 token 级分块、top-p 预算、位置重排和格式无关查询解析器，以及两阶段微调策略，在 100k+ 长上下文任务（如代码推理）上匹配或超越全上下文准确率，显著优于无训练基线，并在多个模型间可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用需要处理 100k+ token 的长上下文，而现有训练无关的注意力压缩方法在代码推理等长上下文任务上存在显著差距，需要更有效的上下文压缩方法以降低预填充成本同时保持任务准确性。

Method: 提出 LongAttnComp，包含：(1) 微调轻量级交叉注意力评分层；(2) token 级分块；(3) token 预算 top-p 算法；(4) 位置重排；(5) 格式无关查询解析器。设计两阶段微调：Stage 1 用 NIAH 数据构建检索基础，Stage 2 用多跳和推理数据扩展长上下文覆盖。

Result: 在 InfiniteBench Code-Debug 上匹配或超越全上下文准确率，大幅优于训练无关基线；在四个目标模型（三个模型家族）上可迁移；在 LongBench v2 上，两阶段策略弥补了 Stage 1 在多文档推理上的差距，同时保持了 Code-Debug 性能。

Conclusion: LongAttnComp 有效弥合了长上下文压缩在复杂推理任务上的性能差距，验证了轻量级微调压缩器在长上下文场景中的有效性和可迁移性。

Abstract: As real-world applications increasingly require processing inputs of 100k+ tokens, the gap between context length and inference efficiency has become a critical bottleneck. Context compression offers a way to reduce prefill costs while preserving task accuracy. However, existing training-free attention-based methods leave substantial gaps in demanding long-context tasks such as code reasoning. We present LongAttnComp, a long-context adaptation of AttnComp that fine-tunes a lightweight cross-attention scoring layer and introduces tokenlevel chunking, a token-budget top-p algorithm, positional reordering, and a formatagnostic query parser. We further design a two-stage fine-tuning recipe for the compressor: Stage 1 builds a general retrieval foundation from NIAH-style data, and Stage 2 extends it with multi-hop and reasoning data for broader long-context task coverage. On InfiniteBench Code-Debug, LongAttnComp matches or exceeds full-context accuracy, substantially outperforms training-free baselines, and transfers across four target models from three families. On LongBench v2, the two-stage recipe largely closes the Stage 1 gap on multi-document reasoning while preserving Code-Debug performance.

</details>


### [101] [Benchmarking Local LLMs for Natural-Language-to-SQL Querying in Biopharmaceutical Manufacturing: An Empirical Benchmark on Consumer-Grade Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01338)
*Sagar Bhetwal,Rajan Bastakoti,Nirajan Acharya,Gaurav Kumar Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了四种开源大语言模型在本地部署下，将自然语言转换为SQL查询以用于制药制造数据库的能力。Qwen 2.5 Coder 7B、Llama 3.1 8B和Mistral 7B成功完成所有任务，其中Llama 3.1 8B的SQL合规性最高，Qwen 2.5 Coder 7B的文本相似性和事实一致性最强，而领域专用模型Meditron 7B因上下文窗口限制几乎完全失败。结果表明代码调优的通用LLM优于领域专用模型，但当前性能仍需人工监督和下游验证才能用于受监管场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制药制造组织在FDA、EU GMP及EU AI Act等监管框架下，云基AI系统受限。本地部署的LLM提供了隐私保护的替代方案，但其在制药制造任务中的适用性尚未充分探索。

Method: 使用FastAPI开发PharmaBatchDB AI评估平台，基于合成Microsoft SQL Server数据库（约63,000条记录，涵盖Batch、MES、CIP模块）。在60个领域特定自然语言问题上，以SQL提取率、SQL合规性、事实一致性、ROUGE-L、幻觉率、吞吐量和延迟为指标，对比四种本地部署的开源LLM。

Result: Qwen 2.5 Coder 7B、Llama 3.1 8B和Mistral 7B为所有任务生成了SQL；Meditron 7B因上下文窗口限制和SQL生成能力差而几乎全部失败。Llama 3.1 8B的SQL合规性最高，Qwen 2.5 Coder 7B的文本相似性和事实一致性最强，二者性能差异无统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 代码调优的通用LLM在制药制造数据的结构化查询生成任务上优于领域专用生物医学模型。完全本地化、符合GxP要求的NLQ系统在消费级硬件上可行，但当前性能仍需人工监督和下游验证才能用于受监管场景。

Abstract: Biopharmaceutical manufacturing organizations operate under regulatory frameworks such as FDA guidance, EU Good Manufacturing Practice (GMP), and the EU AI Act, which can restrict the use of cloud-based artificial intelligence systems. Locally deployed large language models (LLMs) offer a privacy-preserving alternative, but their suitability for pharmaceutical manufacturing tasks remains underexplored. This study evaluates four open-source LLMs (Qwen 2.5 Coder 7B, Llama 3.1 8B, Mistral 7B, and Meditron 7B) deployed locally via Ollama for natural-language-to-SQL generation over a pharmaceutical manufacturing database.
  A FastAPI-based evaluation platform, PharmaBatchDB AI, was developed using a synthetic Microsoft SQL Server database containing approximately 63,000 records across Batch, Manufacturing Execution System (MES), and Clean-In-Place (CIP) modules. Models were benchmarked on 60 domain-specific natural-language questions using metrics including SQL extraction rate, SQL compliance, factual consistency, ROUGE-L, hallucination rate, throughput, and latency.
  Qwen 2.5 Coder 7B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Mistral 7B generated SQL for all evaluation tasks, while Meditron 7B failed on nearly all tasks due to context-window limitations and poor SQL generation capability. Llama 3.1 8B achieved the highest SQL compliance, whereas Qwen 2.5 Coder 7B achieved the strongest overall text similarity and factual consistency. Performance differences between the two leading models were not statistically significant.
  The results show that code-tuned general-purpose LLMs outperform a domain-specific biomedical model on structured query generation for pharmaceutical manufacturing data. Although fully local, GxP-aligned NLQ systems are feasible on consumer hardware, current performance levels still require human oversight and downstream validation for regulated use.

</details>


### [102] [Dr. DocBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Expert-Level and Difficult Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01393)
*Minglai Yang,Xinyan Velocity Yu,Pengyuan Li,Xinyu Guo,Zhenting Qi,Konwoo Kim,Longtian Ye,Xiaolong Luo,Jinhe Bi,Henry Zhang,Haris Riaz,Xuan Zhang,Yunze Xiao,Bangya Liu,Tom Tang,Yunfei Zhao,Qunshu Lin,Zihan Wang,Minghao Liu,Michael Lingzhi Li,Yilun Du,Jesse Thomason,Rogerio Feris,Alex Pentland,Zexue He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Dr. DocBench是一个面向专家级文档解析的难度感知基准，基于大规模多语言书籍语料库构建，涵盖52个BISAC学科领域，包含4,514页带标注的页面和65k条高质量注释。通过对现有最先进系统的评估，发现其在专家级文档解析任务上表现不佳，揭示了现有基准的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的OCR和文档解析基准在覆盖范围和难度上日益受限：多数聚焦于常见文档类型或均匀采样页面，现代解析器在这些场景下表现已很好；同时缺乏对化学公式、乐谱、复杂表格和跨页布局等专业领域结构的注释。

Method: 从大规模多语言书籍语料库构建基准，覆盖52个BISAC学科领域；采用基于解析器失败的采样策略，选取多个最先进系统都难以处理的困难文档；包含4,514页来自长文档（平均约100页）的页面，提供65k页面级和块级注释，涵盖布局、阅读顺序、层次关系和领域特定视觉内容。

Result: 基于管道的解析器和通用视觉语言模型在现有基准上表现良好，但在专家级文档解析（Dr. DocBench）上表现不理想；在不同学科、内容类型和结构属性上均存在显著失败情况。

Conclusion: Dr. DocBench可作为诊断和推进文档智能的全面测试平台，强调了现有基准的不足以及构建更具挑战性的专家级文档解析评估的必要性。

Abstract: Document parsing and recognition are fundamental capabilities for vision-language models (VLMs) and document processing systems. However, existing Optical Character Recognition (OCR) and document parsing benchmarks are increasingly limited in coverage and difficulty: many focus on common document genres or uniformly sampled pages where modern parsers already perform strongly, while offering limited annotation for expert-domain structures such as chemical formula, music notation, complex tables, and cross-page layouts. We introduce Dr. DocBench, a difficulty-aware benchmark for expert-level document parsing. Built from a large-scale multilingual book corpus, Dr. DocBench spans 52 BISAC subject domains and selects challenging documents through parser-failure-based sampling, targeting cases where multiple state-of-the-art systems struggle. It contains 4,514 annotated pages from long documents averaging around 100 pages, with 65k high-quality page- and block-level annotations for layout, reading order, hierarchical relations, and domain-specific visual contents. Evaluations of pipeline-based parsers and general-purpose VLMs show that strong performance on existing benchmarks does not transfer to our expert-level document parsing. Our analysis reveals substantial failures across subjects, content types, and structural attributes, highlighting Dr. DocBench as a comprehensive testbed for diagnosing and advancing document intelligence.

</details>


### [103] [UniD$^3$: A Knowledge Graph-Enhanced RAG Framework for Drug-Disease Discovery and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01394)
*Qing Wang,Tianshi Liu,Minghao Zhou,Jialu Liang,Sen Guo,Guangyu Wang,Jing Su,Qianqian Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniD$^3$是一个统一框架，结合大语言模型与知识图谱增强检索生成技术，从大量PubMed文献中系统提取、组织和验证药物-疾病关系，构建了大规模结构化数据集和知识图谱，在外部基准和临床专家评估中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物-疾病关系的系统表征对药物发现和重定位至关重要，但现有数据集依赖人工整理且不完整，而纯LLM方法存在幻觉和证据基础薄弱的问题，亟需一种可扩展、自动化的方法来从异构且快速增长的生物医学文献中提取高质量结构化的药物-疾病知识。

Method: 采用Llama 3.3-70B处理157,849篇PubMed文献，通过双阶段策略（论文级提取+以药物和疾病实体为中心的KG级整合）构建知识图谱，再基于KG-RAG生成结构化数据集，涵盖药物-疾病匹配、药物有效性评估和药物-靶点分析三个任务。

Result: 构建了6个知识图谱和包括28,915个DDM、15,042个DEA及超过4,000个DTA问答对的大规模数据集；外部验证F1分数为0.85-0.87（DDM/DEA）和0.82（DTA）；临床专家审查确认高可靠性（AUROC=0.90）。

Conclusion: UniD$^3$提供了一个可扩展、可扩展的框架，将非结构化生物医学文献转化为高质量结构化的药物-疾病知识，KG-RAG增强模型优于纯LLM方法，支持AI驱动的药物发现、重定位和精准医学。

Abstract: Systematic characterization of drug-disease relationships is essential for drug discovery and repurposing, yet is hindered by the heterogeneity and rapid growth of biomedical literature. Existing datasets rely on labor-intensive curation and are often incomplete, while LLM-only approaches suffer from hallucination and weak evidence grounding. We introduce UniD$^3$, a unified framework that integrates Large Language Models with Knowledge Graph-enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG) to extract, organize, and validate drug-disease knowledge across Drug-Disease Matching (DDM), Drug Effectiveness Assessment (DEA), and Drug-Target Analysis (DTA). UniD$^3$ processes 157,849 PubMed articles with Llama 3.3-70B and constructs knowledge graphs via a dual-stage strategy combining paper-level extraction with KG-level consolidation centered on drug and disease entities. These graphs support KG-RAG-based generation of structured datasets, evaluated through external benchmarks, fuzzy matching with curated resources, and clinician review. UniD$^3$ produces six knowledge graphs and large-scale datasets, including 28,915 DDM, 15,042 DEA, and over 4,000 DTA QA pairs. External validation shows strong performance (F1: 0.85-0.87 for DDM/DEA; 0.82 for DTA), with clinician review confirming high reliability (AUROC = 0.90). KG-RAG-augmented models outperform standalone LLMs, and the UniD$^3$ chatbot enables interpretable, citation-supported exploration of drug-disease relationships. UniD$^3$ provides a scalable, extensible framework for transforming unstructured biomedical literature into high-quality, structured drug-disease knowledge, supporting AI-driven discovery, repurposing, and precision medicine.

</details>


### [104] [Consistent and Distinctive: LLM Benchmark Efficiency via Maximum Independent Set Prompt Selection on Similarity Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01400)
*Denica Kjorvezir,Marko Djukanović,Ana Gjorgjevikj,Gjorgjina Cenikj,Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于图的最大独立集（MIS）框架，从基准测试中选择多样化、非冗余的提示子集。实验证明，在不同随机种子下，子集生成的LLM排名与全基准高度一致（Kendall's W ≥ 0.90，99.2%配置），同时可实现25-48%的提示缩减。排名分歧主要出现在低阈值和密集图场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全面评估大语言模型（LLM）的成本高昂且耗时，需要一种高效的提示选择方法来降低评估开销，同时保持排名可靠性。

Method: 提出图式提示选择框架：将每个基准建模为相似度图（节点为提示，边由嵌入距离高于可配置阈值决定），应用最大独立集（MIS）算法选择多样性最大、无冗余的子集。评估了四种MIS求解器（CPLEX, GREEDY, Online-MIS, ReduMIS）、六种嵌入模型、三种距离度量、六个百分位阈值和四个基准（GPQA, IFEval, MMLU-Pro, Omni-MATH），覆盖66个LLM。

Result: 核心假设得到强验证：随机种子下重复选择产生一致的LLM排名（Kendall's W ≥ 0.90 在99.2%的随机配置中，平均W=0.997±0.008）；在较高百分位阈值下，子集平均实现25-48%的提示缩减；全基准排名分歧（ρ<0.95）仅出现在15.95%的配置中，主要集中在低阈值（p₁₀-p₂₀）和GPQA、IFEval基准上，识别出过度密集图是主要失败模式。

Conclusion: 基于MIS的图式提示选择框架能够在显著减少提示数量的同时，稳定保持与全基准一致的LLM排名，是一种高效且可靠的评估降本方案。密集图（低阈值）是导致排名分歧的主要原因，需避免此类配置。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) across comprehensive benchmarks is expensive and time-consuming. We propose a graph-based prompt selection framework that models each benchmark as a similarity graph -- nodes are prompts connected if their embedding-space distance falls above a configurable threshold -- and applies Maximum Independent Set (MIS) algorithms to select a maximally diverse, non-redundant subset. We evaluate four MIS solvers (CPLEX, GREEDY, Online-MIS, ReduMIS) across six embedding models, three distance measures, six percentile thresholds, and four benchmarks (GPQA, IFEval, MMLU-Pro, Omni-MATH) covering 66 LLMs. Our central hypothesis -- that repeated selection under different random seeds yields consistent LLM rankings that may also differ from the full-benchmark baseline -- is strongly confirmed: Kendall's $W \geq 0.90$ in 99.2\% of stochastic configurations (mean $W = 0.997 \pm 0.008$), while at higher percentile thresholds selected subsets achieve 25--48\% prompt reduction on average. Ranking divergence from the full benchmark ($ρ< 0.95$) occurs in only 15.95\% of configurations, concentrated at low thresholds ($p_{10}$--$p_{20}$) and benchmarks (GPQA, IFEval), identifying overly dense graphs as the primary failure mode.

</details>


### [105] [DrugClaw and DrugAudit: A Primary-Source-Grounded Agent and Authority-Aware Benchmark for Drug-Information Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01434)
*Qing Wang,Bo Li,Jialu Liang,Daling Shi,Bob Zhang,Qianqian Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DrugClaw是一个多智能体检索增强系统，通过反射驱动的状态机工作流查询药物和药物警戒技能库，生成基于原始监管或同行评审记录的答案。同时贡献了DrugAudit基准（3,772项）和评估面板。在DrugAudit、MedQA和PubMedQA数据集上，DrugClaw在所有指标上均排名第一，主要来源率达0.918，忠实度达0.887。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物信息问答中，幻觉事实可能误导临床决策，且每个引用事实的来源与事实本身同等重要，现有方法在这两方面存在不足。

Method: 采用多智能体检索增强架构，通过反射驱动的状态机工作流查询药物和药物警戒技能注册库，答案基于原始监管或同行评审记录。同时构建了DrugAudit基准（3,772项），包含权威感知的评估面板，采用双法官LLM评估协议，法官间kappa=0.88。

Result: 在DrugAudit、MedQA（751）和PubMedQA（512）上，DrugClaw在所有指标上均排名第一：复合证据指数、法官介导的答案正确性、主要来源率（0.918，领先第二10.1个百分点）、忠实度（0.887，领先5.9个百分点）、MedQA（0.920）、PubMedQA（0.693）。

Conclusion: DrugClaw在多药物信息问答基准上实现了最先进的性能，尤其在来源追踪和答案忠实度方面显著优于现有方法，适合高风险的临床决策场景。

Abstract: Drug-information question answering is a high-stakes setting where hallucinated facts can mislead clinical decision-making and the provenance of each cited fact matters as much as the fact itself. We present DrugClaw, a multi-agent retrieval-augmented system that queries a registry of drug and pharmacovigilance skills via a reflection-driven state-machine workflow and returns answers grounded in primary regulatory or peer-reviewed records. We also contribute DrugAudit, a 3,772-item authority-aware benchmark with an evaluation panel that scores upstream-of-gold source match, token-level semantic snippet overlap, and citation faithfulness under a dual-judge LLM-as-judge protocol with inter-judge kappa = 0.88 (almost-perfect). Across DrugAudit plus drug-related subsets of MedQA (751) and PubMedQA (512), DrugClaw is top-1 on every column of the headline table: composite Evidence Index under both judges, judge-mediated answer correctness, primary-source rate (0.918, +10.1 pp over next-best), faithfulness (0.887, +5.9 pp), MedQA (0.920), and PubMedQA (0.693).

</details>


### [106] [Learning from Saturated Data: Signals Beyond Correctness for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01436)
*Hanno Hiss,Jasper Dekoninck,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在基准测试已饱和的情况下，能否利用已被完美解决的训练问题中的细粒度质量信号（成对自评判和词元级熵）来提升下游性能。在简单算术任务上，该方法比SFT提升高达18.6%，但在GSM8K等复杂任务上效果有限，且自评判信号可能与强外部评判者不一致，甚至导致性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型能力增强，许多基准测试和训练数据集已饱和（问题被完美解决），传统基于二元正确性的训练信号已无法提供有效区分度。作者探究能否从这些"已饱和"的问题中提取更细粒度的质量信号来进一步提升模型性能。

Method: 使用两种细粒度质量信号替代二元正确性：（1）成对LLM自评判——模型对自己生成的多个解答进行相对质量比较；（2）词元级熵——以词元级不确定性作为解答质量的代理指标。将这些信号融入多种训练算法，在Qwen3-1.7B-Base上评估效果。

Result: 在简单算术任务上，基于质量的训练信号比基础模型提升高达18.6%，显著优于SFT。在GSM8K上，增益较小且高度依赖质量信号类型。自评判信号与强外部评判者一致性差，甚至可能使性能低于基础模型。

Conclusion: 基于质量的训练能从已饱和的问题中为基础模型提取有用信号，但在复杂任务上应用此类信号需要仔细校准，仍有待进一步研究。

Abstract: The growing capabilities of large language models (LLMs) have led to the saturation of many benchmarks and training datasets used to improve them. Motivated by this, we investigate whether questions solved with perfect empirical accuracy can nevertheless be used to improve downstream performance. To do so, we replace binary correctness with two sources of more fine-grained quality signals: (1) pairwise LLM self-judgments, in which the model evaluates the relative quality of its own solutions, and (2) token-level entropy, where token-level uncertainty is used as a proxy for solution quality. We incorporate these signals into several training algorithms and evaluate them on Qwen3-1.7B-Base. When training exclusively on a simple arithmetic task, quality-based signals improve performance by up to $18.6\%$ over the base model, substantially outperforming SFT. On GSM8K, however, gains are more modest and depend strongly on the quality signal. For instance, self-judgments show poor agreement with a stronger external judge and can even degrade performance below the base model. Overall, our results suggest that quality-based training can extract useful signal from saturated questions for base models, but that applying such signals to more complex tasks requires careful calibration and further study.

</details>


### [107] [Before and After Temperature: A Distributional View of Creative LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01451)
*V. S. Raghu Parupudi,Harsha Ponnada,Aditi Kaushal,S. Shria Parupudi,Saiteja Dasari,Sahiti Bulusu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，在评估LLM创造力时，一个比困惑度、熵等传统指标更强的信号存在于采样温度重塑token分布的阶段。基于Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在500个创意提示上的实验，单个per-token特征即可达到Spearman ρ=0.918（对GPT-4o/Gemini-2.5-pro评审判）和ρ=0.870（对三人多数投票人类评判），远超四种基线方法（最高|ρ|≈0.76）。该优势源于温度T=1.5时出现的"不连贯 regime"的尖锐分布特征：累积质量宽度n₉₅(q)从~1膨胀到~131个token，且温度后质量从温度前top-90%可信集泄漏约13个百分点。但per-token聚合无法区分T=0.8和T=0.3这两个连贯区间，需要序列级特征来区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无参考评估LLM创造力的方法（困惑度、熵、top-1 margin等）性能有限，本文试图探索是否在采样温度重塑token分布这一步中存在更强的信号，以提升创造力评估的准确性。

Method: 使用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct模型在500个开放创意提示上生成文本，温度T∈{0.3, 0.8, 1.5}。提取per-token特征（基于温度重塑分布），与四种基线（自困惑度、平均预测熵、top-1 margin、gzip压缩比）对比。使用GPT-4o/Gemini-2.5-pro平均评判和人类三人多数投票作为真值。

Result: 单一per-token特征达到Spearman ρ=0.918（LLM评审判）和ρ=0.870（人类评判），远超基线最高|ρ|≈0.76。两个真值面板之间的一致性ρ=0.83高于人类间一致性上限ρ=0.77。T=1.5时出现不连贯特征：n₉₅(q)从~1膨胀到~131个token，质量泄漏约13个百分点。Per-token聚合无法区分T=0.3和T=0.8。

Conclusion: 温度重塑分布中的per-token特征是一种比现有无参考指标更强的创造力评估信号，但其能力局限在区分连贯与不连贯区间，区分两个连贯区间需要序列级特征。

Abstract: Reference-free evaluation of large language model (LLM) creativity relies on perplexity, entropy, and top-1 margin. We show that a much stronger signal lives one step earlier in the pipeline: in how sampling temperature \emph{reshapes} the model's token distribution before the next token is drawn. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct generations of 500 open-ended creative prompts at $T \in \{0.3, 0.8, 1.5\}$, a single per-token feature derived from this reshaping predicts the within-prompt creativity rank at Spearman $ρ{=}0.918$ against an averaged gpt-4o\,/\,gemini-2.5-pro judge ($n{=}500$) and $ρ{=}0.870$ against a three-rater human-majority ranking ($n{=}150$). Each of four standard reference-free baselines (self-perplexity, mean predictive entropy, top-1 margin, gzip compression ratio) tops out at $|ρ|\!\approx\!0.76$ on both ground truths: a gap of $+0.165$ on averaged-LLM and $+0.110$ on human-majority, both far larger than the spread among the baselines themselves. The two ground-truth panels agree with each other at $ρ{=}0.83$, above the inter-human ceiling of $ρ{=}0.77$, so the comparison is not bottlenecked by judge noise. Mechanistically, the win comes from a sharp distributional signature of the incoherence regime: at $T{=}1.5$ the cumulative-mass width $n_{95}(q)$ inflates from $\sim\!1$ to ${\sim}\!131$ tokens and post-temperature mass leaks off the pre-temperature top-$90\%$ plausible set by about $13$ percentage points. The per-token aggregates do not separate $T{=}0.8$ from $T{=}0.3$; discriminating the two coherent regimes is left to sequence-level features.

</details>


### [108] [Cross-lingual Self-Consistency for Multilingual Reasoning with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01464)
*Ahmed Elhady,Eneko Agirre,Mikel Artetxe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无监督的强化学习方法，通过跨语言自一致性约束来提升大语言模型的多语言推理能力，在MGSM上10种语言平均提升21.7%，并展现出对未见过语言的强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM的高级推理能力主要局限在英语等少数高资源语言上，而现有方法受限于多语言推理数据的稀缺性，对未见语言的泛化能力薄弱。

Method: 采用无监督强化学习框架，以跨语言自一致性（即模型对不同语言的等价问题应输出相同最终答案）作为奖励信号，无需金标答案或平行数据。

Result: 在MGSM上10种语言平均提升21.7%；在训练中未见的MGSM语言上平均提升18.2%；在3个分布外基准上最高提升6.2%。

Conclusion: 基于一致性的方法能够在无需监督数据的情况下有效提升LLM的多语言推理能力，具有良好的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Despite expanding their multilingual coverage, the advanced reasoning capabilities of LLMs remain largely confined to a few high-resource languages like English. To address this, we propose an unsupervised Reinforcement Learning (RL) approach to enhance multilingual reasoning by enforcing cross-lingual self-consistency: the principle that a model should produce the same final answer for equivalent problems in different languages. Existing methods are limited by the scarcity of multilingual reasoning data and show weak generalization to unseen languages. Our approach requires neither gold answers nor parallel data, and it achieves average gains of up to 21.7% on MGSM across 10 languages. In addition, our method demonstrates strong generalization, with an 18.2% mean improvement on MGSM languages unseen during training, and up to 6.2% gain on 3 out-of-distribution benchmarks. These results show the potential of consistency-based methods to improve the multilingual capabilities of LLMs without requiring supervised data.

</details>


### [109] [Peacemaker at ATE-IT: Automatic term extraction from Italian text for waste management data using encoder model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01469)
*Mahdi Bakhtiyarzadeh,Hadi Bayrami Asl Tekanlou,Jafar Razmara*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本、可解释的自动术语抽取方法，专为ATE共享任务Task A设计。该方法采用微调抽取策略，在少量计算资源上运行，并通过类型级和微观级的精确率、召回率和F1值进行评估。实验结果表明，该方法相比其他团队取得了均衡且稳定的性能，为低资源模型提供了良好起点，并有望在未来实现更高性能且保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动术语抽取在搜索引擎等现代技术中日益重要，但存在标注数据有限、多词表达式抽取复杂等问题，亟需一种低成本且可解释的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种低成本、可解释的自动术语抽取方法，利用微调抽取策略，在少量计算资源上运行，专为ATE共享任务Task A设计。

Result: 使用类型级和微观级的精确率、召回率和F1值进行评估，实验结果表明该方法相比其他团队实现了一致且均衡的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法虽然简单，但为低资源模型提供了良好起点，未来有望在保持可解释性的同时实现更高性能的模型扩展。

Abstract: The development of automatic term extraction has become increasingly important in modern technology. Automatic term extraction can be found in virtually every search engine that is currently available to users. Recent advancements have provided promising results for the extraction of automatic terms; however, accurate labeling is difficult because of several factors, such as the limited number of annotated documents available for training and the complexity of extracting multi-word expressions due to shifts in the domain. In this paper, we will present a low-cost and interpretable method of automatic term extraction, developed specifically for Task A of the ATE Shared Task. This new method utilizes fine-tuning extraction strategies that can run on a small amount of computational resources. We evaluated our automated system using both type-level and micro-level measures of precision, recall, and F1-score to measure both complementary aspects of the extraction performance. According to the experimental results, our proposed approach achieves consistent and balanced performance compared to other teams. Even though the technique itself is relatively straightforward, it serves as a good starting point for low-resource models. Overall, the findings point toward the possibility of significant future advancements (in model expansion) with higher-level performance still able to retain their ability to be interpreted.

</details>


### [110] [Sparse Autoencoders for Interpretable Emotion Control in Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01479)
*Hongfei Du,Jiacheng Shi,Sidi Lu,Gang Zhou,Ye Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用稀疏自编码器分析基于LLM的语音合成模型中情感相关的潜在特征，并提出一种特征级干预方法，通过调节少量稀疏潜在特征实现可解释的情感控制，无需修改模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based TTS系统虽然提升了语音表现力，但情感控制缺乏可解释性，现有方法依赖外部条件或全局激活引导，无法揭示情感控制背后的内部表征机制。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）从LLM-based TTS模型的语义隐藏状态中识别稀疏潜在特征；分析情感变化在多个稀疏特征上的分布特性；提出基于特征级干预的双向情感诱导与抑制框架，不修改主干参数。

Result: 情感变化分布于多个稀疏潜在特征，但仅需干预少量子集即可实现可解释的情感控制；不同潜在特征与特定声学属性（如基频）关联；所提方法在情感诱导与抑制上达到或优于全局引导及现有TTS基线。

Conclusion: 情感表达源于多个潜在特征的协调贡献而非单一全局偏移，特征级稀疏干预为实现可解释、高效的情感控制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Integrating large language models (LLMs) into text-to-speech (TTS) systems has improved speech expressiveness, yet interpretable emotional control remains challenging. Existing approaches primarily rely on external conditioning or global activation steering, offering limited insight into the internal representations underlying emotional control. In this work, we analyze emotion-related variation in the semantic hidden states of LLM-based TTS models using sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to identify sparse latent features. Our analysis shows that emotional variation is distributed across multiple sparse latent features, while intervening on a small subset enables interpretable emotion control. Building on this observation, we introduce a feature-level intervention framework for bidirectional emotion induction and suppression without modifying backbone parameters. We further show that distinct latent features are associated with specific acoustic attributes (e.g., pitch), suggesting that emotional expression arises from coordinated latent contributions rather than a single global shift. Empirically, steering these sparse latent features achieves comparable or superior emotion induction and suppression performance relative to global steering and existing TTS baselines.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Topical Similarity: Contrastive Evidence Retrieval with Interpretable Attention Alignment in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01482)
*Francielle Vargas,João Robiatti,Diego Alves,Lucas Pascotti Valem,Maximilian Seeth,Sebastián Ferrada,Ameeta Agrawal,Daniel Pedronette,André Freitas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CERA是一种通过主观性硬负样本选择和证据注意力对齐来提升RAG系统事实性和可解释性的密集检索框架，在临床试验报告上取得了优异效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在确保事实性和可解释性方面仍存在开放且紧迫的问题，现有检索方法缺乏对证据性信息的显式建模和对检索结果可解释性的关注。

Method: 提出对比证据理性注意力（CERA）框架：1）基于主观性的硬负样本选择策略；2）三元组对比学习目标；3）可解释注意力对齐损失，通过基于词性加权的掩码分布监督CLS到token的注意力，注入证据性归纳偏置。

Result: 在临床试验报告大规模语料上，主观性硬负样本选择显著优于Contriever和基线硬负样本选择方法；理性对齐在保持竞争性检索性能的同时提升了忠实度。

Conclusion: 注意力在人类理性引导下可以作为模型行为更忠实的解释，CERA使检索器超越主题相似性，识别构成支撑证据的特定token，促进RAG系统中更可解释的证据选择。

Abstract: Ensuring factuality and interpretability in RAG remains an open and urgent problem. We introduce Contrastive Evidence Rationale Attention (CERA), the first retrieval framework to employ subjectivity-based hard negative selection and inject an evidential inductive bias into contrastive learning through an auxiliary attention alignment loss. CERA fine-tunes a dense retriever using two training objectives: triplet-based contrastive learning and interpretable attention alignment, which supervises CLS-to-token attention using a part-of-speech-weighted masking distribution over human-annotated factual rationales as evidence signals. Experiments on a large corpus of clinical trial reports demonstrate that the subjectivity-based hard negative selection substantially improves retrieval effectiveness compared to both Contriever and hard negative selection baselines.
