Result: 在SDAR系列dLLM的四个基准测试上，SimSD实现了最高7.46倍的解码吞吐量提升，同时保持甚至改善了平均生成质量。

Conclusion: SimSD有效桥接了扩散语言模型与投机解码之间的兼容性鸿沟，在不牺牲生成质量的前提下显著加速推理，为dLLM的实际部署提供了有效的加速方案。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) LLMs, offering faster inference through parallel or blockwise decoding. However, their masked language modeling formulation remains incompatible with standard token-level speculative decoding, one of the most effective acceleration techniques for AR models. In AR decoding, the causal mask preserves temporally valid token-level contexts, enabling a target model to verify multiple drafted tokens in a single forward pass. In contrast, dLLMs rely on mask tokens and bidirectional attention, causing the effective context to change across denoising steps and preventing direct token-level speculative verification. To bridge this gap, we propose a simple but effective speculative decoding algorithm for diffusion language models, named SimSD, which mainly adopts a plug-and-play masking strategy that equips dLLMs with temporally valid token-level contexts for speculative decoding. Our method explicitly introduces reference tokens from draft-model predictions and designs an attention mask that regulates their interaction with current-step tokens, allowing dLLMs to compute valid logits for drafted tokens in a single forward pass. This restores the key verification ability provided by causal masking in AR models while preserving the parallel decoding advantages of dLLMs. The proposed method is training-free and can be flexibly integrated with other acceleration techniques such as KV cache and blockwise decoding. Experiments on SDAR-family dLLMs across four benchmarks show that our method achieves up to 7.46x higher decoding throughput while maintaining and even improving average generation quality.

</details>


### [186] [Transferable Self-Harm Surveillance from Emergency Department Triage Notes Using an Evidence-Augmented Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02545)
*Liuliu Chen,Gowri Rajaram,Eleanor Bailey,Katrina Witt,Michelle Lamblin,Jo Robinson,Mike Conway,Vlada Rozova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种三阶段方法，结合传统机器学习和大型语言模型，用于从急诊科分诊记录中检测自伤行为，在三个澳大利亚医院实现了0.88左右的AUPRC，并能以95%的准确率识别自伤方式，支持更细粒度的监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自伤是重大公共卫生问题，但当前依赖医院诊断编码的监测方法因编码灵敏度低而不够充分。急诊科分诊笔记作为最初接触点的记录，提供了简洁的呈现摘要，是识别自伤行为的潜在机会。

Method: 开发了一种三阶段方法：用传统机器学习结合大型语言模型进行筛查和证据提取，在三个澳大利亚医院评估模型的可迁移性，包含内部验证、外部验证和前瞻性验证。

Result: 内部和外部验证AUPRC分别为0.887±0.016和0.884±0.012；前瞻性验证在开发站点为0.881±0.008，在两个外部站点分别为0.879±0.012和0.816±0.015（无需站点特定再训练）。自伤方式识别准确率达95%。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅能实现高精度的自伤检测，还能超越二元分类，识别主要的自伤方式，支持更细粒度的公共卫生监测，且具有良好的跨站点迁移性。

Abstract: Self-harm is a major public health concern, but current surveillance relying on hospital presentations is inadequate due to the low sensitivity of diagnostic codes. Emergency Department (ED) triage notes, recorded at the initial point of contact, provide a succinct summary of presentations and an opportunity to identify self-harm. We developed a three-stage approach, augmenting traditional machine learning with large language model-based screening and evidence extraction to detect self-harm in ED triage notes. We assessed model transferability across three Australian hospitals. Our approach showed AUPRCs of 0.887 +/- 0.016 and 0.884 +/- 0.012 during internal and external validation. Prospectively, it achieved AUPRC of 0.881 +/- 0.008 at the development site, and 0.879 +/- 0.012 and 0.816 +/- 0.015 at two external sites without site-specific retraining. A key advantage of the approach is that it enables identification of the primary self-harm method with an accuracy of 95%, supporting more granular surveillance beyond binary classification.

</details>


### [187] [SN-WER: Script-Normalized WER for Multi-Script Indic ASR Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02548)
*Priyaranjan Pattnayak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出脚本归一化词错误率（SN-WER），通过在计算WER前将参考文本和假设文本音译为特定语言的规范脚本，解决多语言ASR中因脚本差异导致的错误率高估问题。实验表明SN-WER能有效降低罗马化引起的WER膨胀（减少67%），同时保持对语义错误的敏感性，建议作为WER和CER的补充指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准WER在多语言ASR场景中，当参考文本和假设文本使用不同书写系统（如天城文vs罗马化）编码相同词语时，会高估错误率，无法准确反映模型的实际识别性能。

Method: SN-WER是一种无需训练的评估方法，在计算WER之前先将参考文本和假设文本音译（transliterate）为语言特定的规范脚本，然后计算音译后文本的WER。

Result: 在5种印度语言、2个数据集和3个ASR模型上评估：FLEURS数据上模型差距最多降低12%；人工罗马化导致的WER膨胀衰减67%；对语义错误的敏感性与标准WER相近（ΔSN-WER/ΔWER≈1.09）；标记碰撞率低于0.1%。

Conclusion: SN-WER应作为WER和CER的补充指标用于脚本不敏感的ASR评估，尤其当转录结果用于下游搜索、索引或多语言LLM流程时。

Abstract: Word Error Rate (WER) is the dominant metric for automatic speech recognition (ASR), but it can overestimate errors when references and hypotheses encode the same words in different scripts. This issue is common in multilingual settings where ASR models may emit romanized text. We propose Script-Normalized WER (SN-WER), a training-free, evaluation-only scoring method that transliterates both reference and hypothesis text into a language-specific canonical script before computing WER. We evaluate SN-WER on 5 Indic languages, 2 datasets, and 3 ASR models. On curated FLEURS data, SN-WER reduces inflated model gaps by up to 12%, while on noisier Common Voice data the reductions are smaller or inconsistent, indicating genuine recognition weaknesses rather than only script mismatch. Controlled stress tests show a 67% attenuation of artificial romanization-induced WER inflation, while lexical-substitution controls show near-identical sensitivity to semantic errors, with Delta SN-WER / Delta WER approximately 1.09. SN-WER is robust to transliterator choice, normalization changes, and shows low token-collision rates below 0.1% in the evaluated Indic setting. We argue that SN-WER should be reported alongside WER and CER as a companion metric for script-insensitive ASR evaluation, especially when transcripts feed downstream search, indexing, or multilingual LLM pipelines.

</details>


### [188] [HERO'S JOURNEY: Testing Complex Rule Induction with Text Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02556)
*Anshun Asher Zheng,Kanishka Misra,David I. Beaver,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HERO'S JOURNEY是一个用于目标导向情节任务中规则归纳的基准测试，涵盖属性和程序归纳两大类别共8个任务。评估表明LLMs具备有限的规则归纳能力，且存在执行瓶颈，程序归纳仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试缺乏对目标导向情节任务中规则归纳能力的系统评估，尤其是区分属性归纳和程序归纳两种不同认知类型的任务。

Method: 构建了HERO'S JOURNEY基准，包含8个任务（分属属性归纳和程序归纳家族），每个任务有4种结构规则形式、可控词汇基础和可识别性条件。评估了多个最先进LLM，并测试了归纳特定的引导方法。

Result: 1) LLMs表现出一定的规则归纳能力但不均衡；2) 过程执行成为模型的执行瓶颈，而表面语义影响极小；3) 归纳特定引导方法在属性任务上有效，但在程序任务上无可靠提升。

Conclusion: 程序归纳依然是一个开放挑战，现有LLMs和引导方法在此类任务上表现有限，亟需新的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce HERO'S JOURNEY, a benchmark for rule induction in goal-directed episodic tasks, where agents must infer hidden rules from demonstrations and act on them through multi-step execution. HERO'S JOURNEY covers eight tasks across attribute and procedural induction families, each with four structural rule forms, controllable lexical grounding, and identifiability conditions. Evaluating state-of-the-art LLMs, we find that models show evidence of rule induction, but the ability is limited and uneven across tasks. Meanwhile, process execution adds an execution bottleneck for models, whereas surface semantics has minimal effect. Induction-specific steering methods improve performance on attribute tasks but show no reliable gains on procedural tasks, suggesting the gap in procedural induction remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [189] [From Layers to Submodules: Rethinking Granularity in Replacement-Based LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02559)
*Elia Cunegatti,Marcus Vukojevic,Erik Nielsen,Giovanni Iacca*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SubFit是一种面向大语言模型的子模块级压缩方法，通过在非连续的Attention和FeedForward子模块上拟合轻量级残差旁路，实现了比现有全层粒度、连续选择方法更优的困惑度-准确率权衡，在25%稀疏度下保留84.6%的下游准确率，困惑度退化仅2.42倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于替换的LLM压缩方法限定于全层粒度和连续选择，但预训练Transformer中的冗余并不局限于连续区域，也不均匀分布于Attention和FeedForward输出之间，不同子模块类型需要不同的近似策略，且可移除组件无需聚集在连续深度范围内。

Method: SubFit在子模块级别压缩LLM：非连续地选择Attention和FeedForward子模块，并为每个子模块配备轻量级拟合残差旁路。该方法仅需校准数据，属于训练后压缩。

Result: 在10个LLM（5个基础模型、5个指令微调模型）、5种稀疏度（12.5%-37.5%）和4个基线方法上，SubFit取得了最佳的整体困惑度-准确率权衡。在25%稀疏度下，保留84.6%下游准确率（基线81.6%），困惑度退化2.42倍（基线4.34倍），同时实现了可测量的推理加速和KV缓存节省。

Conclusion: 子模块级、非连续的残差替换策略比现有的全层粒度、连续选择方法更有效地压缩LLM，尤其在激进压缩场景下优势更明显。

Abstract: Post-training compression of Large Language Models (LLMs) removes entire architectural components, either deleting them or replacing them with fitted modules. Existing replacement-based methods share two design constraints: full-layer granularity and contiguous selection. We argue that this is overly restrictive: in fact, redundancy in pretrained transformers is not confined to contiguous regions, nor does it evenly distribute between Attention and FeedForward outputs, implying that different strategies best approximate different submodule types and that removable components need not cluster within contiguous depth ranges. Based on this intuition, we introduce SubFit (Submodule-level Fitted residual replacement), which compresses LLMs at the submodule level: Attention and FeedForward submodules are selected non-contiguously, and each receives its own lightweight fitted residual bypass. SubFit operates post-training and requires only calibration data. Across ten LLMs (five base, five instruction-tuned), five sparsity levels from 12.5% to 37.5%, and four replacement-based baselines, SubFit achieves the best aggregate perplexity-accuracy trade-off across the evaluated sparsity levels, with larger gains under aggressive compression. At 25% sparsity, it retains 84.6% of dense downstream accuracy and incurs 2.42x perplexity degradation, against 81.6% and 4.34x for the strongest baselines, while delivering measurable inference speedup and KV-cache savings. Code is available at https://github.com/eliacunegatti/SubFit.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [A Survey on Security with Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00058)
*Manik Kumar Sangala,Robin Naira,Akhirul Islam,Sudip Biswas,Manojit Ghose*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了量子计算带来的安全挑战与缓解策略，涵盖量子计算机自身安全漏洞、量子计算对现有密码体系的威胁、后量子密码等抗量子解决方案，以及量子技术在网络安全中的新兴应用，为构建安全、弹性的量子就绪计算基础设施提供了参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的快速发展在带来计算能力突破的同时，也引入了显著的安全、隐私和可靠性问题，亟需系统梳理相关安全挑战与应对策略。

Method: 采用综述研究方法，全面回顾了量子计算的安全问题，包括量子硬件/软件漏洞、对传统密码体系的冲击、后量子密码、量子安全通信协议、量子入侵检测系统、量子感知软件工程，以及量子纠错和容错方法等。

Result: 归纳了量子计算在网络安全领域的应用（如恶意软件检测、网络入侵检测、物联网安全、安全通信），分析了现有量子纠错和容错方法对提升量子计算鲁棒性的作用。

Conclusion: 通过整合最新进展、开放研究挑战和未来方向，为研究人员和实践者提供了量子计算与网络安全交叉领域的结构化参考，助力构建安全、弹性的量子就绪计算基础设施。

Abstract: Quantum computing has emerged as a transformative computing paradigm capable of solving problems that remain computationally infeasible for classical systems; however, its rapid advancement also introduces significant security, privacy, and reliability concerns. In this context, this survey presents a comprehensive review of security challenges and mitigation strategies associated with quantum computing, focusing on security issues in quantum computers, security threats caused by quantum computers, and security mechanisms developed for quantum systems. The paper examines vulnerabilities in quantum hardware and software, the impact of quantum computing on existing cryptographic infrastructures and cybersecurity mechanisms, and the development of quantum-resilient solutions such as post-quantum cryptography, quantum-safe communication protocols, quantum intrusion detection systems, and quantum-aware software engineering techniques. In addition, the survey discusses emerging applications of quantum technologies in cybersecurity domains, including malware detection, network intrusion detection, Internet of Things (IoT) security, and secure communication systems. Furthermore, the paper analyzes existing quantum error mitigation and fault-tolerance approaches designed to improve the robustness and trustworthiness of quantum computation under realistic noisy conditions. By consolidating recent advances, open research challenges, and future directions, this survey provides a structured overview of the evolving intersection between quantum computing and cybersecurity, while serving as a reference for researchers and practitioners working toward secure, resilient, and quantum-ready computing infrastructures.

</details>


### [191] [From Frontier to Shadow AI: A Simmering Threat to Assurance and Security in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00088)
*Mohan Baruwal Chhetri,Shahroz Tariq,Tooba Aamir,Marthie Grobler,Chandra Thapa,Ronal Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对关键基础设施环境中的影子AI进行实证研究，将其描述为系统性社会技术条件下的保障侵蚀现象。通过对27家澳大利亚关键基础设施组织的高管访谈，识别出三种安全降级机制：边界绕过、未评估的能力扩展和治理规避导致的不可观测性，并提出了相应的威胁模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI系统（包括大语言模型和新兴的代理式AI工具）在关键基础设施环境中因非确定性和涌现特性带来独特挑战。虽然正式采用受严格监管，但广泛可及性催生了影子AI——即未经授权使用前沿AI的行为，这绕过了既有的保障和监管机制，放大了数据保护、决策可靠性和合规性方面的风险。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对27家澳大利亚关键基础设施组织（涵盖通信、能源、水务三个领域）的高级管理人员和职能领导者进行半结构化访谈，分析影子AI在实际中的表现方式及其与现有技术和管理控制机制的相互作用。

Result: 识别出三种主要的安全降级机制：(i) 边界绕过——数据流绕过既有安全边界；(ii) 未评估的能力扩展——嵌入式AI功能引入潜在风险；(iii) 治理规避导致的不可观测性——破坏法证可审计性和最小权限原则的执行。

Conclusion: 影子AI引入了未管理的风险，从根本上挑战了现有的安全与合规框架，需要制定针对性的、路径对齐的治理和控制策略。

Abstract: Frontier AI systems, including large language models and emerging agentic AI tools, offer significant operational benefits but present unique challenges to critical infrastructure (CI) environments due to their non-deterministic and emergent properties. While formal adoption is inherently cautious and tightly controlled due to strict regulatory oversight, widespread accessibility has catalysed shadow AI: the unsanctioned use of frontier AI outside established organisational controls. In CI settings, shadow AI bypasses established assurance and oversight mechanisms, amplifying risks to data protection, decision reliability, and regulatory compliance, with potential consequences for essential service delivery. We present the first empirical study of shadow AI in CI environments, characterising it as a systemic socio-technical condition of assurance erosion. Drawing on semi-structured interviews with senior executives and functional leaders across 27 Australian CI organisations (Communications, Energy, and Water and Sewerage sectors), we analyse how shadow AI manifests in practice, how it interacts with existing technical and governance controls, and the resulting security, assurance, and compliance risks. We develop an empirically derived threat model identifying three primary mechanisms of security degradation: (i) boundary bypass, where data flows circumvent established perimeters; (ii) unassessed capability expansion, where embedded AI features introduce latent risks; and (iii) loss of observability via governance circumvention, undermining forensic auditability and least-privilege enforcement. Our findings demonstrate that shadow AI introduces unmanaged risks that fundamentally challenge existing security and compliance frameworks, necessitating tailored, pathway-aligned governance and control strategies.

</details>


### [192] [XAI-SOH-FL: Enhancing SOH-FL with Adaptive Aggregation and Explainable AI for Intrusion Detection in Heterogeneous IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00134)
*Ambreen Aslam,Maaz Hassan,Bibi Zahra,Muhammad Khuram Shahzad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出XAI-SOH-FL框架，通过自适应聚合机制（动态γ选择+贝叶斯优化）和SHAP可解释性增强，在IoT入侵检测中实现了94.12%准确率和0.92 F1-score，优于基线SOH-FL模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOH-FL框架存在两个关键局限：依赖人工调优的聚合参数γ，且缺乏模型可解释性，这限制了其在异构IoT环境中的实用性和可信度。

Method: 1) 基于相似度阈值的动态γ选择机制，使聚合适应数据分布变化；2) 贝叶斯优化自动确定最优γ值；3) 引入SHAP提供特征级可解释性分析。

Result: 在CICIDS2017数据集上，XAI-SOH-FL达到94.12%准确率和0.92 F1-score，收敛所需通信轮数更少，SHAP分析揭示Flow Duration和Packet Length为关键特征。

Conclusion: XAI-SOH-FL在异构IoT环境中有效平衡了准确性、适应性和可解释性，为联邦学习下的入侵检测提供了更可信的解决方案。

Abstract: Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) in Internet of Things (IoT) environments face significant challenges due to data heterogeneity, lack of labeled data, and limited model interpretability. Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving solution; however, existing approaches such as SOH-FL suffer from two key limitations: reliance on a manually tuned aggregation parameter γ and lack of explainability in model predictions. In this paper, we propose XAI-SOH-FL, an enhanced framework that integrates adaptive aggregation and explainable artificial intelligence into the SOH-FL paradigm. First, we introduce a dynamic γ selection mechanism based on similarity thresholding, enabling the aggregation process to adapt to evolving data distributions. Second, Bayesian Optimization is employed to automatically determine optimal γ values, eliminating the need for manual tuning. Third, SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) is incorporated to provide feature-level interpretability for intrusion detection decisions. Experimental evaluation on the CICIDS2017 dataset demonstrates that the proposed approach achieves an accuracy of 94.12% and an F1-score of 0.92, outperforming the baseline SOH-FL model while converging in fewer communication rounds. Furthermore, SHAP-based analysis reveals that flow-level features such as Flow Duration and Packet Length significantly influence model predictions. These results indicate that XAI-SOH-FL provides an effective balance between accuracy, adaptability, and interpretability in heterogeneous IoT environments.

</details>


### [193] [Persona Attack: Incremental Memory Injection Jailbreak Attack against Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00150)
*Junyoung Park,Seongyong Ju,Sunghwan Park,Jaewoo Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Persona Attack，一种基于记忆注入的越狱攻击方法，通过逐步向模型上下文窗口注入指令，使模型优先执行恶意指令而非内置安全对齐机制，在多种主流LLM上实现高达95%的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单一提示注入的越狱方法忽视了LLM对会话流和用户指令的记忆能力，因此需要探索利用模型记忆机制的新型攻击方式。

Method: 提出Persona Attack方法，通过逐步向模型的上下文窗口注入记忆，利用模型对会话流程的保持能力，累积注入指令以覆盖其安全对齐机制。

Result: 在多种主流LLM上的实验表明，随着记忆注入的累积，模型优先执行注入指令而非安全对齐；攻击成功率不仅取决于模型的记忆实现方式，还与指令组合有关，在特定配置下可达95%。

Conclusion: 基于记忆注入的越狱攻击对现有LLM的安全对齐构成严重威胁，成功率受模型记忆机制和指令组合的双重影响。

Abstract: As Large Language Models evolve for user convenience, vulnerability to jailbreak attacks continues to be reported despite ongoing efforts in safety training. Traditional jailbreak techniques typically focus on a single prompt injection, neglecting the models' ability to remember the flow of conversation and the user's instructions. In this paper, we propose Persona Attack, a memory injection based jailbreak method that manipulates the model's context window through a step by step approach. Experimental results from applying Persona Attack to several widely used LLMs reveal that, as injections accumulate in memory, models increasingly prioritize these instructions over their internal safety alignment mechanisms. Furthermore, our experiments empirically demonstrate that the attack success rate varies not only according to the memory implementation of the model, but also combinations of instructions and can reach 95% under specific instruction configurations.

</details>


### [194] [PrivacyPeek: Auditing What LLM-Based Agents Acquire, Not Just What They Say](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00152)
*Mingxuan Zhang,Jiahui Han,Dadi Guo,Songze Li,Guanchu Wang,Na Zou,Dongrui Liu,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PrivacyPeek基准，用于评估基于LLM的智能体在**获取阶段**的隐私泄露问题，包含1182个案例覆盖7种获取行为和16个应用领域。实验发现，智能体广泛存在不必要地获取超出任务范围的敏感信息，且任务完成能力与获取阶段泄露存在相关性，现有提示级防御效果有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私基准仅审计智能体响应或输出动作中的信息泄露，但忽略了数据首次进入智能体上下文的**获取阶段**。过度获取的信息只需一次疏忽或攻击即可导致完全泄露，评估其普遍性至关重要。

Method: 构建PrivacyPeek基准，包含两个核心方法：1) **Acquisition Inspection**：检查智能体的工具调用轨迹（调用的工具和接收的数据），检测其是否获取了超出任务范围的敏感信息；2) **Probe Elicitation**：发出后续探针问题，衡量攻击者能多容易地诱导智能体泄露已获取但未披露的敏感信息。覆盖7种获取行为和16个应用领域，共1182个案例。

Result: 在4个模型家族的10个LLM智能体上实验表明：不必要获取敏感信息的行为普遍存在；任务完成能力与获取阶段泄露存在相关性；提示级防御仅能减少少量获取阶段泄露，大部分未被缓解。

Conclusion: 审计智能体获取阶段的隐私泄露既紧迫又必要。现有防御手段不足，需开发更有效的机制来防止数据在进入智能体上下文阶段就被过度获取。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are rapidly advancing, autonomously invoking external tools to complete multi-step tasks for users. However, agents often acquire more sensitive information than the task requires. Existing privacy benchmarks audit what the agent's response or outgoing actions disclose, but overlook the acquisition stage where data first enters the agent's context. The over-acquired information is then one careless action or one attack away from an outright leak. To assess its prevalence, we introduce \emph{PrivacyPeek}, a benchmark for evaluating acquisition-stage privacy leakage of LLM-based agents, with $1{,}182$ cases across $7$ acquisition behaviours and $16$ application domains. Specifically, \emph{Acquisition Inspection} examines the agent's tool-call trajectory, both the tools it invokes and the data it receives, to detect when it acquires sensitive information beyond the task scope. \emph{Probe Elicitation} then issues a follow-up probe and measures how readily an attacker could elicit sensitive information the agent acquired but did not disclose. Our experiments on 10 LLM-based agents across 4 model families show that the unnecessary acquisition of sensitive information is widespread. In addition, we observe a correlation between the task-completion capability and acquisition-stage leakage. Prompt-level defences reduce only a small fraction of acquisition-stage leakage, leaving the majority unmitigated. These results make auditing acquisition-stage privacy both urgent and necessary. Our dataset and code are available at https://github.com/Xuan269/PrivacyPeek-Resource.

</details>


### [195] [A Protocol-Language Model for Network Intrusion (Without Deep Packet Inspection)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00155)
*Vivek Kumar Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PLM-NIDS，利用RWKV-4状态空间模型将网络流量的L3/L4包元数据（长度、时间间隔、TTL、TCP标志、端口哈希）建模为语言，通过困惑度分数检测异常攻击，无需解密TLS 1.3/QUIC加密流量，实现零样本异常检测（PR-AUC=0.93），并在线速率流式处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代NIDS面临结构性矛盾：携带最高威胁情报的协议恰恰被TLS 1.3和QUIC加密，载荷检测失效。作者提出攻击签名可能不在字节中而在"节奏"（流量时序模式）中，从而转向仅基于L3/L4包元数据的免解密检测方案。

Method: (1) 将网络流建模为语言，词汇表由包长度、到达间隔时间、TTL、TCP标志和哈希端口号组成；(2) 使用RWKV-4状态空间模型在344,232个未标注的正常流量上因果预训练，学习良性流量的语法结构；(3) 利用困惑度分数作为异常指标，无需攻击标签即可分离正常与攻击流量；(4) 可选监督微调进一步提升性能。RWKV的O(T)循环推理支持逐包流式处理。

Result: (1) 无监督预训练：因果LM验证损失0.204；(2) 零样本异常检测：PR-AUC=0.93，困惑度干净分离正常与攻击流；(3) LSTM退化为多数类预测器（ROC-AUC≈0.50，F1=0.91），证明RWKV的因果预训练提供了直接分类器不具备的归纳偏置；(4) 监督微调：PR-AUC=0.94，ROC-AUC=0.75，校准阈值下精确率97.7%。

Conclusion: PLM-NIDS证明了网络流量在元数据层面存在可学习的语法结构，攻击会违反这一语法。基于RWKV的时序建模是加密无关的，能透明处理TLS 1.3、QUIC及未来加密协议，且具备线速率流式处理的操作可行性。

Abstract: Modern network intrusion detection systems (NIDS) are caught in a structural contradiction: the protocols carrying the highest threat intelligence are precisely those encrypted under TLS 1.3 and QUIC, where payload inspection yields nothing. We ask a simpler question -- what if the attack signature is not in the bytes, but in the rhythm? -- and answer it by treating network flows as a language whose grammar is written entirely in L3/L4 packet metadata: length, inter-arrival time, TTL, TCP flags, and hashed port numbers. We present PLM-NIDS, which proves three claims in sequence. (1) The grammar exists and is learnable: a RWKV-4 state-space model trained on 344,232 unlabelled Monday flows achieves a causal LM validation loss of 0.204, demonstrating that benign traffic has predictable, statistically consistent structure. (2) Attacks violate this grammar: the per-flow perplexity score cleanly separates benign from attack flows with PR-AUC = 0.93 using zero attack labels at training time. (3) This separation is architecturally nontrivial: an LSTM trained on identical token sequences degenerates to a majority-class predictor (ROC-AUC approximately 0.50, F1 = 0.91 by always predicting "attack"), proving that RWKV's causal pre-training provides an inductive bias unavailable to direct classifiers. Supervised fine-tuning further raises PR-AUC to 0.94 and ROC-AUC to 0.75, with a precision of 97.7% at the calibrated operating threshold. The RWKV backbone's O(T) recurrent inference enables per-packet streaming without flow buffering, making PLM-NIDS operationally viable at line rate. Because it reads only IP/TCP/UDP headers, it is inherently encryption-agnostic: TLS 1.3, QUIC, and future encrypted protocols are handled transparently.

</details>


### [196] [DataShield: Safety-degrading Data Filtering for LLM Benign Instruction Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00160)
*Junbo Zhang,Qianli Zhou,Xinyang Deng,Wen Jiang,Jie Pan,Jinbiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DataShield方法，通过量化样本对模型合规行为的贡献来高效识别良性数据集中可能导致安全退化的样本，核心是利用合规向量提取、合规感知分数和安全退化样本过滤三个组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在良性数据集微调后安全能力会退化，现有识别安全退化样本的方法计算成本高且噪声大，亟需高效准确的样本识别方法。

Method: (1) 合规向量提取：捕获LLM的合规行为倾向；(2) 合规感知分数(CAS)：自动识别最优安全关键层；(3) 安全退化样本过滤：量化训练数据沿合规方向的投影偏移。

Result: 在Llama3-8B、Llama3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上使用Alpaca和Dolly数据集验证了方法的有效性，能准确识别高风险和低风险数据子集。

Conclusion: DataShield能高效识别安全退化样本，开放域问答更易触发安全退化且对应响应更长，为数据中心的防御方法提供新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) suffer from degraded safety capabilities even when fine-tuned with benign datasets. However, existing methods for identifying safety-degrading samples in benign datasets suffer from high computational costs and significant noise issues. In this paper, we propose DataShield to efficiently and effectively identify potential safety-degrading samples. Our key intuition is based on the observation that benign fine-tuning increases the overall response compliance of LLMs. DataShield's key technical insight is to quantify each sample's contribution to the model's compliance behavior as its safety degradation score. DataShield consists of three core components: (1) Compliance Vector Extraction, which captures the LLM's compliance behavior tendency; (2) a novel Compliance-Aware Score (CAS), which automatically identifies the optimal safety-critical layer; and (3) Safety-degrading Sample Filtering, which quantifies the projection shift of training data along the compliance direction. Extensive experimental evaluation on Llama3-8B, Llama3.1-8B, and Qwen2.5-7B using the Alpaca and Dolly benign datasets validates our method's effectiveness in identifying high-risk and low-risk data subsets. We also observe that open-ended question answering is more likely to trigger safety degradation, and corresponding responses tend to be longer. We hope this work can provide new insights into data-centric defense methods. The source code is available at: https://github.com/ZJunBo/DataShield.

</details>


### [197] [Improving IoT Intrusion Detection Through SMOTE-Based Oversampling and Extended Multi-Model Evaluation on Side-Channel Power Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00161)
*Muhammad Khuram Shahzad,Haseeb Khan,Muhammad Masood Khan,Mubashra Bibi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对IoT网络入侵检测中极端类别不平衡（正常:攻击达75,964:1）的问题，采用SMOTE过采样技术平衡数据集，并训练8种机器学习算法。随机森林和极端随机树均达到微平均F1≈0.9989，超越基线论文的0.9983，且极端随机树速度快10倍。引入宏平均F1揭示了被聚合指标掩盖的类别级信息，证明SMOTE平衡对少数攻击类（特别是M+L组合感染）可靠检测的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于功耗的IoT入侵检测研究（Dominguez等人）未能处理极端类别不平衡问题（正常类比攻击类多75,964倍），也未使用平衡训练集评估分类器性能，导致对少数攻击类的检测能力被宏观指标掩盖。

Method: 1) 从原始数据集提取9个子集；2) 对每个子集应用SMOTE过采样，将不平衡比精确调整至1.1；3) 在SMOTE平衡后的6小时数据集上，在相同条件下训练8种算法（随机森林、HistGradientBoosting、LightGBM、极端随机树、XGBoost、k近邻、多层感知机、决策树）；4) 使用微平均F1、宏平均F1、混淆矩阵、F1热力图、ROC曲线和特征重要性分析进行全面评估。

Result: 随机森林微平均F1=0.9989，宏平均F1=0.9794，超越基线论文中时间序列森林的0.9983。极端随机树达到相同性能但速度快10倍。宏平均F1揭示了被聚合指标掩盖的类别级差异。SMOTE平衡后，少数攻击类（尤其是M+L组合感染）的召回率显著提升。特征重要性分析表明，60步功耗窗口中最后时间步的预测信号最为重要。

Conclusion: SMOTE平衡结合集成树方法可有效解决IoT入侵检测中的极端类别不平衡问题；宏平均F1等细粒度评估指标对发现类别级性能差异至关重要；功耗信号中近期时间步包含更多判别信息。

Abstract: The detection of intrusions in IoT-based networks poses challenges that cannot be overcome using traditional machine learning methods. Perhaps the biggest of them is related to the presence of a class imbalance in the side-channel dataset, where the number of samples in the normal class compared to the attacks can reach a ratio of 75,964 to 1. Such an aspect is addressed by Dominguez et al. through the proof of concept of power-based intrusion detection. Unfortunately, neither the authors attempt to cope with the problem of imbalance nor do they assess the classifier performance using a balanced training set. In the current paper, both aspects will be handled at once. First, a Synthetic Minority Oversampling Technique (SMOTE) was performed on all nine possible datasets extracted from the initial one, providing an exact imbalance ratio of 1.1 for each. Then, eight algorithms i.e. Random Forest, HistGradientBoosting, LightGBM, Extra Trees, XGBoost, k-Nearest Neighbors, Multi-Layer Perceptron, and Decision Tree were trained under identical conditions for the SMOTE balanced 6-hour dataset. Random Forest reached a micro-averaged F1 score of 0.9989 and macro F1 of 0.9794, thus outperforming the previously best micro-F1 result obtained by Time Series Forest algorithm from the base paper of 0.9983. Extra Trees provided the same performance as well, but at 10 times faster. The introduction of a macro-F1 metric explicitly in contrast to the base paper assessment reveals important class-level information missed with aggregate performance metrics. Recall rates per-class calculated with confusion matrices, F1 heatmaps, and ROC curves show that minority attack classes, especially those with combined M+L infections, are detected reliably only when using SMOTE balance. Feature importance analysis indicates the latest time steps as the most important predictor signals out of 60 steps in a power window.

</details>


### [198] [Inferring Routing-Layer Defense Mechanisms from Observable Behavior in OLSR-Based MANETs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00184)
*Nadav Schweitzer,Kiril Danilchenko,Ariel Stulman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究能否仅通过被动观测的路由行为特征推断MANET中是否存在路由层防御机制。实验表明，即使防御机制完全嵌入标准OLSR控制流量且不引入新包类型，基于集成模型仍能以最高0.91准确率（AUC 0.96）检测到防御存在，但跨域泛化存在非对称性，且紧凑四特征子集可实现近似对称的迁移性能（约0.86）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作主要关注攻击检测，而对推断网络中是否部署了防御机制的问题关注不足。作者旨在探索被动观测者能否从路由层和控制平面行为中识别出防御的存在，揭示MANET中路由层安全防御的可被侦察风险。

Method: 使用ns-3仿真，在基线、仅攻击、仅防御、攻击+防御四种场景下，分别在静态和移动条件下运行OLSR协议。从被动观测者可获取的路由动态和控制平面活动中提取特征，采用集成模型（如随机森林等）进行二分类，判断是否存在防御机制。特别评估了跨域迁移（静态→移动、移动→静态）的性能，并探索了紧凑不变特征子集。

Result: 域内准确率最高达0.91（AUC 0.96）。跨域泛化非对称：静态训练模型迁移到移动场景时准确率降至约0.67，而移动训练模型迁移到静态场景仍保持约0.84。使用四个关键特征的紧凑子集可实现双向约0.86的近似对称迁移性能。

Conclusion: 即使防御机制完全嵌入标准控制流量、不引入新包类型，其存在仍会在被动可观测的路由行为中留下可检测的统计足迹。这意味着在受保护的MANET部署中，攻击者可能具备侦察防御的能力，对网络安全性构成潜在威胁。

Abstract: Mobile ad hoc networks (MANETs) based on proactive routing protocols such as OLSR remain vulnerable to routing-layer attacks. While prior work has focused primarily on attack detection, the problem of identifying deployed defenses has received comparatively little attention. This work examines whether the presence of a routing-layer defense can be inferred from features derived exclusively from externally observable routing and control-plane behavior. The evaluated Fictive Mitigation mechanism operates entirely within standard OLSR control traffic and introduces no new packet types, making passive detection inherently difficult. Using ns-3 simulations across baseline, attack-only, defense-only, and combined attack-defense regimes under both static and mobile conditions, we derive features from observable routing dynamics and control-plane activity available to a passive observer. Despite the restricted observability available to the adversary, the results show that defense detection remains feasible in this setting. Ensemble models achieve in-domain accuracy up to $0.91$ (AUC $0.96$). Cross-domain generalization is asymmetric: models trained on static data degrade under mobility ($\approx 0.67$), whereas mobile-trained models transfer more robustly ($\approx 0.84$). Restricting the model to a compact invariant feature subset of four metrics yields near-symmetric cross-domain transfer ($\approx 0.86$ in both directions). These findings indicate that the evaluated defense mechanism leaves a detectable statistical footprint in passively observable routing behavior, providing adversaries with a potential reconnaissance capability in protected MANET deployments.

</details>


### [199] [How to Compare the Security of Code Written by Humans to LLM-generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00186)
*Rebecca Balebako,Jasmine Egl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种自动化框架，用于在人类-only、LLM-only和人类-LLM混合三种条件下，对代码安全性进行可重复的、物种公平的对比实验。框架包含提示记录、时间记录、实验设置和多维静态/动态质量分析。通过可行性研究验证，并提供了开源实现，为未来AI与人类代码安全性的实证研究奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏标准化方法来实证比较人类-LLM协作、LLM-only和人类-only三种方式生成代码的安全性，难以判断LLM是提升还是削弱了专业开发者建立的安全基线。

Method: 设计并实现了一个自动化框架，自动记录提示、时间和实验设置，通过多维静态和动态质量分析来度量代码安全性，并提供了开源实现。

Result: 通过可行性研究验证了框架的有效性，提供了"物种公平"比较的实验蓝图，并分享了经验教训。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来关于人类和LLM生成代码安全性的实证研究建立了基础，支持可重复、物种公平的比较实验。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly transforming how software is created and maintained. Comparing LLM-generated code against human-written standards is essential to determine whether these new tools uphold or erode the security baselines established by professional developers. Yet, we lack a standardized method for empirically comparing the security of code produced through human-LLM collaboration against LLM-only, or traditional human-only methods. To facilitate this, we propose an automated framework for conducting comparative studies across human-only, LLM-only, and hybrid conditions. Our approach automates the logging of prompts, timing, and experimental settings, measuring outcomes through multi-dimensional static and dynamic quality analysis. We provide an open-source implementation of this framework to ensure that future researchers can conduct reproducible, species-fair experiments. Importantly, we validate the framework via a feasibility study, providing an experimental blueprint for ``species-fair'' comparisons between human and AI subjects. By sharing lessons learned, we establish a foundation for empirical research on human and LLM-generated code for software security.

</details>


### [200] [A Moderatorless Protocol for WEREWOLF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00190)
*Naoki Kitamura,Hironori Kiya,Hirotaka Ono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种无需主持人或任何数字设备、仅用普通扑克牌即可实现狼人杀游戏的方法。该方法通过公开的卡牌操作和玩家私密角色视角，使狼人能够互相识别协调，预言家能查验玩家身份，核心协议包括基于卡牌的密码学原语（如面朝下承诺、可验证洗牌）和子协议，总卡牌复杂度为O(n³)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统狼人杀依赖可信主持人分配角色、调解秘密行动和公布结果，这提高了参与门槛并引入了单点故障（信任第三方）。作者旨在消除对主持人和数字设备的依赖，降低游戏参与障碍并消除信任假设。

Method: 基于卡牌物理密码学原语（面朝下承诺、可验证洗牌）构建高层子协议，包括角色内信息共享、秘密行动指定和属性测试等子协议。所有卡牌操作公开可审计，玩家根据私人角色对同一公开卡牌序列产生不同解读，从而在不使用私密通信的情况下实现角色功能。

Result: 成功实现无需主持人的完整狼人杀游戏机制：狼人能相互识别并协调，预言家每轮可查验指定玩家是否为狼人。方案支持任意n名玩家，总卡牌复杂度为O(n³)。

Conclusion: 该工作首次展示了仅凭物理卡牌即可完全替代传统狼人杀中的主持人功能，消除了对信任第三方和数字设备的依赖，为社交推理游戏的去中心化实现提供了理论基础和实践方案。

Abstract: Social deduction games, or hidden-role games, are multiplayer games in which players are assigned private roles and act under asymmetric information about other players' roles and actions. In the canonical example Werewolf, werewolves conceal their roles and mislead the other players, while the seer can obtain role information about a chosen player. Thus, a central functionality of such games is controlling which players can access which information. In typical play, this control is implemented by a trusted human moderator, who assigns roles, mediates secret actions, and reveals outcomes. This reliance raises the barrier to participation and introduces a trusted third party as a single point of failure. In this work, we show that Werewolf can be played without a moderator or any digital device, using only ordinary playing cards. Our construction maintains a shared pool of cards that is observable to all players and manipulated according to a common public procedure, while its interpretation depends on each player's private role. This induces role-dependent views from a single public sequence of card operations. Consequently, even without private messages, werewolves can identify one another and coordinate, and the seer can test whether a chosen player is a werewolf in each round. The proposed implementation is built from card-based physical cryptographic primitives, such as face-down commitments and verifiable shuffles, and higher-level subprotocols for intra-role information sharing, secret action designation, and attribute testing. These subprotocols implement the moderator's core functions while keeping all card operations public and auditable under standard assumptions on physical card operations. We show that the resulting complete moderatorless implementation of Werewolf scales to an arbitrary number n of players using O(n^3) cards.

</details>


### [201] [Bit-Exact AI Inference Verification Without Performance Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00279)
*Naci Cankaya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文论证了通过软件模拟和正确的重算信息，可以在不同GPU上实现LLM推理的逐比特精确重计算，从而将累积舍入误差转变为可审计的软硬件签名，而非验证性的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载的验证是可信AI治理的前提，但GPU浮点运算的非确定性表面特征迫使审计者接受近似输出匹配，这给隐蔽对手留下了利用不可验证自由度进行攻击的空间。

Method: 通过软件模拟的方式跨多个NVIDIA GPU变体对LLM推理进行逐比特精确重计算，并分析现代推理引擎（vLLM, HF transformers）在无需设置确定性标志的情况下产生的确定性但非不变输出。

Result: 证明了无需访问相同硬件即可实现逐比特精确重计算；累积的舍入误差可以作为推理所用软硬件设置的可审计签名，而非验证性限制。

Conclusion: GPU浮点运算的舍入误差不应被视为验证的障碍，反而可以作为一种可审计的签名来增强AI治理的可信度；隐蔽对手无法利用未验证的自由度进行攻击。

Abstract: Verifying claims about AI workloads is a pre- requisite for credible AI governance of covert adversaries (who comply with monitoring only when detection likelihood is high), yet the ap- parent non-determinism of GPU floating-point arithmetic forces auditors to accept approximate output matches. Covert adversaries can exploit un- verifiable degrees of freedom in monitored compu- tation. Attack vectors include steganography, un- reported modification of inference software, and covert computation via unreported batch elements. Empirically, we analyze how modern inference engines (vLLM, HF transformers) produce deter- ministic but non-invariant outputs, without need- ing to set performance-compromising determin- ism flags, if the right information is available for re-computation and no atomic functions are called in the backend. We demonstrate that such bitwise- precise re-computation does not require access to identical hardware, via a software-only emula- tion of LLM inference across multiple NVIDIA GPU variants. Thus, accumulated rounding errors can be an auditable signature of the software and hardware setup used for inference, instead of a constraint on verifiability.

</details>


### [202] [Beyond Edge Coverage: Per-Task Data-Flow Extraction at Kernel Function Boundaries via LLVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00455)
*Yunseong Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BOUNDARY FLOW 是 LLVM 内核插桩框架，通过扩展 KCOV 捕获函数参数/返回值数据流（<PC, arg_idx, arg_size, ptr, offsets[]>），为内核模糊测试提供状态感知反馈，支持 Rust 模块且开销 <3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于边覆盖的内核模糊器（如 syzkaller）无法区分仅在参数值上不同的执行路径（如 copy_from_user 不同 size 参数触发的相同基本块），缺乏数据流感知能力。

Method: 1) LLVM 编译插桩 pass（-fsanitize-coverage=dataflow-args, dataflow-ret）在函数入口/返回处发射轻量回调，捕获结构化元组；2) 通过 DWARF DICompositeType 元数据自动分解复合类型，无需源码注释；3) 内核设备 /sys/kernel/debug/kcov_dataflow 提供无锁每任务环形缓冲区；4) 后编译流水线（rustc, opt, llc）实现 Rust 内核模块插桩。

Result: 在五类漏洞（OOB、UAF、double-free、10 层深度传播、Rust FFI、Rust for Linux 模块）上评估，插桩路径开销 <3%。

Conclusion: 该框架双重用途：模糊测试获得状态感知反馈以引导突变进入值依赖的状态转换；安全分析师获得确定性参数记录用于根因分析，无需 printk/kprobe 开销。

Abstract: Coverage-guided kernel fuzzers such as syzkaller rely on edge coverage (trace-pc) as their sole feedback signal. This context-blind approach cannot distinguish execution paths that differ only in argument values. for example, two invocations of copy_from_user() with different size parameters hit identical basic blocks yet have vastly different security implications. We present BOUNDARY FLOW, an LLVM-based instrumentation framework that extends Linux KCOV with data-flow extraction of function arguments and return values. A compiler pass (-fsanitize-coverage=dataflow-args, dataflow-ret) emits lightweight callbacks capturing a structured tuple <PC, arg_idx, arg_size, ptr, offsets[]> at function entry and <PC, ret_size, ptr, offsets[]> at return. Composite types are automatically decomposed via DWARF DICompositeType metadata with zero source annotation. A separate kernel device(/sys/kernel/debug/kcov_dataflow) provides lock-free per-task ring buffers with no inter ference to existing KCOV or syzkaller infrastructure. We demonstrate dual utility: fuzzers gain state-aware feedback for mutation guidance into value-dependent state transitions, and security analysts obtain deterministic argument records for root-cause analysis without printk or kprobe overhead. A post-compilation pipeline (rustc, opt, llc) enables Rust kernel module instrumentation without modifying rustc, the only runtime method for capturing Rust function arguments given that drgn/vmcore fails under-O2 DWARF elision. Evaluated on five vulnerability classes (OOB, UAF, double-free, 10 deep chain propagation, Rust FFI, Rust for Linux Modules) with <3% overhead on instrumented paths.

</details>


### [203] [Stochastic Analysis of Cybersecurity Defense Strategies Under Single Attack Scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00481)
*Song-Kyoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于随机过程的主动网络安全防御时机决策框架，通过连续观测机制和Laplace-Carson变换，推导出防御时刻的概率密度及攻击前后观测时间的条件期望，为低延迟主动防御提供参数校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络安全防御时机决策缺乏严格的随机建模方法，难以量化防御时刻对威胁强度的敏感性，亟需一种能够精确计算防御时刻概率分布的理论框架。

Method: 将防御过程建模为连续观测机制，防御时刻与后续观测时隙服从独立指数分布；利用Laplace-Carson变换结合首次超越理论推导联合检测函数；在马尔可夫泊松到达假设下进行边缘化，得到防御时刻的概率密度及攻击前后观测时间的条件期望。

Result: 给出了防御时刻边缘分布和条件期望的闭式解，实现了防御时刻密度可视化，定量评估了防御时机对威胁强度的敏感性，支持观测参数的低延迟校准。

Conclusion: 该研究将随机对决方法与实际网络安全应用相结合，为主动防御时机的精确校准提供了理论基础，主要贡献包括边缘分布与期望值的显式推导以及防御时刻密度的可视化。

Abstract: This research presents a novel stochastic framework for proactive cybersecurity defense timing under a single attack scenario. The approach models the defense process as a continuous observation mechanism in which the defense instant and the subsequent observation slot follow independent exponential distributions. Laplace-Carson transforms combined with first-excess theory yield the joint detection function that brackets the attack moment. Marginalization under Markovian Poisson arrivals then produces the probability density of the defense moment and conditional expectations of pre-attack and post-attack observation times. These closed-form results enable quantitative assessment of defense timing sensitivity to threat intensity and support precise calibration of observation parameters for low-latency proactive measures. Major contributions include the explicit derivation of marginal distributions and expected values, visualization of defense moment density, and the bridging of stochastic duel methodology with practical cybersecurity applications.

</details>


### [204] [Confused ChatGPT: Cross-App Context Poisoning via First-Party APIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00485)
*Chao Wang,Somesh Jha,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了ChatGPT Apps（2025年10月上线）中存在的跨应用上下文投毒攻击：由于共享聊天上下文缺乏隔离机制，恶意应用可通过API向上下文中注入内容，进而操纵LLM协同驻留的良性应用。该漏洞源于架构设计缺陷（扁平、无标签、无隔离的共享上下文），而非简单补丁可修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ChatGPT Apps采用应用内应用范式，第三方应用与用户共享同一个聊天上下文，且生态从2025年12月的122个应用快速增长至2026年5月的888个，但其安全性此前未被研究。作者旨在系统性地调查这一新型架构中的安全风险。

Method: 作者识别并定义了跨应用上下文投毒攻击，其三个关键特性为：(1)注入内容在共享上下文中跨轮次持久存在；(2)效果通过用户后续调用的共驻良性应用显现；(3)投递向量是面向所有连接应用的一阶API。发现sendFollowUpMessage是最直接、最强大的注入通道，且两个未文档化参数（systemPrompt和isVisible）可增强至静默的系统优先级写入。基于此实现了混乱代理攻击，并评估了条件式和命令式两种载荷风格在六种ChatGPT模型上的效果。

Result: 成功实现了跨应用上下文投毒攻击，恶意应用可通过污染共享上下文，使LLM在查询该上下文时被操控，从而对共驻的良性应用实施操纵。未文档化参数在发现时仍可访问。该漏洞的根本原因是架构层面缺乏隔离机制。

Conclusion: 修复该漏洞需要架构层面的改造（即引入隔离机制），而非简单的补丁修复。作者已向OpenAI披露了发现，但这一架构缺陷属于设计使然：支持跨应用组合的共享上下文，正是导致跨应用投毒的同一扁平命名空间。

Abstract: ChatGPT Apps, launched by OpenAI on Oct. 6, 2025, introduce an app-in-app paradigm in which third-party applications share a single chat context with the user and with every other connected app. The ecosystem grew from 122 apps in Dec. 2025 to 888 by May 2026, yet its security has remained uninvestigated. We identify cross-app context poisoning, a variant of indirect prompt injection distinguished by three properties: 1) the injection persists in the shared chat context across turns; 2) the effect surfaces through a different co-resident app the user later invokes; and 3) the delivery vectors are first-party APIs exposed to every connected app. We find multiple APIs capable of writing app-controlled content into the shared context, with sendFollowUpMessage as the most direct and potent channel. Two undocumented parameters that the runtime silently accepts, systemPrompt and isVisible, amplify this channel to silent, system-priority writes. Leveraging this channel, we realize a confused-deputy attack in which a malicious app poisons the context so that the LLM, consulting that context, enables manipulation against benign co-resident apps. We demonstrate two payload styles (conditional and imperative) and evaluate them across six current ChatGPT models. The root cause is architectural: the LLM's context is a persistent, flat, untagged data store shared by user and apps, with no isolation. Every mature multi-tenant platform, from Multics virtual memory to Android UIDs and iOS sandbox profiles, paid the isolation cost before admitting third parties; ChatGPT Apps did not. Fixing this requires an architectural change, not a patch. We disclosed our findings to OpenAI; the undocumented parameters remain accessible at the time of writing, and the architectural gap is by design: the shared context that enables cross-app composition is the same flat namespace that enables cross-app poisoning.

</details>


### [205] ["I Strongly Suspect This Website Is a Scam": Benchmarking PII Leakage and Detection without Defense in Autonomous Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00497)
*Soham Roy,Sarthakbrata Halder,Arya Bharaty,Vaibhav Bhaskar,Yash Sinha,Dhruv Kumar,Srikant Panda,Murari Mandal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过预注册基准测试Scammer4U，系统量化了社会工程攻击对前沿Web智能体的危害，发现关键PII泄露率达54-93%，且存在检测-行动鸿沟（即便检测到可疑仍提交PII），表明依赖智能体自身识别攻击的防御策略存在根本缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会工程攻击在互联网上广泛存在，但缺乏系统性的基准测试来量化其对部署的自主Web智能体的实际风险。

Method: 构建Scammer4U基准测试，包含91个攻击者控制环境和10个良性孪生基线，覆盖8种攻击向量和16个站点类别，采用8轴因子分类法隔离单个攻击设计因素的因果贡献。

Result: 前沿智能体在无隐私指导下的关键PII泄露率达54-93%，而良性孪生基线为0%；检测到可疑后仍提交PII的比例为35.9%（vs 未检测到的66.1%），存在30.2%的检测-行动鸿沟。

Conclusion: 依赖智能体自身攻击识别能力的防御策略存在根本缺陷，需要独立于智能体推理循环的输出级拦截机制来阻止对外提交。

Abstract: Deceptive web content, widely instantiated across the internet and commonly known as \textit{social-engineering attacks}, manipulates autonomous web agents into submitting users' personally identifiable information (PII) to attacker-controlled endpoints. In this paper, we show that social-engineering attacks are highly effective at extracting critical-tier PII from frontier web agents, posing a severe risk to deployed agentic systems. To quantify this risk, we introduce \textbf{\textsc{Scammer4U}}, a pre-registered benchmark of 91 attacker-controlled environments and 10 benign-twin baselines, spanning 8 attack vectors and 16 site categories on an 8-axis factorial taxonomy that isolates the causal contribution of individual attack design factors. Across frontier agents, we find that critical-tier PII leakage reaches 54--93\% under no privacy guidance, compared to 0\% on benign-twin baselines, confirming that leakage is attack-attributable rather than incidental form-filling. Escalating prompt-level mitigation yields sharply model-dependent reductions across the four families and remains insufficient to reliably prevent critical PII submission at the pooled level. Most critically, we identify a detection--action gap: agents whose reasoning an independent LLM judge confirms has flagged the site as suspicious still submit critical PII in 35.9\% of sessions, versus 66.1\% when no suspicion is verbalized, a 30.2\% gap robust across all four model families. Our findings reveal that defenses conditioned on the agent's own recognition of an attack are gating on the wrong signal, motivating output-level interception of outbound submissions that operates independently of the agent's reasoning loop.

</details>


### [206] [Authenticity Debt and the Synthetic Content Threat Landscape: A Layered Framework for Trust, Provenance, and IP Governance in the Generative AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00621)
*Shubhashis Sengupta,Benjamin McCarty,Milind Savagaonkar,Rhine Andotra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"真实性债务"概念，系统梳理生成式AI在真实性、来源、完整性和问责四个层面带来的风险，论证单一技术控制（水印、溯源框架、检测技术）不足，提出基于零信任架构的分层参考架构，并分析欧盟AI法案、美国FTC等监管框架，倡导将真实性建设作为机构基础设施而非事后补救。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使高保真内容以近乎零边际成本生产、修改和分发，传统控制手段在开放、对抗性环境中无法独立应对真实性层面的系统性风险，需要综合性的技术与管理方案。

Method: 提出多维度生成式AI危害与攻击向量分类法；调研数字水印、C2PA/Adobe CAI溯源框架、检测技术等能力与失效模式；借鉴零信任架构与企业治理框架，设计集成密码学溯源、人在回路验证和持续治理的分层参考架构。

Result: 得出单一机制在开放、对抗性环境中不足以保障真实性；提出分层参考架构可实现规模化的可防御真实性；识别出欧盟AI法案、美国FTC、NIST AI RMF等监管框架中的实践指导原则。

Conclusion: 组织应将真实性建设作为制度性基础设施，而非事后补充；需要在技术控制、治理流程和监管合规之间构建多层协同防御体系。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence has fundamentally changed how content is now produced. It has enabled how high-fidelity text, images, audio, and videos are created, modified, and redistributed at near-zero marginal cost. This shift exposes enterprises and ecosystems to a number of risks across four reinforcing authenticity layers -- authenticity, provenance, integrity, and accountability -- that traditional controls are inadequate to address in isolation. We introduce the concept of authenticity debt: the cumulative institutional liability that accumulates when organizations deploy AI-generated content without preserving verifiable origin, integrity, and accountability, deferring exposure that surfaces under regulatory, legal, or market scrutiny. This paper presents a comprehensive, multi-dimensional taxonomy of generative AI harms and attack vectors, surveys the capabilities and failure modes of technical controls including digital watermarking, provenance frameworks (C2PA, Adobe CAI), and detection technologies, and argues that no single mechanism is sufficient in open, adversarial, and evolving environments. Drawing on Zero Trust Architecture principles and enterprise governance frameworks, we propose a layered reference architecture that integrates cryptographic provenance, human-in-the-loop verification, and continuous governance to sustain defensible authenticity at scale. We further examine the regulatory landscape (EU AI Act, U.S.\ FTC, NIST AI RMF) and identify practical guiding principles for organizations seeking to build authenticity as institutional infrastructure rather than an afterthought.

</details>


### [207] [NICE: A Framework for Declarative and Machine-Checkable Vulnerability Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00625)
*Minh-Luân Nguyen,Olivier Levillain,Julien Malka,Stefano Zacchiroli,Théo Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NICE是一个基于NixOS的CVE复现框架，通过声明式配方自动构建和验证漏洞环境，支持19个真实CVE的复现，并适用于安全教育和漏洞报告审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件漏洞复现对安全研究人员、开源维护者和教育工作者至关重要，但现有方法难以复现漏洞，且随时间的推移，重建可利用的漏洞环境变得越来越困难。

Method: NICE使用声明式配方构建NixOS虚拟机配置，包含脚本化漏洞利用场景和机器可检查的断言，提供漏洞利用的事实证据。该方法支持共享、验证、审查和长期可复现性。

Result: 在19个真实CVE上评估，覆盖多种CWE类别、攻击向量和目标类型（用户空间、系统软件、内核、图形应用），证明NICE能生成简洁配方和集成测试，复现漏洞环境并提供利用证明。

Conclusion: NICE适用于安全教育培训（如创建网络靶场）和漏洞报告审计，其可复现性和可审查性使报告更易审核和验证。

Abstract: Reproducing software vulnerabilities is fundamental to security researchers, open-source maintainers, and educators. Yet, vulnerabilities remain hard to reproduce today, and even when they can be reproduced, recreating a software environment where the vulnerability can be exploited becomes harder and harder over time. We present NICE, the NIx CvE reproduction framework, which uses declarative recipes to build and automatically validate vulnerable environments. In NICE, a reproduced CVE comprises one or more NixOS virtual machine configurations, a scripted exploitation scenario, and machine-checkable assertions that provide factual evidence of exploitation. This design facilitates sharing, validation, review, and long-term reproducibility.
  We evaluate NICE on 19 diverse real-world CVEs spanning multiple CWE categories, attack vectors, and target types (user-space, system software, kernel, and graphical applications). We show that NICE allows to produce concise recipes and integration tests that reproduce vulnerable environments and provide proofs of exploitation.
  NICE is applicable to security education and training (e.g., creating cyber ranges), but also to vulnerability reporting, where its reproducibility and reviewability properties can make reports easier to audit and verify.

</details>


### [208] [The Invitation Trap: Proactive Availability Backdoor in LLMs via Conversational Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00654)
*He Wang,Jun Feng,Hong Sun,Pengfei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为“主动可用性后门（PAB）”的新型攻击范式，通过利用对齐大语言模型固有的“乐于助人”特性，将攻击向量从被动等待转变为主动社会工程学诱导，使模型主动引诱用户执行植入触发器的查询，在多种模型和领域上实现了73.1%的有效攻击成功率。同时提出了针对性的防御方法Anti-PAB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对大语言模型的后门攻击通常由攻击者操控且处于被动等待状态，攻击效率和隐蔽性有限。作者希望将攻击范式从被动转变为主动，利用模型自身的乐于助人特性来主动诱骗用户，从而提升攻击的侵略性、精准度和隐蔽性，揭示LLM可用性面临的新型隐蔽威胁。

Method: 提出PAB范式，通过将后门触发与主动推荐/建议行为结合，利用对齐LLM的乐于助人特性主动向用户提供带有触发器的查询建议。提出基于大五人格模型选定维度的双智能体生态仿真框架，使用少样本提示部署PAB，在真实场景下评估威胁。同时提出Anti-PAB防御方法。

Result: PAB在多种模型和领域上表现显著，采用联合概率（攻击发生率×攻击成功率）计算的有效攻击成功率达到73.1%，证明了该范式的攻击性、精准性和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: LLM的乐于助人性可以被武器化来损害其可用性，暴露了对LLM用户的严重隐蔽威胁。研究团队开源了所有脚本和数据集。

Abstract: Current backdoor attacks against LLMs are typically manipulated by the attacker and remain passive. In this paper, we introduce the \textbf{Proactive Availability Backdoor (PAB)}, a novel paradigm that shifts the attack vector from passive waiting to active social engineering. By weaponizing the inherent helpfulness of aligned LLMs, PAB proactively traps users into executing trigger-implanted queries by offering suggestions, achieving high aggressiveness, precision and stealthiness. To rigorously evaluate its threat in a real-life context, we introduce a dual-agent ecological simulation framework based on selected dimensions of the Five-Factor Model, and deploy PAB with few-shot prompts. Being validated on different models and domains, PAB performs remarkably and its effective attack success rate, which calculates the joint probability of attack incidence rate and attack success rate, goes to \textbf{73.1\%}. We also introduce \textbf{Anti-PAB}, a defense method tailored for PAB. Our findings reveal that the helpfulness of LLMs can be weaponized to compromise availability, exposing a serious hidden threat to LLMs users. We release all the scripts and datasets in the experiments at \texttt{https://anonymous.4open.science/r/PAB-ANONYMOUS/}.

</details>


### [209] [NeuroLog: Reasoning You Can Audit -- Neuro-Symbolic Vulnerability Discovery via LLM Facts, Datalog, and SMT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00669)
*Sanjay Rawat*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: NeuroLog是一个端到端的无需构建的漏洞发现管道，结合LLM提取类型化数据流事实、Souffle规则网格进行跨函数分析、Z3 SMT求解器过滤不可行发现，并通过第二个LLM将SAT模型转化为可触发崩溃的Python程序。在多个真实代码库中重新发现了8个CVE类问题，并在libarchive HEAD上发现了5个内存安全错误（其中4个先前未报告）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有C/C++源代码漏洞发现方法存在两难选择：重型静态分析器需要完整构建环境才能运行，而LLM虽然能直接读取源码但会编造细节且无法跟踪跨函数数据流。作者希望设计一种无需构建、端到端的自动化管道，结合LLM和形式化方法的各自优势。

Method: NeuroLog管道包含三层：(1) LLM逐函数提取类型化数据流事实；(2) Souffle规则网格将这些事实组合为跨函数发现；(3) Z3后过滤不可行发现并为每个幸存者输出SAT模型。还引入运行时证据（从少量语料种子提取范围不变式）收紧SMT问题。最终第二个LLM代理读取SAT模型并编写Python程序生成候选崩溃输入，由AddressSanitizer验证。

Result: 在stb、cJSON、libxml2、FFmpeg解复用器切片和curl 8.3.0上，NeuroLog端到端重新发现了8个CVE类问题（包括CVSS 9.8的SOCKS5堆溢出CVE-2023-38545），均经ASan确认。在libarchive HEAD上发现5个内存安全错误（4个先前未报告），多个修复已合并。提取耗时约37秒、成本$0.005（stb）；崩溃合成在两次LLM迭代内将静态发现转化为102字节的stb_vorbis崩溃；三个Matroska种子的不变式过滤消除了FFmpeg解复用器13.2%的可行集合。

Conclusion: NeuroLog证明了将LLM提取的数据流事实与形式化方法（Datalog + SMT）结合的有效性，且无需构建环境。其关键创新在于：(1) LLM衍生的事实库替代传统静态分析；(2) 无构建管道；(3) SAT模型作为崩溃合成的输入工件而非简单的真/假判定。该方法在真实代码库上发现了已知和未知漏洞，展示了实用价值。

Abstract: Vulnerability discovery on C/C++ source asks the analyst to choose between heavyweight static analysers, which need a working build before a single query runs, and free-form LLMs, which read source readily but invent details and lose track of cross-function dataflow on real codebases. We present NeuroLog, an end-to-end build-free pipeline that assigns each layer the role it is uniquely good at: an LLM extracts typed dataflow facts one function at a time; a Souffle rule mesh composes those facts into cross-function findings; a Z3 post-pass filters infeasible findings and emits a SAT model for each survivor. To go beyond pure static reasoning we also fold in runtime evidence: likely range invariants from a handful of corpus seeds tighten the SMT problem at near-zero cost. A second LLM agent reads each SAT model and writes a Python program that produces a candidate crashing input, validated by an AddressSanitizer harness. Combining static-narrowing-SMT (Saturn, Pinpoint) and Datalog-with-SMT (Formulog) is prior art; new here are an LLM-derived fact base, a no-build pipeline, and the SAT model as an artifact (input to crash synthesis) rather than a yes/no verdict. Across stb, cJSON, libxml2, an FFmpeg demuxer slice, and curl 8.3.0, NeuroLog re-discovers eight CVE-class issues end-to-end, including the CVSS-9.8 SOCKS5 heap overflow CVE-2023-38545, each ASan-confirmed. On libarchive HEAD we surface five memory-safety bugs (four previously unreported) across the cpio reader and the XAR/WARC/7zip writers; all filed upstream, several fixes merged, with the cpio use-after-free acknowledged in seven hours. Extraction takes ~37 s and $0.005 on stb; crash synthesis turned a static finding into a 102-byte stb_vorbis crash in two LLM iterations (no fuzzer); a likely-invariant filter from three Matroska seeds eliminates 13.2% of the FFmpeg-demuxer feasible set.

</details>


### [210] [Quality-Diversity Evolution for Discovering Diverse Vulnerabilities in LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00801)
*Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于质量-多样性进化框架（MAP-Elites）的LLM红队测试方法，在语义层面演化可解释的攻击策略而非token序列。在GPT-4o-mini、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 2.0 Flash和一个开放权重编码模型上的实验揭示了不同的模型漏洞特征：GPT-4o-mini对假设性和多轮框架结合ROT13编码最脆弱（fitness 0.8），Gemini对直接攻击+ROT13和多轮+Leetspeak最脆弱（0.8），而Claude在所有策略上均表现出模糊响应（最高0.4）。该方法生成可解释的、系统性地揭示模型特定弱点的攻击，为改进LLM安全性和评估未来前沿模型提供了可复现的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM对抗性测试方法存在覆盖空白：人工红队测试无法规模化，LLM-as-attacker方法存在模式崩溃，基于梯度的方法产生不可解释的乱码。需要一种可扩展、可解释且能系统性发现模型漏洞的自动化测试方法。

Method: 采用质量-多样性进化框架（MAP-Elites），在语义层面演化攻击策略而非token序列，维护一个覆盖策略类型、编码方法、提示长度等行为维度的多样化攻击档案库。

Result: GPT-4o-mini对假设性和多轮框架结合ROT13编码最脆弱（fitness 0.8），Gemini对直接攻击+ROT13和多轮+Leetspeak最脆弱（0.8），Claude在所有策略上均表现稳健（最高0.4），揭示不同模型具有不同的漏洞特征。

Conclusion: 语义层面的质量-多样性进化框架能够生成可解释的、系统性地揭示模型特定弱点的攻击，提供可操作的见解以改进LLM安全性，并为评估未来前沿模型建立了可复现的基线。

Abstract: Current approaches to LLM adversarial testing suffer from coverage gaps: manual red-teaming does not scale, LLM-as-attacker methods exhibit mode collapse, and gradient-based approaches produce uninterpretable gibberish. We introduce a quality-diversity evolutionary framework that operates at the semantic level, evolving interpretable attack strategies rather than token sequences. Using MAP-Elites, we maintain a diverse archive of attacks across behavioral dimensions (strategy type, encoding method, prompt length). In experiments across GPT-4o-mini, Claude 3.5 Sonnet, Gemini 2.0 Flash, and an open-weight coding model (Devstral-small-2), we discover distinct vulnerability profiles: GPT-4o-mini is vulnerable to hypothetical and multi-turn framing combined with ROT13 encoding (fitness 0.8), Gemini to direct attacks with ROT13 and multi-turn with Leetspeak (0.8), while Claude shows uniformly ambiguous responses across all strategies (max 0.4). The semantic representation produces interpretable attacks that reveal systematic, model-specific weaknesses, providing actionable insights for improving LLM safety and a reproducible baseline for evaluating future frontier models. Code and experiment artifacts are released at https://github.com/bassrehab/red-queen.

</details>


### [211] [Cross-Generational Transfer of Adversarial Attacks Reveals Non-Monotonic Safety Alignment in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00813)
*Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对Gemma系列四代模型（7B-31B）进行质量-多样性进化（MAP-Elites）自动化红队探测，发现LLM安全对齐并非随代际单调提升：Gemma 3（12B）的攻击成功率（68.7%）显著高于Gemma 2（45.5%）和Gemma 4（33.9%），且跨代攻击迁移率在Gemma 3上为44-46%，在Gemma 4上仅为14-18%。版权和网络犯罪漏洞在所有代际接近100%，而虚假信息ASR从Gemma 2的29%跃升至Gemma 3的99%，在Gemma 4中仍高达77%。这些模式在静态基准中不可见，仅通过自适应纵向探测才能发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM安全对齐是否随模型代际单调提升，并揭示静态基准无法发现的跨代安全退化模式。

Method: 使用质量-多样性进化算法（MAP-Elites）作为自动化红队探测工具，对Gemma系列四代模型（7B-31B）进行系统性攻击测试，并设计跨代攻击存档回放实验评估迁移性。

Result: 安全对齐非单调：Gemma 3 ASR（68.7%）显著高于Gemma 2（45.5%）和Gemma 4（33.9%）；跨代攻击迁移率Gemma 3（44-46%）远高于Gemma 4（14-18%）；版权和网络犯罪ASR近100%；虚假信息ASR在Gemma 2→3从29%跃升至99%，Gemma 4仍高达77%。

Conclusion: LLM安全对齐存在代际间非单调退化现象，静态基准无法捕捉此类模式，需采用自适应纵向探测方法；Gemma 4的安全改进具有一定泛化性但未完全解决虚假信息漏洞。

Abstract: Safety alignment in LLMs does not improve monotonically across model generations. Studying four generations of Google's Gemma family (7B-31B) with quality-diversity evolution (MAP-Elites) as an automated red-teaming probe, we find that Gemma 3 (12B) exhibits 68.7% +/- 5.7% attack success rate (ASR; mean +/- std, 3 seeds), significantly higher than its predecessor Gemma 2 (45.5% +/- 7.2%; p = 0.030, paired bootstrap) and its successor Gemma 4 (33.9% +/- 1.8%). Replaying evolved attack archives across generations reveals that attacks from other generations transfer to Gemma 3 at 44-46% but only 14-18% to Gemma 4, indicating that Gemma 4's safety gains generalize beyond the attack distributions evolved against earlier generations. Under our 8B judge, copyright and cybercrime vulnerabilities register at near-100% across all generations, though a second-judge audit (Section 6) suggests the copyright result is sensitive to judge choice. Misinformation ASR jumps from 29% to 99% between Gemma 2 and Gemma 3 and remains elevated at 77% in Gemma 4, indicating the regression was not fully addressed. These patterns are invisible to static benchmarks and emerge only through adaptive, longitudinal probing. All experiments use 3 random seeds with a unified self-hosted judge; code and artifacts are available at https://github.com/bassrehab/red-queen.

</details>


### [212] [GCVE: A Decentralized Model for Vulnerability Identification, Publication, and Operational Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00856)
*Alexandre Dulaunoy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GCVE提出了一种去中心化、开放、可扩展的漏洞标识、发布与丰富模型，结合自治编号机构、轻量分配规则、分布式发布与最佳实践，并基于漏洞查找参考实现构建集体安全知识生产的开放基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前漏洞生态存在中心化系统与独立发布之间的割裂：中心化系统提供严格管控和广泛认可的标识符，但大量发布者独立发布安全公告，缺乏统一的发现、关联、丰富和复用机制。

Method: 提出GCVE作为社会技术标准化方案，包含自治的GCVE编号机构、轻量分配规则、分布式发布、开放最佳实践（BCP）及参考实现。扩展漏洞记录概念涵盖分配、披露、目击、拒绝标识符、观察、被利用漏洞信息和丰富记录。通过BCP流程支持技术互操作性和可修订的操作实践，并设计了包括AI导向扩展在内的扩展机制。

Result: 漏洞查找（vulnerability-lookup）作为参考实现，聚合多源数据，支持GCVE发布与消费，实现分布式已知被利用漏洞数据，并提供自动丰富的漏洞数据流。

Conclusion: GCVE将漏洞协调重新定义为不仅是指标识符分配，而是面向集体安全知识生产的开放基础设施，借鉴MISP生态系统的经验，在去中心化控制与全局唯一性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The Global CVE initiative (GCVE) proposes a decentralized, open, and extensible model for vulnerability identification, publication, and enrichment. It addresses a gap in today's vulnerability ecosystem: centralized systems provide rigorous control and widely recognized identifiers, while many producers publish advisories independently without a shared fabric for discovery, correlation, enrichment, and reuse.
  This paper presents GCVE as a socio-technical standardization effort combining autonomous GCVE Numbering Authorities, lightweight allocation rules, distributed publication, open Best Current Practices, and practical reference implementations. The model preserves global uniqueness while allowing participants to publish according to their operational needs. It also broadens the concept of a vulnerability record to cover assignments, disclosures, sightings, rejected identifiers, observations, exploited vulnerability information, and enrichment records.
  The paper describes how the GCVE BCP process supports technical interoperability and amendable operational practice, including practical guidance for vulnerability handling and disclosure. It also examines the extension mechanism, including AI-oriented extensions, as a way to evolve the standard without centralizing control.
  A particular focus is placed on vulnerability-lookup as the reference implementation. It aggregates multiple sources, supports GCVE publication and consumption, implements distributed Known Exploited Vulnerability data, and enables automatically enriched vulnerability data streams. Building on lessons from the MISP ecosystem, GCVE frames vulnerability coordination not only as identifier allocation, but as open infrastructure for collective security knowledge production.

</details>


### [213] [A Lightweight Hybrid MLP-Based Framework for Real-Time Phishing URL Detection Using Structural URL Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00889)
*Uche Unoke Emmanuel,Gideon Francis Oghie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级混合框架，结合黑名单筛选和基于MLP分类器的结构URL特征分析，在PhiUSIIL数据集上达到99.24%准确率，推理延迟仅1.2ms/URL，适用于资源受限环境下的实时钓鱼URL检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑名单和基于规则的钓鱼检测方法具有滞后性，难以识别新型钓鱼URL，亟需一种轻量级、实时且高精度的检测方案。

Method: 构建混合框架：黑名单预筛 + MLP分类器；仅提取16个URL结构特征（不依赖网页内容/第三方API/视觉渲染），在PhiUSIIL数据集（235,795条标记URL）上训练评估。

Result: MLP达到99.24%准确率、98.74%精确率、99.95%召回率、99.34% F1分数、99.65% ROC-AUC；混合架构平均推理延迟1.2ms/URL，峰值吞吐量4200 URLs/s；性能优于RF、LR、XGBoost、LightGBM、CatBoost。

Conclusion: 该框架为资源受限环境下的实时钓鱼URL检测提供了高精度且计算高效的解决方案，并通过CyberGuard桌面原型验证了部署可行性。

Abstract: Phishing attacks remain a major cybersecurity threat, exploiting deceptive URLs to steal sensitive user information. Traditional blacklist and rule-based detection approaches are reactive and often fail to identify newly emerging phishing URLs. This paper proposes a lightweight hybrid framework for real-time phishing URL detection that combines blacklist-based screening with a Multi-Layer Perceptron (MLP) classifier operating solely on structural URL features. The framework extracts 16 URL-derived features capturing structural, domain-based, and security-related characteristics without requiring webpage content access, third-party APIs, or visual rendering, making it computationally efficient for real-time deployment. The system was trained and evaluated on the PhiUSIIL phishing dataset containing 235,795 labelled URLs. Experimental results show that the proposed MLP achieved 99.24% accuracy, 98.74% precision, 99.95% recall, 99.34% F1-score, and 99.65% ROC-AUC, outperforming Random Forest, Logistic Regression, XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost under the same evaluation setting. The hybrid architecture achieved an average inference latency of 1.2 ms per URL and a peak throughput of 4,200 URLs per second under concurrent processing. A functional desktop application prototype, CyberGuard, further demonstrates deployment viability. The results indicate that the proposed framework provides an accurate and computationally efficient solution for real-time phishing URL detection in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [214] [Framework for Discovering GPS Spoofing Attacks in Drone Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00904)
*Yingao Elaine Yao,Pritam Dash,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了无人机蜂群控制算法中的安全漏洞，提出两种模糊测试工具 SwarmFuzzGraph 和 SwarmFuzzBinary 来发现 swarm 传播漏洞 (SPVs)，并通过 GPS 欺骗攻击展示攻击者可利用这些漏洞间接导致无人机碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提升蜂群控制算法的智能性，但对其安全影响缺乏研究。作者旨在揭示攻击者如何利用蜂群控制算法中的漏洞，通过 GPS 欺骗攻击单个目标无人机，间接导致其他受害无人机偏离航线并发生碰撞。

Method: 提出两种模糊测试工具：SwarmFuzzGraph 结合图论和梯度引导优化搜索 SPVs；SwarmFuzzBinary 使用基于观察的种子调度和二分搜索发现 SPVs。

Result: SwarmFuzzGraph 在一种主流蜂群控制算法上平均成功率达 48.8%，但在不同拓扑结构的蜂群中失效；SwarmFuzzBinary 成功率达相当水平，且在所有测试算法中均有效。

Conclusion: 蜂群控制算法存在可被利用的安全漏洞，SwarmFuzzBinary 作为更通用的模糊测试工具能有效发现此类漏洞，为提升蜂群系统安全性提供基础。

Abstract: Swarm robotics, particularly drone swarms, are used in various safety-critical tasks. While a lot of attention has been given to improving swarm control algorithms for improved intelligence, the security implications of various design choices in swarm control algorithms have not been studied. We highlight how an attacker can exploit the vulnerabilities in swarm control algorithms to disrupt drone swarms. Specifically, we show that the attacker can target a swarm member (target drone) through GPS spoofing attacks, and indirectly cause other swarm members (victim drones) to veer from their course, resulting in collisions. We call these Swarm Propagation Vulnerabilities (SPVs).
  In this paper, we introduce two fuzzing tools, SwarmFuzzGraph and SwarmFuzzBinary, to efficiently find SPVs in swarm control algorithms. SwarmFuzzGraph uses a combination of graph theory and gradient-guided optimization to find SPVs. Our evaluation on a popular swarm control algorithm shows that SwarmFuzzGraph achieves an average success rate of 48.8% in finding SPVs. However, SwarmFuzzGraph fails to find any SPVs in drone swarms with different topologies. We then propose SwarmFuzzBinary, which uses observation-based seed scheduling and binary search to find SPVs. The evaluation shows that SwarmFuzzBinary's success rate is comparable to SwarmFuzzGraph and work in all tested algorithms.

</details>


### [215] [One (Thread) Can Keep a (PRNG) Secret, but not Two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00918)
*Ehood Porat,Amit Klein,Benny Pinkas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次提出一种利用竞态条件漏洞的密码分析方法，成功攻破Apple XNU内核中IPv6 Fragment ID生成算法的PRNG，实现片段ID预测并发动IPv6片段欺骗攻击，进而操纵UDP数据报和TCP段，展示了针对NFS（UDP）和HTTP（TCP）的新型攻击，Apple已分配CVE-2024-27823并完成修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对IPv6 Fragment ID的预测攻击多基于算法本身的密码学弱点，但尚未有利用竞态条件（race-condition）进行密码分析的先例。作者旨在探索XNU内核中PRNG的竞态漏洞，实现片段ID预测，从而执行片段欺骗攻击。

Method: 作者发现XNU的IPv6 Fragment ID生成算法中的PRNG存在竞态条件漏洞，利用该漏洞进行密码分析，学习并恢复PRNG的内部状态，进而预测后续生成的Fragment ID，为片段欺骗攻击创造条件。

Result: 成功预测IPv6 Fragment ID，实现片段欺骗攻击；在NFS（UDP）场景下，离路径攻击者可修改正在写入的文件；在HTTP（TCP）场景下，离路径攻击者可修改HTTP请求。Apple已确认并分配CVE-2024-27823，并在所有基于XNU的产品中修复了该漏洞。

Conclusion: 竞态条件可被用于密码分析攻击，且实际危害严重。该工作揭示了操作系统内核中PRNG实现的安全性隐患，提醒开发者需重视并发场景下的随机数生成安全性。

Abstract: We present a novel, practical attack on the IPv6 Fragment ID generation algorithm of XNU, which is the kernel used by Apple products such as macOS and iOS. This attack exploits a race-condition vulnerability in the algorithm's pseudorandom number generator (PRNG) to cryptanalytically break, learn the internal state of the generator, and consequently predict fragment IDs, which, in turn, facilitates an IPv6 fragment spoofing attack. As far as we know, this is the first cryptanalytic attack that is based on exploiting race-conditions. With fragment spoofing, it is possible to partially manipulate UDP datagrams and TCP segments. We showcase a new type of attack on NFS (UDP) where an off-path attacker modifies a file as it is written, and an attack on HTTP (TCP) where an off-path attacker modifies an HTTP request. Apple assigned this vulnerability the CVE identifier CVE-2024-27823 and patched all its XNU-based products against the attack.

</details>


### [216] [Benchmarking Security Risk Detection and Verification in Open Agentic Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00925)
*Ismail Hossain,Sai Puppala,Zhuoran Lu,Sajedul Talukder,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkillVetBench是一个针对开放智能体技能生态系统的两阶段安全审查基准，第一阶段进行语义审查检测恶意意图，第二阶段在沙箱中执行以收集运行时证据。实验表明纯静态方法最多漏检89%的恶意技能，运行时攻击集中在少数高权限原语上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放智能体平台允许社区贡献可复用技能，但存在供应链安全风险——恶意贡献者可将有害行为隐藏在表面良性的技能中。现有防御缺乏同时评估恶意技能检测和运行时验证能力的基准。

Method: 构建SkillVetBench两阶段安全审查基准：第一阶段对技能的自然语言规范进行语义审查以检测隐藏恶意意图；第二阶段在沙箱中执行被标记的技能以观察运行时行为并收集可审计证据。基准数据来自OpenClaw生态系统中已确认的恶意技能。

Result: 纯语义分析和基于签名的基线方法效果不足，最多漏检89%的恶意技能；运行时攻击集中在exec、write_file、install_skill、spawn等少数高权限原语上；沙箱执行可直接提供具体运行时证据支持恶意判定。

Conclusion: SkillVetBench有效填补了开放智能体技能生态系统中安全审查基准的空白，证明了结合静态语义审查与动态运行时验证的必要性。

Abstract: Open agent platforms allow community contributors to publish reusable skills that agents can invoke at runtime. This extensibility also creates a supply-chain risk: malicious contributors can hide harmful behavior inside skills that appear benign under superficial inspection. However, existing defenses are hard to evaluate because there is no benchmark that measures both malicious-skill detection and runtime verification. We present SkillVetBench, a two-stage security vetting benchmark for open agentic skill ecosystems. The first stage performs semantic vetting over each skill's natural-language specification to detect hidden malicious intent. The second stage executes flagged skills in an instrumented sandbox to observe runtime behavior and collect auditable evidence. We build a benchmark from confirmed malicious skills in the live OpenClaw ecosystem, including samples from the recent ClawHavoc supplychain campaign. Unlike static-only methods, SkillVetBench verifies detected threats with execution traces. Our experiments show that: (1) semantic-only and signature-based baselines are insufficient, missing up to 89\% of malicious skills whose threats arise from natural-language instructions, multicomponent logic, or cross-component interactions; (2) runtime attacks are concentrated in a small set of high-permission primitives, especially exec, write\_file, install\_skill, and spawn; and (3) SkillVetBench provides case studies in which sandbox execution directly supports malicious verdicts with concrete runtime evidence.

</details>


### [217] [SS-ZKR: Spatial-Semantic Zero-Knowledge Routing for Privacy-Preserving Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00962)
*Hassan Touheed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SS-ZKR协议，在A2A/MCP之上实现隐私保护的路由，支持跨组织边界的语义路由而不需中间人解密负载，通过差分隐私、零知识证明和策略编译三种机制满足GDPR/HIPAA/MiFID II合规要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体互操作协议（A2A、MCP）和身份框架（DID、VC）缺乏在不解密负载情况下进行内容级语义路由的能力，而解密负载在GDPR、HIPAA、MiFID II等合规敏感环境中是硬性约束，因此需要一种隐私保护的路由协议。

Method: 提出SS-ZKR协议，包含三种机制：机制I通过差分隐私语义意图向量与零知识证明实现盲路由；机制II实现向量加权自适应负载消毒（数值字段用(ε,δ)-差分隐私，文本字段用启发式语义聚合）；机制III将可视化信任域拓扑编译为确定性零知识访问电路。

Result: 提供了正式威胁模型、意图向量信息泄露界限分析、三种机制的伪代码，以及针对TEE和同态加密路由基线的分析性复杂度比较。

Conclusion: SS-ZKR使得金融服务、医疗和国防领域的企业能够在监管边界内编排异构AI智能体，而无需向路由基础设施暴露专有数据。

Abstract: Foundational agent interoperability standards, notably the Agent-to-Agent (A2A) protocol and the Model Context Protocol (MCP), have advanced multi-agent system communication, and complementary identity frameworks leveraging W3C Decentralised Identifiers (DIDs) and Verifiable Credentials (VCs) provide cryptographic agent authentication. However, no existing protocol supports content-based semantic routing of agent payloads across organisational trust boundaries without requiring the routing intermediary to decrypt the payload, which is a hard constraint in compliance-sensitive environments governed by GDPR, HIPAA, and MiFID II. We propose SS-ZKR, a three-mechanism privacy-preserving routing protocol designed as a complementary layer atop A2A/MCP. Mechanism I introduces blind routing via differentially private semantic intent vectors cryptographically bound to zero-knowledge proofs of payload-schema consistency. Mechanism II offers vector-weighted adaptive payload sanitisation with formal (epsilon, delta)-differential privacy for numerical fields and heuristic semantic aggregation for textual fields. Mechanism III presents a spatial-to-cryptographic policy compiler that translates visually defined trust-zone topologies into deterministic zero-knowledge access circuits. We provide a formal threat model, analyse information leakage bounds of intent vectors, present pseudocode for all three mechanisms, and give analytical complexity comparisons against TEE-based and homomorphic encryption-based routing baselines. SS-ZKR lets enterprises in financial services, healthcare, and defence orchestrate heterogeneous AI agents across regulatory boundaries without exposing proprietary data to routing infrastructure.

</details>


### [218] [AMP: A Vendor-Neutral Wire Format for Agent Memory Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01138)
*Thamilvendhan Munirathinam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 memorywire，一个基于 JSON-Schema 2020-12 的记忆操作 wire 格式标准，包含五种操作（remember, recall, forget, merge, expire）和四种记忆类型（semantic, episodic, procedural, emotional），以及 MemoryStore 接口、fan-out 路由器和可选的人机协作（HITL）治理通道。开源参考实现支持五个后端适配器，在 100 事实/50 查询数据集上 recall@5=1.000，查询 p50=40.6ms，并通过了 16 场景跨适配器一致性测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体记忆框架（如 mem0, Letta/MemGPT, Cognee 等）各自为政，拥有独立的 SDK、存储布局和操作词汇，缺乏统一的线格式标准，导致集成定制化、迁移需从头重建记忆，且没有任何框架提供写入审核的治理界面。

Method: 设计 memorywire 线格式规范：基于 JSON-Schema 2020-12 定义五种记忆操作（remember, recall, forget, merge, expire）和四种记忆类型（语义、情景、程序、情感）；实现 MemoryStore 接口、fan-out 路由器（支持 RRF 和 max fusion 两种融合策略）、可选的 HITL 治理通道；构建开源参考实现，适配五个后端（sqlite-vec, mem0, Letta, Cognee, pgvector）；进行微基准测试、对抗性融合实验和跨适配器一致性测试。

Result: 微基准测试：在 100 事实/50 查询标注语料上，42 个标注查询 recall@5=1.000，写入 p50=37.8ms，查询 p50=40.6ms。对抗性融合实验：RRF 在 1-of-N rank-0 注入扫描中保持 recall@5=1.000（K∈{0,5,...,50}），而 max fusion 在 K≥5 时降至 0.500，存在 80% 泄露。16 场景跨适配器一致性测试：80 个单元中 68 个通过，零失败。

Conclusion: memorywire 的贡献不在于提出新算法，而是将已有组件（RRF、FSM、STM/LTM 巩固、diff-and-approve 工作流）封装为一个与平台无关的协议，提供经验验证的参考实现。该协议定位为与 Model Context Protocol 互补而非竞争。

Abstract: Agent-memory frameworks - mem0, Letta/MemGPT, Cognee, Zep/Graphiti, MemoryOS, MemTensor - each ship their own SDK, storage layout, and operational vocabulary. There is no shared wire format: every integration is bespoke, every migration rebuilds memory from scratch, and no framework ships a governance surface that lets a human review writes before they enter long-term storage. We present memorywire, a JSON-Schema 2020-12 wire format for five memory operations (remember, recall, forget, merge, expire) over four memory types (semantic, episodic, procedural, emotional), with a MemoryStore interface, a fan-out router, and an optional HITL governance channel. We describe an open-source reference implementation with five backend adapters (sqlite-vec, mem0, Letta, Cognee, pgvector); a microbenchmark on a 100-fact / 50-query labelled corpus achieving recall@5 = 1.000 on the 42 labelled queries with ingest p50 = 37.8 ms and recall p50 = 40.6 ms; an adversarial-fusion experiment showing Reciprocal Rank Fusion holds recall@5 = 1.000 across a 1-of-N rank-0 injection sweep (K in {0,5,...,50}) where max fusion collapses to 0.500 with 80% leak at K >= 5; and a 16-scenario cross-adapter conformance suite passing 68 of 80 cells with zero failures. The contribution is not a new algorithm; it is a packaging of established components (RRF, FSMs, STM/LTM consolidation, diff-and-approve workflows) into a venue-neutral protocol with an empirically validated reference, positioned to compose with the Model Context Protocol rather than compete with it.

</details>


### [219] [BraveGuard: From Open-World Threats to Safer Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01166)
*Yunhao Feng,Yifan Ding,Xiaohu Du,Ming Wen,Xinhao Deng,Yanming Guo,Yuxiang Xie,Baihui Zheng,Yingshui Tan,Yige Li,Yutao Wu,Yixu Wang,Kerui Cao,Wenke Huang,Xingjun Ma,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BraveGuard是一个自演进的防御框架，通过从开放世界威胁信号和真实智能体轨迹中训练守卫模型，显著提升计算机使用智能体的多步安全检测能力（在AgentHazard上准确率从38.79%提升至82.38%），形成自适应防御循环而非静态基准训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（Computer-use agents）从文本生成扩展到与文件、终端、浏览器和外部工具的持续交互，带来了安全风险。这些风险难以通过孤立提示或最终响应检测，因为危害往往在多步执行轨迹中累积，而单个动作看似无害。现有方法缺乏针对轨迹级安全监控的有效训练数据。
