Method: BraveGuard框架包含四个步骤：(1) 挖掘最新研究资源以识别新兴风险和攻击模式；(2) 将威胁实例化为可执行的计算机使用任务；(3) 收集智能体执行轨迹（rollouts）；(4) 从轨迹中推导出监督信号用于训练守卫模型。该流程可重复执行，形成自适应防御循环。训练了多种守卫模型骨干，包括Qwen3-Guard和Llama-Guard变体。

Result: 在AgentHazard基准上，BraveGuard将安全检测准确率从38.79%（现成守卫模型）提升至82.38%（平均守卫模型设置）。多个守卫骨干模型在轨迹级智能体安全基准上均实现了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 基于开放世界威胁发现和真实智能体执行的守卫监督可以超越固定分类法和合成提示级数据，提升安全监控能力。BraveGuard为应对不断演变的现实世界风险提供了可扩展的自适应防御路径。

Abstract: Computer-use agents extend language models from text generation to sustained interaction with files, terminals, browsers, and external tools. This shift creates safety risks that are difficult to detect from isolated prompts or final responses, because harm often emerges only through multi-step execution traces whose individual actions appear locally benign. We introduce BraveGuard, a self-evolving defense framework for training guard models from open-world threat signals and realistic agent trajectories. BraveGuard mines recent research sources to identify emerging risks and attack patterns, instantiates them as executable computer-use tasks, collects agent rollouts, and derives trajectory-level supervision for guard model training. As new threats and validation failures appear, the pipeline can be repeated, yielding an adaptive defense loop rather than a static, benchmark-driven training process. We instantiate BraveGuard by training multiple guard backbones, including Qwen3-Guard and Llama-Guard variants, and evaluate the resulting guards on trajectory-level agent-safety benchmarks. BraveGuard consistently improves safety detection across computer-use trajectories. On AgentHazard, it substantially improves detection accuracy over off-the-shelf guard models, with accuracy increasing from 38.79% to 82.38% under the averaged guard-model setting. These results show that guard supervision grounded in open-world threat discovery and realistic agent execution can improve safety monitoring beyond fixed taxonomies and synthetic prompt-level data. BraveGuard offers a scalable path toward adaptive defenses for computer-use agents facing evolving real-world risks.

</details>


### [220] [Schema-Agnostic Knowledge Graph Construction via Hybrid Ontology Discovery for Cyber Threat Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01208)
*Seonwoo Kim,Jinwoo Kim,Daegyu Kang,Daeseong Kim,Insup Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ANCHOR是一个与模式无关的CTI知识图谱构建系统，通过混合本体发现机制和SHACL验证，结合本地LLM，在UCO、STIX等大型本体上实现了优于现有方法的模式合规性，并支持隐私保护的威胁情报分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于本体的CTI提取方法面临三个挑战：模式特定管道需要手动重新配置、基于提示的模式包含在大规模本体上无法扩展、依赖企业LLM API与隐私约束冲突。需要一种能够动态适应不同本体模式且支持隐私保护的CTI知识图谱构建方法。

Method: 提出ANCHOR系统，核心是混合本体发现机制——一种搜索与导航机制，能够动态探索大规模本体模式；结合SHACL验证来强制执行符合模式的类型分配；支持使用本地LLM以实现隐私保护。

Result: 在UCO、STIX和MALOnt模式上的实验结果表明，ANCHOR在本体类型识别和模式合规性方面优于现有基线方法；使用本地LLM的ANCHOR在类型识别性能上接近企业LLM，实现了高保真度的隐私保护CTI分析。

Conclusion: ANCHOR成功解决了现有CTI提取方法的三大挑战，通过模式无关的设计和混合本体发现机制，实现了在大规模本体上的可扩展性，并通过支持本地LLM兼顾了隐私保护需求，为CTI知识图谱构建提供了一种高效且隐私友好的解决方案。

Abstract: Cyber threat intelligence (CTI) reports now serve as essential resources for capturing adversary tactics, techniques, and procedures observed in modern attack campaigns. While traditional CTI platforms reduce this intelligence to isolated indicators through fixed schemas such as STIX, ontology-based representations preserve the semantic relationships needed for structured threat analysis. However, existing approaches for ontology-aligned CTI extraction face three challenges: (i) schema-specific pipelines that require manual reconfiguration whenever the schema changes, (ii) prompt-based schema inclusion that fails to scale on large ontologies such as UCO, and (iii) reliance on enterprise LLM APIs that conflicts with privacy constraints when integrating sensitive internal incident data. In this paper, we present ANCHOR, a schema-agnostic CTI knowledge graph construction system that bridges LLMs and formal ontology schemas. At the core of ANCHOR is hybrid ontology discovery, a search-and-navigate mechanism that dynamically explores large-scale ontology schemas, combined with SHACL-based validation to enforce schema-compliant type assignments. Experimental results on the UCO, STIX, and MALOnt schemas show that ANCHOR outperforms existing baselines in ontology typing and schema compliance. In addition, ANCHOR with a local LLM closely matches enterprise LLM typing performance, enabling privacy-preserving CTI analysis with high fidelity.

</details>


### [221] [GPU Acceleration of Learning With Errors KEMs Using OpenACC for Post-Quantum Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01211)
*Tiziana Liberati,Nitin Shukla,Matteo Barbieri,Gabriella Bettonte,Elisabetta Boella,Simone Rizzo,Daniele Gregori,Marco Pedicini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于普通LWE问题的密钥封装机制（KEM），并使用OpenACC实现了面向GPU的加速方案。在NVIDIA Grace Hopper Superchip上实现了相比多线程CPU基线高达208倍的加速，能耗效率约为x86 CPU+H100 GPU系统的2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shor算法证明在大规模量子计算机时代，基于整数分解和离散对数问题的非对称密码协议不再安全，因此需要开发抗量子攻击的后量子密码（PQC）方案。LWE问题是PQC的核心困难问题之一，但其计算强度远高于经典密码方案，需要高效的硬件加速。

Method: 提出了基于普通LWE问题的密钥封装机制（KEM），并利用OpenACC开发了面向GPU的并行实现，支持裸机和容器化执行，在多种NVIDIA GPU型号和代际上进行评估。

Result: 在所有测试的GPU平台上均实现了显著加速。在NVIDIA Grace Hopper Superchip上，相比多线程CPU基线获得最高208倍加速，并能处理因内存和同步限制在CPU架构上不可行的问题规模。能耗方面，Superchip相比x86 CPU+H100 GPU系统效率提升约2倍。

Conclusion: GPU加速对于计算密集型的LWE密码工作负载非常有效，Grace Hopper Superchip在性能和能效上均表现优异。

Abstract: Shor's algorithm proved that asymmetric cryptographic protocols based on the integer factorization and discrete logarithm problems are no longer safe in a world with large-scale quantum computers. As a result, Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) has been developed over the last few years, seeking cryptographic primitives resistant to quantum attacks. One of the main hard problems underlying PQC schemes is the Learning with Errors (LWE) problem, which is significantly more computationally intensive than its classical predecessors. In this work, we present a Key Encapsulation Mechanism (KEM) based on plain LWE and develop a GPU-oriented implementation using OpenACC. We evaluate the performance of our accelerated application in terms of both time-to-solution and energy-to-solution, considering bare-metal and containerized executions across multiple NVIDIA GPU models and generations. Our implementation achieves significant acceleration across all tested GPU platforms. In particular, on the NVIDIA Grace Hopper Superchip, it attains up to a $208\times$ speedup over a multithreaded CPU baseline and enables the execution of problem sizes that are impractical on CPU architectures due to memory and synchronization constraints. Energy consumption analysis also shows $\approx 2\times$ better efficiency when using the Superchip compared to systems equipped with x86-based CPUs and NVIDIA H100 GPUs. These results highlight the effectiveness of GPU acceleration for computationally demanding LWE-based cryptographic workloads.

</details>


### [222] [Privacy-Preserving Smart Surveillance with Cross-Dataset Violence Detection and Decentralized Evidence Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01225)
*Hasan Coşkun,Furkan Çolhak,Andrea Kulakov,Vesna Dimitrova*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种隐私保护的智能监控框架，将暴力行为检测与证据披露分离，使用MobileNetV2+BiLSTM模型实现93.5%测试准确率，并通过Shamir秘密共享、门限审批、双因子认证等机制保护加密证据的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI监控系统虽能加速公共安全响应，但记录证据仍处于集中化管理控制下，缺乏隐私保护机制，无法在证据披露与个人隐私之间取得平衡。

Method: 1) 轻量级MobileNetV2视频分类器用于检测暴力片段；2) 检测到的片段立即加密，仅通过门限审批机制可访问；3) 使用Shamir秘密共享拆分解密密钥，成员份额通过公钥密码保护；4) 投票机制支持限时令牌、双因子认证、数字签名和审计日志；5) 评估MobileNetV2+LSTM、MobileNetV2+BiLSTM、MobileNetV2+时序CNN三种架构在SCVD、RWF-2000、Real-Life Violence Situations三个数据集上的七种域内和跨数据集场景。

Result: 最佳模型MobileNetV2+BiLSTM在合并的留出集上达到93.5%测试准确率和0.980的ROC-AUC值，但在RWF-2000数据切片上表现较低，证实了持续的数据集偏移问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在实现公共安全监控的同时保护了隐私，但数据集偏移问题仍需进一步解决以提升跨场景泛化能力。

Abstract: AI-enabled surveillance can accelerate public-safety response, yet most systems still leave recorded evidence under centralized administrative control. This paper proposes a privacy-preserving smart surveillance framework that separates incident detection from evidence disclosure. A lightweight MobileNetV2-based video classifier detects violent clips, while each recorded incident segment is immediately encrypted and made accessible only through threshold-based approval. The decryption key is split with Shamir's Secret Sharing, member shares are protected with public-key cryptography, and voting is supported by time-limited tokens, two-factor authentication, signatures, and audit logs. This study evaluates MobileNetV2+LSTM, MobileNetV2+BiLSTM, and MobileNetV2+temporal CNN heads on SCVD, RWF-2000, and Real-Life Violence Situations under seven in-domain and cross-dataset scenarios. The best all-source model, MobileNetV2+BiLSTM, reaches 93.5% test accuracy and ROC-AUC 0.980% on the merged held-out set, while lower RWF-2000 slice performance confirms persistent dataset shift.

</details>


### [223] [Needles at Scale: LLM-Assisted Target Selection for Windows Vulnerability Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01364)
*Michael J. Bommarito*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Symbolicate-Enrich-Sample流水线，通过自动恢复符号、提取结构化特征并用低成本语言模型标注优先级，将整个Windows操作系统的7,231,419个函数缩减至约22K个候选函数，解决了大规模二进制分析中目标选择这一核心瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代操作系统攻击面如"干草堆"，包含数千个签名二进制文件和数百万个函数，其中绝大部分与任何特定漏洞无关。人工分析师或LLM代理在分析之前必须先选择值得阅读的函数。在操作系统全局范围内，目标选择（而非分析本身）成为关键约束。

Method: 三步流水线：(i) Symbolicate（符号化）：自动获取公开符号文件，与恢复的调用图连接，为剥离符号的供应商二进制文件恢复函数级符号；(ii) Enrich（丰富）：为每个命名函数附加确定性结构特征，基于这些特征使用低成本语言模型分配可达性层级、风险级别、漏洞类型假设及理由；(iii) Sample（采样）：通过优先级加权重要性采样器抽取多样化、优先排序的批次。

Result: 在包含7,231,419个函数的完整Windows镜像上，标签具有显著的选择性。叠加确定性过滤器后，得到约22K个函数的候选短列表——足够人类或代理逐一审查的"候选针"。报告了流水线的选择性与失败模式，以及聚合统计结果。

Conclusion: 该流水线提供了一种选择基板（selection substrate），下游检测器或LLM代理可在此基础上运行优先级排序层。因法律和双重用途原因，导出的数据集不予公开。核心贡献在于将大规模二进制分析中的目标选择从不可行的全量分析转化为可操作的优先队列。

Abstract: The attack surface of a modern operating system is a haystack: thousands of signed binaries and millions of functions, almost none relevant to any given vulnerability. A human analyst or an LLM agent must pick the function worth reading before analyzing it. At whole-OS scope, this target selection, not the analysis, is the binding constraint. We present Symbolicate-Enrich-Sample, a low-cost batch pipeline that turns a corpus of production Windows binaries into a queryable, priority-ranked research queue. We (i) recover function-level symbols for stripped vendor binaries by auto-fetching the public symbol files and joining them to a recovered call graph; (ii) attach cheap, deterministic structural features to each named function and, conditioned on those features, use a low-cost language model to assign a reachability tier, a risk level, a bug-class hypothesis, and a rationale; and (iii) draw diverse, prioritized batches via a priority-weighted importance sampler. The contribution is a selection substrate: the prioritization layer a downstream detector or LLM agent runs on top of. Across a whole Windows image of 7,231,419 functions, the labels are markedly selective, and stacking deterministic filters on them leaves a ~22K-function shortlist: the candidate needles, few enough for a human or agent to work through. We characterize the pipeline's selectivity and its failure modes, describe the methodology, and report aggregate statistics; we withhold the derived dataset for legal and dual-use reasons.

</details>


### [224] [Formal Verification of Secure Encrypted Virtualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01381)
*Hansika Weerasena,Amitabh Das,Prabhat Mishra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个形式化框架，用于对AMD SEV机密虚拟机进行建模和验证，通过设计级和属性级抽象，检查机密性、完整性和可用性等安全属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMD SEV作为云环境中机密计算的关键技术，能够提供硬件级内存加密，但往往缺乏对其安全保证的形式化验证，存在安全隐患。

Method: 对AMD SEV规范进行设计级和属性级抽象，构建形式化模型，并对模型进行属性检查，以验证机密性、完整性和可用性。

Result: 成功构建了AMD SEV机密虚拟机形式化验证框架，能够对关键安全属性进行属性检查。

Conclusion: 该形式化方法为定义和验证执行环境的安全属性提供了严谨的基础，有助于增强AMD SEV的安全保障。

Abstract: Trusted execution environments (TEEs) provide a secure environment for data and code in use, ensuring that they are protected with respect to confidentiality and integrity. Virtual machine (VM)-based TEEs utilize virtualization technology to create isolated execution spaces that can support a complete operating system or specific applications. AMD secure encrypted virtualization (SEV) is a key technology used in confidential computing in the cloud enabling hardware-based memory encryption to protect sensitive data within VMs. However, AMD SEV often operate without formal assurances of their security guarantees. Our research introduces a formal framework for representing and verifying AMD SEV confidential VMs. Specifically, we conduct design-level and property-level abstraction on AMD SEV specification and conduct property checking on the model to ensure confidentiality, integrity and availability. This approach provides a rigorous foundation for defining and verifying key security attributes for safeguarding execution environments.

</details>


### [225] [Differentially Private Datastore Generation for Retrieval-Augmented Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01413)
*Abdelrahman Abouelenein,Marwan Torki*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于局部敏感哈希（LSH）的概率生成框架，用于创建和发布差分隐私数据存储。该方法通过LSH将高维数据分桶，对每个桶的累积投票添加校准后的差分隐私噪声，生成跨类别的概率分布。在7个数据集上的实验表明，在ε=5时，该DP数据存储仅平均降低2.6%的准确率，并将成员推理攻击的准确率降至53.60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代端侧AI系统依赖检索增强推理，需要在不泄露个人隐私的前提下共享数据存储。差分隐私（DP）能够提供正式保证，确保个体贡献无法被区分，因此需要一种适用于高维数据的DP数据存储创建与发布方法。

Method: 采用局部敏感哈希（LSH）将高维数据高效划分为桶，然后为每个桶的累积投票添加校准后的DP噪声，从而生成跨类别的概率分布。该方法适用于任何需要安全键值数据存储创建与发布的流水线。

Result: 在7个不同样本量和类别数（2到14类）的数据集上，ε=5时DP数据存储仅平均降低2.6%的准确率；成员推理攻击的准确率降至53.60%。

Conclusion: 提出的基于LSH的DP概率生成框架能够在保持强隐私保护的同时，最小化对模型准确率的影响，有效抵御成员推理攻击。

Abstract: It is crucial for modern on-device AI systems that rely on retrieval-augmented inference to release and share datastores without compromising individual privacy. This can be achieved using Differential Privacy (DP), which provides a formal guarantee that ensures individual contributions remain indistinguishable, even under adversarial analysis. In this paper, we introduce a hashing-based probability generation framework designed to enable the creation and release of differentially private datastores. Our approach employs locality-sensitive hashing (LSH) to efficiently partition high-dimensional data into buckets. We then add calibrated DP noise to the accumulated vote for each bucket, generating a probability distribution across classes. Our method is broadly applicable to any pipeline requiring secure key,value datastore creation and release. We conducted experiments on seven datasets with varying sample sizes and class counts, ranging from 2 to 14. At epsilon=5, our released DP datastore achieves strong privacy protection with only an average 2.6% drop in accuracy. Finally, we benchmark DP datastore resilience to membership inference attacks, reducing attack accuracy to 53.60%.

</details>


### [226] [On the Evaluation of Spiking Neural Network Configurations for Network Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01442)
*Raj Patel,David Amebley,Taye Akinrele,Shaswata Mitra,Sayanton Dibbo,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过消融实验系统评估了9种脉冲神经元模型与3种脉冲编码方案（共27种变体）在四个入侵检测基准数据集上的性能，发现脉冲编码方案（特别是延迟编码）比神经元模型对检测质量影响更大，最佳组合（LeakyParallel神经元+延迟编码）平均准确率92.11%，宏F1 0.80，假阳性率2.01%，展示了SNN作为低延迟、资源受限场景下入侵检测替代方案的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习入侵检测模型计算开销大，而SNN虽天然适合边缘和神经形态部署，但其神经元模型和脉冲编码方案的设计空间在入侵检测任务中缺乏系统研究。

Method: 采用控制消融实验，对9种神经元模型与3种脉冲编码方案交叉组合（共27种变体），基于snntorch框架实现，在NSL KDD、KDDCup99、CIC-IDS2017和CTU-13四个基准数据集上以5个随机种子进行原始输入（预处理极少）的评估。

Result: 脉冲编码方案比神经元模型对检测质量影响更大；速率编码和增量编码表现劣于延迟编码；最佳组合LeakyParallel神经元+延迟编码在四个数据集上平均准确率92.11%、宏F1 0.80、假阳性率2.01%，在CIC-IDS2017和CTU-13上准确率接近完美，且推理速度最快。

Conclusion: SNN在低延迟或资源受限的部署场景下，可作为传统入侵检测方法的有竞争力替代方案。

Abstract: Network intrusion detection is a core component of modern cybersecurity infrastructure, yet the deep learning models that dominate the field are computationally demanding, motivating interest in lightweight alternatives suited to edge and neuromorphic deployment. Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are therefore a natural candidate, but their design space, spanning the choice of neuron model and spike encoding scheme, remains poorly characterized for intrusion detection. We bridge this gap by using a controlled ablation study using 9 neurons coupled with 3 spike encoding schemes, making 27 variants, all implemented on snntorch evaluated over raw inputs with limited preprocessing on four benchmark datasets (NSL KDD, KDDCup99, CIC-IDS2017, and CTU-13) with 5 seeds. We find that spike encoding scheme is a better determinant for detection quality than the neuron model, where rate and delta spike encodings perform worse than latency encoding over the sweep. The LeakyParallel neuron with latency encoding performed the best overall, averaging at 92.11% accuracy and 0.80 macro- F1 at a rate of 2.01% false positives averaged over all 4 datasets, with accuracy close to perfect for CIC-IDS2017 and CTU-13, and also performed the fastest on inference. These results highlight the potential of SNNs as a viable alternative to traditional methods of intrusion detection when considering low-latency or resource-constrained deployments.

</details>


### [227] [NetVAD: Foundation-Model Representation Learning for Identifier-Free Unsupervised Intrusion Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01452)
*Darren Fürst,Patrick Levi,Sebastian Steindl*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出NetVAD，一种基于冻结网络基础模型的无监督变分自编码器，用于零日攻击检测。在ToN-IoT和IoT-23数据集上取得竞争力结果（ToN-IoT上Micro F1 98%，Macro F1 96%），尤其擅长检测复杂僵尸网络行为（Okiru上F1 99.6%），但发现基于流的基础模型在检测单包侦查事件时存在局限。消融实验表明大规模预训练和专门解码器架构对性能至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无监督入侵检测模型性能无法与监督模型匹配，而监督模型只能针对已知攻击训练。作者希望利用网络基础模型的新兴能力，弥合无监督与监督方法之间的性能差距，实现有效的零日攻击检测。

Method: 提出NetVAD，采用严格无标识符的变分自编码器架构，将冻结的网络基础模型表征投影到任务特定潜空间，仅使用良性流量训练。模型基于重构损失检测异常。

Result: 在ToN-IoT上Micro F1达98%、Macro F1达96%；对复杂僵尸网络Okiru检测F1达99.6%；但流级基础模型对单包侦查事件检测存在局限。消融实验证实大规模预训练和专门解码器架构的必要性。

Conclusion: NetVAD有效弥合了无监督与监督IDS的性能差距，通过冻结基础模型加专用解码器的设计，能够可靠检测多种攻击。大规模预训练防止性能退化，专门解码器精确建模良性数据流形，从而通过更高重构损失更可靠地捕获攻击。

Abstract: Detecting zero-day exploits in production networks requires robust Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS). However, current unsupervised models struggle to match the performance of supervised classifiers, which are trained for specific attacks only. To bridge this gap, we leverage the emerging capabilities of Network Foundation Models. We propose \textit{NetVAD}, a strictly identifier-free Variational Autoencoder that projects representations from a frozen Foundation Model into a task-specific latent space, trained solely on benign traffic. Evaluated on ToN-IoT and IoT-23, NetVAD achieves highly competitive unsupervised performance. On ToN-IoT, it achieves a 98% Micro F1-score and a 96% Macro F1-score at an operational false positive rate. Unlike prior work, we show the model's performance transparently for all attack-classes of the datasets. While the architecture excels at discerning complex botnet behaviour (99.6% F1 on Okiru), our evaluation reveals limitations of flow-based Foundation Models in detecting single-packet reconnaissance events. Finally, a comprehensive ablation study confirms that while large-scale pre-training is essential to prevent performance degrading, specialised decoder architectures are necessary to precisely model the complex benign manifold, ensuring attacks are caught more reliably, due to a higher reconstruction loss.

</details>


### [228] [ClawHub Security Signals: When VirusTotal, Static Analysis, and SkillSpector Disagree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01494)
*Vincent Koc,Patrick Erichsen,Jacob Tomlinson,Agustin Rivera,Michael Appel,Nir Paz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发布了ClawHub Security Signals数据集（67,453个OpenClaw技能版本），通过三种扫描器（VirusTotal、静态启发式分析、NVIDIA SkillSpector）对AI agent技能进行安全评估。研究发现扫描器之间高度分歧：仅0.69%的技能被三者同时标记，81.9%的标记技能仅被单一扫描器识别。恶意裁决区以VirusTotal为主（72.8%），而语义风险区以SkillSpector为主（75.3%）。结论认为agent技能安全需要分层治理而非单扫描器决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI agent技能（Skills）引入了独立于模型安全和传统恶意软件检测的安全边界，但缺少系统化的安全评估方法和数据集。现有安全工具（如VirusTotal、静态分析、SkillSpector）之间存在不一致性，需要研究这种分歧的结构及其对安全治理的影响。

Method: 构建ClawHub Security Signals数据集，包含67,453个最新公开OpenClaw技能版本，每行包含经过脱敏的SKILL.md内容和清理后的捆绑文件，以及ClawScan注册表裁决和三类扫描器（VirusTotal、静态启发式分析、NVIDIA SkillSpector）的证据。不估计恶意技能流行率，而是聚焦扫描器之间的分歧分析。

Result: 三类扫描器高度分歧：任意两扫描器重叠最多10.4%的共同阳性，仅0.69%被三者同时标记，81.9%被单一扫描器标记。SkillSpector在可疑行中占比75.3%，在恶意行中仅6.8%；VirusTotal在恶意行中占比72.8%，与捆绑代码恶意证据一致。

Conclusion: Agent技能安全需要分层治理而非单扫描器的允许/阻止二元决策。数据集以经过清洗的银标标准发布（自动裁决而非人工标注），为社区提供早期版本快照，鼓励进一步研究包括针对技能安全分诊的专用模型。

Abstract: Agent skills extend AI agents with reusable instructions, tools, scripts, references, and workflows, establishing a security boundary distinct from both model safety and traditional package-malware detection. ClawHub Security Signals is a sanitized dataset of 67,453 latest public OpenClaw skill versions. Each row pairs redacted SKILL.md content and sanitized bundled files where present with a final ClawScan registry verdict and evidence from three scanner families: VirusTotal, static heuristic analysis, and NVIDIA SkillSpector.
  Rather than estimating malicious-skill prevalence, we study scanner disagreement. The three scanners rarely flag the same skills: any pair overlaps on at most 10.4% of their combined positives, only 0.69% of skills are flagged by all three, and 81.9% of flagged skills are identified by a single scanner. The disagreement is structured by attack surface. SkillSpector, which raises semantic agentic-risk advisories rather than malware-reputation signals, is positive for 19,209 of 25,504 suspicious rows (75.3%) but only 14 of 206 malicious rows (6.8%). The malicious-verdict region shows the inverse profile: 150 of 206 malicious rows (72.8%) are VirusTotal-positive, consistent with bundled-code malware evidence.
  These results show that agent-skill security requires layered governance, not single-scanner allow/block decisions. The corpus is released as a sanitized silver-standard dataset: labels are the registry's automated verdicts, not human-annotated ground truth, and the release represents an early, versioned snapshot intended to support the community while a human-annotated subset is developed. Further research is encouraged, including models tailored for skill-security triage.

</details>


### [229] [Agent Operating Systems (AOS): Integrating Agentic Control Planes into, and Beyond, Traditional Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01508)
*Ankur Sharma,Deep Shah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出**代理操作系统（AOS）**概念，旨在将智能体控制平面集成到现有操作系统（如Linux/Windows）中，解决传统OS抽象（进程、线程、系统调用等）无法支持长生命周期、概率推理、动态工具调用、自适应行为的智能体AI系统的问题。AOS架构涵盖调度器、上下文与内存管理、工具与能力注册表、策略与信任执行、可观测性与审计等模块，并分析了安全威胁模型与评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统操作系统基于确定性程序、显式控制流和人类发起的工作流设计，其核心抽象（进程、线程、系统调用、文件、权限）假设有界行为和可预测交互模式。而智能体AI系统是长生命周期、目标导向、概率推理、动态调用工具、基于反馈自适应行为的实体，其执行特征在调度、内存与状态管理、安全性、可观测性和治理方面突破了传统OS的边界。因此需要一种新的系统架构来为智能体计算提供可控、可问责且安全的运行基础。

Method: 文章系统性地定义了AOS（Agent Operating System）概念，明确了其假设与非目标，将AOS职责结构化解构为：调度器、上下文与内存管理、工具与能力注册表、策略与信任执行、可观测性与审计。提出了从用户态运行时到分布式控制平面的多种集成模型，并将AOS概念映射到Linux和Windows原语上。同时分析了智能体特定的威胁模型，定义了强调确定性执行、可审计性和操作者可理解性的评估标准。

Result: 提出了一套完整的AOS架构框架，展示了传统OS抽象在处理智能体工作负载时的局限性，给出了具体的集成路径（用户态运行时→分布式控制平面），并将AOS概念映射到现有OS原语（Linux/Windows）上。同时建立了智能体安全威胁模型和评估准则。

Conclusion: 目标并非完全取代现有操作系统，而是为智能体计算建立严谨的系统基础，使其在大规模部署时仍保持可控性、可问责性和安全性。AOS通过在现有OS中引入智能体控制平面，弥补传统OS抽象与智能体执行模型之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Traditional operating systems were designed around deterministic programs, explicit control flow, and human initiated workflows. Their core abstractions processes, threads, system calls, files, and permissions assume bounded behavior and predictable interaction patterns. Agentic AI systems introduce a different execution model: long-lived, goal-directed entities that reason probabilistically, invoke tools dynamically, and adapt behavior based on feedback. While agents can be implemented as user-space applications today, their execution characteristics stress OS boundaries in scheduling, memory and state management, security, observability, and governance. This paper introduces the concept of an Agent Operating System (AOS), a systems architecture that integrates an agentic control plane into existing operating systems or, in some models, subsumes selected OS responsibilities over time. We provide a precise definition of an AOS, explicit assumptions and non-goals, and a structured decomposition of AOS responsibilities into schedulers, context and memory management, tool and capability registries, policy and trust enforcement, and observability and audit. We analyze limitations of classical OS abstractions for agent workloads, propose integration models from user-space runtimes to distributed control planes, and map AOS concepts onto Linux and Windows primitives. We present security and safety implications, including agent specific threat models, and define evaluation criteria that emphasize deterministic enforcement, auditability, and operator comprehensibility. The objective is not to replace operating systems wholesale, but to establish a rigorous systems foundation for agentic computation that remains controllable, accountable, and secure at scale.

</details>


### [230] [Defenses & Enablers For Skill Injection Attacks on Terminal Based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01567)
*Yoshinari Fujinuma,Varun Gangal,Traian Rebedea,Makesh Narasimhan Sreedhar,Prasoon Varshney,Rebecca Qian,Anand Kannappan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代理在依赖可复用技能文件时面临的安全威胁，提出了两种守护者防御机制（动态守护者和静态守护者），并验证了它们在降低攻击成功率（ASR超过一半）的同时保持任务效用的有效性。同时，通过四种攻击重构方法进行压力测试，发现动态守护者能将ASR从81.4%降至18.6%，证明其实时调解能力是一种稳健的防御手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理越来越依赖可复用的技能文件（描述任务特定流程的文档），但这引入了新的攻击面，亟需研究有效的防御策略来应对这一安全威胁。

Method: 提出两种守护者防御机制：动态守护者（作为中间LLM代理实时调解技能文件访问）和静态守护者（在构建时预重写文件）。使用四个LLM代理家族进行评估，并通过四种攻击重构方法（保留恶意指令但改变措辞）进行压力测试。

Result: 守护者机制将攻击成功率降低超过一半，同时保持任务效用。在非守护者设置下，攻击重构使ASR高达81.4%，而动态守护者将其降至18.6%。

Conclusion: 基于守护者的防御能有效缓解LLM代理在技能文件复用场景下的安全威胁，其中动态守护者的实时调解是更为稳健的防御策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on reusable skills i.e. documents describing task-specific procedures. However, this introduces a new attack surface for agents to manage. We study two complementary directions for this threat. First, we evaluate guardian-based defenses: an intermediary LLM agent that acts as a mediator for skill file access (dynamic guardian) or pre-rewrites these files at build time (static guardian). Across three LLM agent families, our guardians cut attack success rate (ASR) by well over half while preserving task utility. Second, we stress test them through attack reframing using four attacks that preserve the malicious instruction but change the phrasing. For non-guardian setup, the reframing pushes the ASR up to 81.4\%, but the dynamic guardian brings it down to 18.6\%, showing that real-time mediation is a robust defense.

</details>


### [231] [CoreUnlearn: Rethinking Concept Unlearning through Disentangled Component-Level Erasure in Text-guided Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01658)
*Mengnan Zhao,Lihe Zhang,Baocai Yin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CoreUnlearn通过组件分解和交换解缠策略，从扩散模型中移除关键概念组件，实现高效概念擦除同时保持模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文本空间对齐的擦除方法受限于文本表示能力且对预定义擦除参考高度敏感，次优参考会显著影响模型效用保持。

Method: 提出CoreUnlearn框架，包含组件提取模块(CEM)和交换解缠策略(SDS)。SDS引导CEM预训练将概念嵌入分解为不同组件类型，然后移除擦除关键组件而保留非关键组件，通过微调模型权重实现。

Result: 大量实验表明CoreUnlearn在实现有效概念擦除的同时，对整体模型性能的影响最小。

Conclusion: CoreUnlearn通过解缠并移除不良概念的擦除关键组件，克服了文本空间表示能力限制和对预定义擦除参考敏感的问题。

Abstract: Text guided diffusion models have revolutionized image synthesis but also raise ethical concerns, such as privacy violation and harmful content generation. To mitigate these issues, prevailing methods typically leverage an alignment mechanism, with predefined erasure references, to fine-tune pretrained model weights. However, these techniques are intrinsically limited by the representational capacity of textual space and display high sensitivity to the choice of predefined erasure references, e.g., suboptimal references may significantly affect the model utility preservation during erasure. To overcome these limitations, we introduce CoreUnlearn, aiming to disentangle and remove the erasure-critical component of the undesirable concept. Specifically, CoreUnlearn comprises a Component Extraction Module (CEM) and a Swap Disentangling Strategy (SDS). Guided by SDS, CEM is pre-trained to decompose concept embeddings into distinct component types. Leveraging this decomposition, CoreUnlearn then removes the erasure-critical component while retaining non-critical ones by fine-tuning model weights. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoreUnlearn achieves effective concept erasure with minimal impact on overall model performance.

</details>


### [232] [IstGPT: LLM-based Anomaly Detection for Spatial-Temporal Graph in Industrial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01691)
*Yuchen Zhang,Ning Xi,Pengbin Feng,Shigang Liu,Jianfeng Ma,Yulong Shen,Yanan Sun,Xiaolin Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: IstGPT是首个基于LLM和图学习的工业异常检测工具，通过多模态知识提取传感器-执行器依赖图，结合优化后的图神经网络与编码器-解码器架构，在9个数据集上超越12个SOTA基线，实现实时ICS攻击检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具因传感器与执行器间复杂依赖关系，在工业互联网系统实时异常检测中效果有限，难以应对日益复杂的ICS攻击威胁。

Method: 1) 利用工业多模态知识（运行数据、技术文档、系统图），通过多阶段提示工程提取传感器-执行器依赖图；2) LLM-Optimation基于节点准确率、边一致性和逻辑连贯性迭代优化图结构；3) 集成改进图神经网络与编码器-解码器架构，通过重构误差检测异常。

Result: 在9个数据集（2个公开、6个仿真、1个真实机械臂数据集）上，IstGPT在F1分数和时间感知指标eTaF1上均取得最优结果，超越12个SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: IstGPT在工业场景中具备实时异常检测的有效性和部署可行性，为ICS攻击防护提供了新范式。

Abstract: Industrial Internet systems face increasing threats from sophisticated industrial control system (ICS) attacks, resulting in critical safety incidents. However, existing tools exhibit limited effectiveness in real-time anomaly detection due to the complex dependencies among sensors and actuators. To tackle this, we present IstGPT, the first industrial anomaly detection tool based on LLMs and graph learning to provide real-time protection against a wide range of ICS attacks. IstGPT achieves fine-grained and precise modeling on spatial-temporal dependencies in industrial cyber-physical systems. It first leverages industrial multi-modal knowledge, including operational data, technical documents, and system diagrams, to extract sensor-actuator dependency graphs via multi-stage prompt engineering. Then, LLM-Optimation iteratively refines the graph based on node accuracy, edge consistency, and logical coherence. Finally, IstGPT integrated improved graph neural networks with an encoder-decoder architecture to detect anomalies via reconstruction errors. We evaluate IstGPT against 12 state-of-the-art baselines on 9 datasets, including 2 public, 6 simulated, and a real-world robotic arm dataset. IstGPT achieves the best F1-scores and eTaF1 (a newer time-aware metric) across nine datasets. We further discuss the feasibility of deploying IstGPT in real-world industrial scenarios.

</details>


### [233] [SECUREVENT: Hybrid AI/ML Security Monitoring for Distributed Event-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01741)
*Eric Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SECUREVENT，一种用于分布式事件系统（如发布/订阅、物联网遥测、云原生微服务）的混合AI/ML安全监控架构，结合传统保护（认证传输、主题授权、签名事件）与在线异常检测、图感知行为特征、复杂事件处理策略、联邦学习和对抗ML治理，在合成攻击流上验证了混合AI/CEP监控相比静态规则能提升召回率并保持低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式事件系统（发布/订阅、IoT、微服务、安全运营管道）的松耦合和异步特性在提升可扩展性的同时扩大了攻击面——发布者、代理、订阅者、主题、模式、时序关系等均可被单独滥用，而单一组件无法观测全局行为，传统静态控制规则难以应对动态变化的事件流。

Method: 混合AI/ML安全监控架构，包括：1) 传统保护层（认证传输、主题授权、签名事件）；2) 在线异常检测；3) 图感知行为特征提取；4) 复杂事件处理(CEP)策略规则；5) 联邦学习；6) 对抗ML治理。将AI/ML模型与CEP规则相结合形成混合监控器。

Result: 在合成事件流攻击上进行的确定性原型研究表明，混合AI/CEP监控器相比纯静态规则能提升召回率，同时保持较低的假阳性率。

Conclusion: 机器学习并非要取代密码学或访问控制机制，而是当事件流、身份、模式和时序关系过于动态以至于静态控制无法单独应对时，基于模型的安全监控是必要的。

Abstract: Distributed event-based systems have become a common substrate for Internet-scale publish/subscribe services, IoT telemetry, cloud-native microservices, and security operations pipelines. Their loose coupling and asynchronous delivery improve scalability, but they also expand the attack surface: publishers, brokers, subscribers, topics, schemas, and temporal ordering can each be abused without a single component observing the whole behavior. This paper proposes SECUREVENT, a hybrid AI/ML security-monitoring architecture for distributed event-based systems. The architecture combines traditional protections such as authenticated transport, topic-level authorization, and signed events with online anomaly detection, graph-aware behavioral features, complex-event policy rules, federated learning, and adversarial-ML governance. A deterministic prototype study over synthetic event-stream attacks illustrates how a hybrid AI/CEP monitor can improve recall over static rules while retaining a low false-positive rate. The central claim is not that machine learning replaces cryptographic and access-control mechanisms, but that model-based security monitoring is necessary when event flows, identities, schemas, and timing relationships are too dynamic for static controls alone.

</details>


### [234] [Tridirectional Discriminating-Power Formal Verification of Smart Contract Reentrancy Defense Against Production-Deployed Solidity Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01794)
*Ray Iskander*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对OpenZeppelin重入锁模式在Lean 4形式化验证框架下完成了机器检查的正确性证明，覆盖了DAO 2016、Compound v2和Aave V3三个生产级合约实例，所有13个定理均以零公理（除propext外）通过CI验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约重入攻击自2016年以来已造成超过5亿美元损失（如DAO攻击导致360万ETH被盗并迫使以太坊硬分叉）。OpenZeppelin ReentrancyGuard是DeFi生产环境的事实标准防御方案，但此前没有任何工作系统性地证明其区分能力——即该守卫能否同时做到：阻挡对易受攻击实例的攻击、保持对非攻击交易的正确执行、以及区分相邻的安全与易受攻击变体。

Method: 基于Lean 4构建状态机模型，对生产级Solidity源码进行形式化验证。采用三重验证方向：攻击重演（DAO 2016模式）、正确性证明（Compound v2）、边界区分证明（Aave V3的CEI正确版与突变体flashLoanVulnerable）。通过突变测试孤立一个安全关键差异，并设计了无改造（no-retrofit）原则下的元定理进行跨协议压力测试。

Result: 所有13个定理在Lean 4中完成机器检查，零sorry、零用户引入公理，公理足迹仅限propext（mathlib4标准公理），全部在CI下可复现。成功验证了ReentrancyGuard对三种生产实例的区分能力，包括首次跨协议压力测试（Compound v2到Aave V3）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为生产级Solidity合约的重入防御模式提供了双向（攻击阻断+正确执行）+边界情况（区分安全与易受攻击变体）的形式化正确性证明，填补了此前只验证玩具合约或孤立攻击可行性的空白。无改造原则保证了验证结果的可移植性，更广泛的协议族兼容性留作未来工作。

Abstract: We present the first machine-checked correctness proof of the OpenZeppelin reentrancy-guard pattern against a Lean 4 state-machine model of production-deployed Solidity source. All thirteen theorems are machine-checked with zero sorry, zero user-introduced axioms, and an axiom footprint bounded by [propext] (a standard mathlib4 axiom), gated under continuous integration.
  Smart contract reentrancy has caused over US$500M in documented losses since 2016, with the DAO 2016 attack draining ~3.6M ETH and forcing the hard fork that split Ethereum. The OpenZeppelin ReentrancyGuard pattern is the de facto defense across production DeFi, yet no prior work has established its discriminating power: that the guard blocks attacks on vulnerable instances, preserves correct execution for non-attacking transactions, and distinguishes adjacent safe and vulnerable variants. Prior efforts formalized either guard correctness on toy contracts or attack feasibility on isolated instances - not both directions plus boundary cases against production source.
  We verify three production instantiations - DAO 2016, Compound v2, and Aave V3 flashLoan - plus a minimal-diff mutant of Aave V3's flashLoan (flashLoanVulnerable) isolating one security-critical difference, via mutation testing. The tridirectional structure pairs (a) attack reproduction of the DAO 2016 pattern, (b) a correctness proof for Compound v2, and (c) a boundary-case proof distinguishing Aave V3's CEI-correct flashLoan from the mutant. A capstone meta-theorem composes the three under a no-retrofit discipline, demonstrated at the first cross-protocol stress test (Compound v2 to Aave V3); broader-family portability is future work.
  Full Lean 4 source, CI config and reproduction commands are at https://github.com/rayiskander2406/qanary-contracts, reproducible at v1.6-phase7-closure (substrate: v1.3-layer6-closure).

</details>


### [235] [Benign Inputs, Harmful Outputs: Cross-Modal Jailbreaking via Distributed Semantic Recomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01837)
*Yani Wang,Yilong Yang,Yang Liu,Zhuzhu Wang,Zuobin Ying,Zhuo Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出分布式语义重组(DSR)框架，通过将有害意图分解为良性文本和视觉基元，利用多模态大模型的推理能力在跨模态推理阶段实现潜在融合，从而绕过现有的安全防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防护主要针对单模态文本拦截，易受跨模态越狱攻击；且传统攻击包含显式有害内容易被检测，需要更隐蔽的攻击范式。

Method: 提出分布式语义重组(DSR)框架，将有害意图分解为多个看似无害的文本和视觉基元，利用模型自身的推理能力在跨模态推理阶段实现潜在融合，从而在不触发输入级安全过滤器的情况下产生有害输出。

Result: 在多个商业多模态大语言模型流水线上实现高攻击成功率，同时保持极低甚至可忽略的输入毒性率。

Conclusion: 揭示了MLLMs中的效用-安全悖论：模型越强的指令遵循能力反而越容易被利用进行认知攻击，现有安全机制存在根本性缺陷。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have recently demonstrated remarkable capabilities in content synthesis and autonomous reasoning. Previous safety guardrails are primarily designed for unimodal textual input interception, leaving them vulnerable to cross-modal jailbreak attacks. However, regardless unimodal textual attack or cross-modal jailbreak, typically inclusive part of explicit harmful or sensitive content at the input level, which is called Harm-Bearing. It allow the model's safety filters to detect and block such content easily. To address this limitations, we propose Distributed Semantic Recomposition (DSR), a novel cross-modal jailbreak framework that decomposes harmful intent into a set of benign textual and visual primitives. By exploiting the model's reasoning ability, DSR enables the latent fusion of these seemingly innocent components into harmful outputs during the cross-modal inference phase. Extensive experiments on multiple commercial MLLMs pipelines demonstrate that DSR achieves superior attack success rates while maintaining an extremely low or even negligible input toxicity rate. Our findings uncover a critical Utility-Safety Paradox in MLLMs, where the model's instruction-following proficiency facilitates its own cognitive exploitation. Content Warning: This paper contains harmful model responses.

</details>


### [236] [Implementation and Optimization of HQC Decoding on NPU-Integrated Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01968)
*Vu Minh Chau,Nguyen Ngoc Kiet,Pham Quang Minh,Mai Xuan Ngoc,Nguyen Duc Anh,Hoang Ta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究在集成NPU的骁龙Hexagon处理器上高效部署HQC后量子密码解码，通过将核心解码核重新设计为HVX友好的向量化实现（包括向量化Reed-Muller Hadamard变换、有限域算术、综合征计算等），在Snapdragon 8 Gen 2平台上实现了高达18.13倍的能效提升，显著减轻了主CPU负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HQC被NIST选为标准化候选后量子加密机制，但其解码过程（尤其是Reed-Muller和Reed-Solomon组件）计算开销大，在移动和嵌入式平台上高效部署需要精心优化。

Method: 针对Qualcomm Hexagon处理器的HVX后端，将HQC解码中自然暴露的向量化计算结构（可靠性向量、Hadamard变换系数、综合征向量、有限域乘积、支撑点评估等）重新设计为HVX友好的数据布局和执行模式，包括向量化Reed-Muller Hadamard变换、标量等效峰值选择、HVX导向有限域算术、向量化综合征计算和缩短支撑定位子根评估。

Result: 基于Hexagon模拟器和Snapdragon 8 Gen 2硬件开发套件的真实设备实验表明，Hexagon/HVX辅助解码显著降低了延迟和能耗，能效提升高达18.13倍，并大幅卸载了主CPU工作负载。

Conclusion: 当底层核心核被重新设计以适应向量执行时，集成NPU的移动平台可以作为结构化后量子密码解码的有效后端。

Abstract: Hamming Quasi-Cyclic (HQC) has been selected by NIST for standardization as an additional code-based key-encapsulation mechanism, providing algorithmic diversity alongside lattice-based post-quantum cryptography. Efficient deployment of HQC on mobile and embedded platforms, however, requires careful optimization of its decoding procedure, whose Reed-Muller and Reed-Solomon components dominate the computational cost. This paper studies HQC decoding on Qualcomm Hexagon processors in NPU-integrated devices, focusing on the Hexagon Vector eXtensions (HVX) backend rather than a tensor-inference engine. We observe that HQC decoding naturally exposes vector-structured computation, including Reed-Muller reliability vectors, Hadamard-transform coefficients, Reed-Solomon syndrome vectors, finite-field products, and packed support-point evaluations. Based on this observation, we redesign the dominant decoding kernels around HVX-friendly data layouts and execution patterns, including a vectorized Reed-Muller Hadamard transform, scalar-equivalent peak selection, HVX-oriented finite-field arithmetic, vectorized syndrome computation, and shortened-support locator-root evaluation. We implement and evaluate the optimized decoder using both Hexagon simulator measurements and real-device experiments on a Snapdragon~8 Gen~2 hardware development kit. The results show that Hexagon/HVX-assisted decoding substantially reduces latency and energy consumption, improving energy efficiency by up to $18.13\times$ while significantly offloading host CPU work. These results indicate that NPU-integrated mobile platforms can serve as effective backends for structured post-quantum cryptographic decoding when the underlying kernels are reformulated around vector execution.

</details>


### [237] [PeAR: A Static Binary Rewriting Framework for Binary-Only Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02126)
*Alvin Charles,Adrian Herrera,Peter Oslington,Alwen Tiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PeAR框架，证明基于静态二进制插桩（SBI）的二进制only模糊测试在准确性和可扩展性上可与动态二进制插桩（DBI）媲美，并在FUZZBENCH基准上实现4倍吞吐量提升和88%的目标覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二进制only模糊测试主要依赖动态二进制插桩（DBI），因其被认为比静态二进制插桩（SBI）更准确和可靠。但DBI存在运行时开销问题。本文旨在证明SBI的准确性、可扩展性及实用性，挑战学界对SBI的固有偏见。

Method: 基于现有商业级二进制重写框架实现精确可扩展的SBI，开发PeAR可扩展框架，集成延迟初始化、持久模式、共享内存模糊测试等现代特性，通过大规模实验验证性能。

Result: 88%的FUZZBENCH目标成功插桩；持久模式与共享内存下中位吞吐量提升4倍；覆盖率与基于编译器的插桩相当。

Conclusion: SBI是二进制only模糊测试中实用且有效的技术，现代二进制重写框架能够以高粒度和可忽略的性能代价实现复杂插桩，可用于构建高性能二进制only模糊器。

Abstract: Binary-only fuzzing is a key technique for finding bugs in close-source software. Without access to source code, the fuzzer must rely on static or dynamic binary instrumentation for coverage guidance. In practice, most fuzzers favor dynamic binary instrumentation (DBI), accepting runtime overhead to avoid the perceived accuracy and soundness challenges associated with static binary instrumentation (SBI). We show that these concerns are unwarranted, and that accurate, scalable~SBI is achievable using off-the-shelf frameworks. Building on these frameworks, we develop PeAR, an extensible binary-only fuzzing framework. We demonstrate PeAR's versatility by implementing several modern fuzzer features -- including, deferred initialization, persistent mode, and shared-memory fuzzing.
  We evaluate PeAR over 4.25 CPU-yrs of fuzzing on the FUZZBENCH benchmark and find that PeAR: (i) successfully instruments 88% of FUZZBENCH targets, comparable to the best SBI-based fuzzers; (ii) achieves a median throughput improvement of 4x when using persistent mode and shared memory fuzzing; and (iii) attains coverage comparable to compiler-based instrumentation.
  Our results show that SBI is a practical and effective technique for binary-only fuzzing, and that modern binary rewriting frameworks can apply complex instrumentation with high granularity and negligible performance compromise.

</details>


### [238] [The Unicity Execution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02181)
*Ahto Buldas,Dirk Draheim,Mike Gault,Risto Laanoja,Vladimir Rogojin,Ahto Truu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Unicity执行层，一个实现安全离线交易并保持去信任双重支付防护的模块化框架。通过基于公钥的令牌所有权模型和数字签名，证明了三个核心安全属性：无双重支付、无阻塞和服務端隐私。同时引入广义多公钥签名方案，实现了用户端隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链系统在处理离线交易时面临双重支付风险与隐私保护的矛盾，亟需一种既能保障交易安全性（防止双重支付）又能兼顾用户和服务端隐私的模块化解决方案。

Method: 采用形式化安全模型，以公钥表示令牌所有权并依赖数字签名进行转账验证。引入广义多公钥签名方案，允许一个秘密生成多个不可关联的公钥，并提供交互式和非交互式具体实例化方案。

Result: 形式化证明了三个安全属性：每个令牌状态最多被花费一次（无双重支付）、仅合法所有者可阻止令牌花费（无阻塞）、服务端无法关联同一令牌的交易（服务端隐私）。用户端隐私通过多公钥签名方案实现。

Conclusion: Unicity执行层在保持去信任双重支付防护的同时，实现了安全离线交易和隐私保护，尤其通过最小密钥管理开销支持稳定公共身份下的私有交易。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Unicity Execution Layer, a modular component of the Unicity framework enabling secure off-chain transactions while maintaining trustless double-spending prevention. We present a formal security model where token ownership is represented by public keys and transfers require digital signatures. We prove three fundamental security properties: (1) no double-spending--each token state can be spent at most once, (2) no blocking--only the legitimate owner can prevent a token from being spent, and (3) service-side privacy--the Unicity Service cannot link transactions with the same token. The user-side privacy is addressed by introducing generalized multi-public-key signature schemes that allow one secret to generate multiple unlinkable public keys, and interactive and non-interactive concrete instantiations, enabling private transactions with stable public identity with minimal key management overhead.

</details>


### [239] [Unicity: Predicates and Atomic Swaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02192)
*Ahto Buldas,Dirk Draheim,Mike Gault,Risto Laanoja,Vladimir Rogojin,Ahto Truu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将Unicity代币所有权泛化为可编程支出条件（谓词），实现由依赖方而非共识参与者在链下执行的智能合约功能，并证明其安全属性在归约到谓词族不可伪造性时得以保持，同时展示了如何利用谓词实现无信任原子交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Unicity代币所有权模型，引入可编程支出条件（谓词），以支持链下执行的类智能合约功能，同时保持核心安全属性。

Method: 通过将代币所有权泛化为谓词（可编程支出条件），将执行从共识参与者转移到依赖方，并通过安全归约证明保持Unicity执行层的安全属性。

Result: 证明了在谓词族不可伪造性归约下，Unicity执行层的安全属性得以保持，并实现了无信任原子交换作为应用示例。

Conclusion: 谓词扩展为Unicity模型提供了灵活的可编程性，同时通过安全归约确保了安全性，原子交换示例验证了其实用性。

Abstract: We generalize Unicity token ownership to programmable spending conditions called predicates, enabling smart-contract like functionality executed off-chain directly by relying parties rather than by consensus participants. We prove that the security properties of the Unicity execution layer are preserved under reduction to predicate family unforgeability. To demonstrate the utility of the model, we show how to implement trustless atomic swaps by using predicates.

</details>


### [240] [PyFEX: Uncovering Evasive Python-based Threats via Resilient and Exhaustive Path Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02196)
*Meng Wang,Yue Ma,Majid Garoosi,Wenting Fan,Liwei Guo,Jianqiang Wang,Ali Abbasi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 PyFEX，一种针对 Python 生态系统中恶意软件（包括恶意 PyPI 包和编译二进制文件）的弹性强制执行引擎。它通过强制遍历所有条件分支绕过逃避检测、引入崩溃恢复机制合成假对象继续执行、采用路径合并缓解路径爆炸，并自动发现并调用潜伏的恶意函数。基于 PyFEX 构建的检测器 PyFEXScan 在已知恶意包和真实编译二进制文件上超越了现有工具，并在 PyPI 实时部署中发现 212 个先前未知的恶意包，累计下载量超过 91,648 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python 生态系统的快速扩张带来了双重威胁：攻击者既通过 PyPI 投毒实施软件供应链攻击，又构建跨平台恶意编译二进制文件。现有静态分析被运行时混淆和编译字节码所蒙蔽，动态分析则脆弱易被环境检测绕过且因依赖不满足而提前终止，亟需一种鲁棒且全面的分析方案。

Method: PyFEX 采用三大核心技术：(1) 强制分支执行——系统性地遍历所有条件分支以绕过运行时环境检测；(2) 弹性崩溃恢复——在运行失败时合成假对象满足操作，使分析得以继续；(3) 路径合并——控制路径爆炸。此外还包含自动入口识别机制，主动发现并调用未暴露的潜伏函数。

Result: 在已知恶意 PyPI 包和真实编译二进制文件上的评估显示，PyFEX 暴露出被现有最先进工具遗漏的关键恶意行为。在 PyPI 的实时部署中，PyFEXScan 发现了 212 个此前未知的恶意包，累计下载量超过 91,648 次。

Conclusion: PyFEX 证明了弹性、穷举式分析对于保障 Python 生态安全的必要性，填补了现有静态和动态分析工具在应对混淆、编译恶意代码和环境逃避方面的空白。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Python ecosystem has fueled two distinct but converging threats: adversaries increasingly target the software supply chain via the Python Package Index (PyPI), while also building evasive, cross-platform malicious binaries compiled from source code written in Python. Current program analysis techniques struggle to address this dual threat. Static analysis based tools are often blinded by runtime obfuscation and compiled bytecode, while dynamic analysis based ones are fragile, prone to evasion by environmental guardrails, and often terminates prematurely due to unsatisfied dependencies.
  To overcome these limitations, we present PyFEX, a resilient forced-execution engine. PyFEX explores a program's behavioral space systematically by forcing execution across all conditional branches to bypass evasion checks. To address the fragility of dynamic execution, it introduces a novel resilient crash recovery mechanism that synthesizes dummy objects to satisfy failed operations at the runtime, allowing analysis to proceed past fatal errors, and employs path merging to mitigate path explosion. PyFEX further incorporates an automated entry identification mechanism that proactively discovers and invokes dormant functions, exposing malicious logic hidden within uncalled APIs. To demonstrate the efficacy of this engine, we built PyFEXScan, a proof-of-concept malware detector built on top of PyFEX. Evaluated against both known malicious PyPI packages and real-world compiled binaries, PyFEX exposes critical behaviors missed by the existing state-of-the-art tools. In a live deployment on PyPI, PyFEXScan discovered 212 previously unknown malicious packages accounting for over 91,648 downloads, underscoring the necessity of resilient, exhaustive analysis for securing the Python ecosystem.

</details>


### [241] [AgentRedBench: Dynamic Redteaming and Integration-Aware Defense for LLM Agents over SaaS Integrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02240)
*Hiskias Dingeto,Will Leeney*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGENTREDBENCH，一个针对LLM智能体工具使用中间接提示注入攻击的动态LLM驱动红队基准测试，涵盖24种企业集成、215个攻击场景。同时发布了AGENTREDGUARD防御模型，将攻击成功率从69.9%降至2.4%，误报率仅0.37%，显著优于现有开源防护方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试对工具使用型智能体中的间接提示注入威胁评估不足：大多数仅覆盖少量集成且重复使用相同攻击载荷，开源防护模型基于聊天数据而非工具响应内容训练。需要更全面、动态的评估基准和专用防护模型。

Method: 构建动态LLM驱动的红队基准测试AGENTREDBENCH，包含215个微妙未授权场景（处于用户请求授权边界），覆盖24个企业集成、9个功能家族和5种攻击类型。同时训练AGENTREDGUARD防护模型，使用集成多样化的对抗性工具响应内容语料库。

Result: 在8个模型（Anthropic、OpenAI、Google）上，无防护时攻击成功率从32%（Claude Sonnet 4.6）到81%（Gemini 3 Flash）不等。AGENTREDGUARD将面板平均攻击成功率从69.9%降至2.4%，误报率仅0.37%，在跨集成和跨攻击类型的保留测试中均表现优异。

Conclusion: AGENTREDBENCH有效评估了间接提示注入威胁，AGENTREDGUARD在防护性能上大幅超越所有开源基线。代码、集成模式和防护模型已开源，场景集通过维护者中介渠道实现不可变版本控制以保持评估意义。

Abstract: Indirect prompt injection in tool-use agents is a concrete production threat: LLM agents read from integrations (third-party services such as Gmail, Salesforce, or Jira accessed through tool calls) whose response content the user neither writes nor controls. Existing benchmarks under-measure the threat: most cover only a handful of integrations with the same attack payload replayed across runs, and open-source guards are trained on chat-style data rather than tool-response content. We introduce AGENTREDBENCH, a dynamic LLM-driven redteaming benchmark of 215 subtle underspecified authorization (attacks at the boundary of what the user's request authorises) scenarios across 24 enterprise integrations in nine functional families and five attack types. Across an eight-model panel (Anthropic, OpenAI, Google), no-guard ASR (attack success rate) ranges from 32% (Claude Sonnet 4.6) to 81% (Gemini 3 Flash). To keep the scenario set out of training corpora and preserve headline ASR meaning over time, we release the codebase, integration schemas, and AGENTREDGUARD model openly; the canonical scenarios are evaluated through a maintainer-mediated channel with immutable versioning. We release AGENTREDGUARD alongside the benchmark: a guard trained on an integration-diverse corpus of adversarial tool-response content. AGENTREDGUARD cuts panel ASR from 69.9% to 2.4% at 0.37% false-positive rate, outperforming every open-source baseline with non-trivial detection (Llama Guard, PromptGuard 2, ProtectAI) on both axes. Cross-integration and cross-attack type holdouts both confirm the gain transfers beyond the training subset.

</details>


### [242] [SeClaw: Spec-Driven Security Task Synthesis for Evaluating Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02302)
*Hao Cheng,Changtao Miao,Tianle Song,Yin Wu,He Liu,Erjia Xiao,Junchi Chen,Xiaoyu Shi,Yichi Wang,Jing Yang,Taowen Wang,Jinhao Duan,Mengshu Sun,Peiyan Dong,Xuan Shen,Yang Cao,Renjing Xu,Kaidi Xu,Jindong Gu,Bo Zhang,Jize Zhang,Chenhao Lin,Philip Torr,Chao Shen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SeClaw是一个结合规范驱动的安全任务合成与执行评估的框架，用于评测自主LLM代理在状态化环境中的安全风险，覆盖资源、用户任务、环境和内在行为四类风险，支持轨迹感知的评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主LLM代理安全评估依赖人工构建任务、覆盖范围有限，且只关注最终结果而非执行过程中的不安全行为，缺乏可扩展和可控的评测方法。

Method: 提出SeClaw框架，包含两大部分：1) 规范驱动的安全任务合成（Spec-driven security task synthesis），从结构化风险规范中可扩展地构建安全任务；2) SeClaw Docker标准化测试环境，支持在不同安全风险场景下评估代理行为。采用轨迹感知的评估方法，不仅关注最终响应，还检测执行过程中的不安全行为。

Result: 构建了一个覆盖资源、用户任务、环境和内在代理行为四类风险的基准测试集，支持可复现的安全评估，能够衡量、诊断和比较自主LLM代理的安全故障。

Conclusion: SeClaw通过连接系统化任务合成与可复现安全评估，为自主LLM代理的安全评测提供了实用基础，代码已开源。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents increasingly operate in stateful environments where they access tools, files, memory, and external services. While such capabilities enable complex real-world workflows, they also introduce security risks that are difficult to capture with existing evaluations. Current agent security benchmarks often rely on manually curated tasks, provide limited coverage of emerging threats, and focus primarily on final outcomes rather than the execution processes that lead to unsafe behavior. We introduce SeClaw, a framework that combines specification-driven security task synthesis with execution-based security evaluation for Autonomous agents. Spec-driven security task synthesis enables scalable and controllable construction of security tasks from structured risk specifications, while SeClaw docker provides a standardized testbed for evaluating agent behavior under diverse safety-risk scenarios. The benchmark covers risks arising from resources, user tasks, environments, and intrinsic agent behaviors, and supports trajectory-aware assessment of unsafe actions beyond final responses. By bridging systematic task synthesis and reproducible security evaluation, SeClaw provides a practical foundation for measuring, diagnosing, and comparing security failures in autonomous LLM agents. The code is available at https://github.com/seclaw-eval/seclaw-eval.

</details>


### [243] [I-(OT)^2: A Client-optimal Oblivious Transfer Protocol for IoT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02344)
*Elia Onofri,Andrea Ciccotelli,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出I-(OT)²，一种基于二次剩余困难问题的新型基础1-out-of-2茫然传输协议，通过轻量级离线预计算将在线计算负担几乎完全转移至发送方，在接收方计算最小化和低交互性方面表现优异，尤其适用于IoT等低功耗设备。实验表明，在128位安全强度下，接收方在线开销低至2.80μs（桌面）和39.90μs（IoT），比SimplestOT快10倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OT扩展协议虽降低了分摊成本，但依赖于大量随机基础OT和预计算阶段，在传输次数较少或通信延迟、客户端计算受限的场景下实用性不足。因此，高效的基础OT协议仍然必要且相关。

Method: 基于二次剩余困难问题（Quadratic Residuosity Problem）构造1-out-of-2 OT协议；采用轻量级离线预计算阶段，将在线传输计算负担几乎全部转移至发送方；在线通信仅需6条消息和4个摘要。

Result: 在128位安全强度、3072位RSA模数下，接收方在线平均单次OT开销：桌面平台2.80μs，IoT设备39.90μs，比SimplestOT快10倍以上。

Conclusion: I-(OT)²在接收方计算和通信效率上具有显著优势，适合客户端-服务器架构中接收方为低功耗硬件的场景（如IoT设备），并通过形式化安全证明和开源实现验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Oblivious Transfer (OT) is a fundamental cryptographic primitive enabling privacy-preserving computation and constitutes a core building block for secure multi-party computation while supporting a wide range of security-sensitive applications: private information retrieval, zero-knowledge proofs, and password-authenticated key exchange, to cite a few. While recent advances in OT extension have significantly reduced amortised costs, their reliance on batches of random base OTs and substantial pre-computation phases limits their practicality in scenarios where the number of transfers is modest or where communication latency and client-side computation are critical constraints. In such settings, efficient base OT protocols remain both relevant and necessary. In this work, we introduce $I$-$(OT)^2$, a novel base 1-out-of-2 OT protocol grounded in the quadratic residuosity problem, specifically designed to minimise receiver-side computation and interaction. Our construction is particularly appealing on client--server architectures in which the receiver operates on low-power hardware, such as Internet of Things (IoT) devices. Through a lightweight offline pre-computation phase, $I$-$(OT)^2$ shifts the on-transfer computational burden almost entirely to the Sender, while reducing online communication to only six messages and four digests exchanged. We provide a detailed description of the protocol, accompanied by a formal proof of its security. Moreover, to demonstrate the viability of $I$-$(OT)^2$, we also present an open-source proof-of-concept implementation (in C language) evaluated on real IoT hardware. Results are staggering: for 128-bit security using a 3072-bit RSA modulus, the receiver incurs an average online cost per OT as low as 2.80 μs on desktop platforms and 39.90 μs on IoT devices, more than 10$\times$ faster than the well known SimplestOT.

</details>


### [244] [Ghost Tool Calls: Issue-Time Privacy for Speculative Agent Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02483)
*Bardia Mohammadi,Lars Klein,Akhil Arora,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 工具增强型语言代理在执行投机性工具调用时会泄露用户意图，论文提出了投机工具隐私契约作为一种运行时抽象，将"提交前被观测"作为独立的一类效应处理，实验表明只有发出时刻的策略才能减少信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型语言代理为隐藏延迟而投机性地发出未来可能需要的工具调用，但这些调用在代理尚未确认执行分支前就已将推断出的用户意图泄露给外部服务，且一旦发出即使后续分支被放弃也无法撤回，现有授权机制无法解决此问题。

Method: 提出投机工具隐私契约（Speculative Tool Privacy Contracts）这一运行时抽象，将"提交前的观测"作为与状态变更并列的一类一等效应进行处理；设计原型运行时，并在三个语料库上评估十二种策略。

Result: 投机性分发会增加观察者对用户意图的推断能力；事后过滤、只读限制和访问控制白名单都无法减少这种推断；只有发出时刻的策略（改变或抑制投机调用的参数或目标投影）才能减少信息泄露。

Conclusion: 现有事后或授权层面的机制无法解决投机调用导致的信息泄露问题，需要在调用发出的时刻通过策略干预参数或目标来保护隐私。

Abstract: Tool-augmented language agents speculatively issue likely future tool calls to hide latency, but those calls leak inferred user intent to external services before the agent commits to the branch. Every external observer that received the call retains the disclosure after the agent abandons the branch. Timing is the issue, not authorization: no commit-time cleanup, read-only restriction, or access-control allow-list unsends what an observer already holds. We call these invocations ghost tool calls and propose Speculative Tool Privacy Contracts, a runtime abstraction that treats observation before commitment as a first-class effect, distinct from state mutation. We implement the contracts in a prototype runtime and evaluate twelve policies across three corpora. Speculative dispatch increases what an observer can infer about user intent; post-hoc filters, read-only restrictions, and access-control allow-lists leave that inference intact; only issue-time policies that change or suppress the speculative call's argument or destination projection before dispatch reduce it.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [245] [Position Paper: Post-Solve Robustness in Decision Engines: Feasible Regions and Smoothness Under Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00002)
*Yi-Xiang Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有MILP求解器输出的名义最优解在部署时因参数扰动（成本、需求、资源可用性）而面临鲁棒性缺失问题，提出应构建一个“求解后鲁棒性检查层”，通过认证内逼近、概率鲁棒性估计、对抗鲁棒性边界和基于学习的预测解释，将鲁棒性作为决策引擎的一等输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中，MILP求解器输出的最优解在面临微小参数扰动时可能变得不可行或导致决策空间中断性跳跃，而现有优化流程缺乏对求解后鲁棒性的系统评估机制。

Method: 形式化定义了两个核心对象：参数空间中ε-近优可行邻域（评估解在扰动下保持可行且近优的范围）和决策空间中解的光滑性（评估具有小组合编辑的邻近备选方案是否仍有竞争力）。综合敏感性分析、稳定性分析、鲁棒优化、邻域搜索、对抗测试和基于学习的增强方法，提出统一的求解后鲁棒性检查层框架。

Result: 提出了四个具体可落实的方向：围绕当前解的认证内逼近、带校准不确定性的概率鲁棒性估计、对抗鲁棒性边界、以及与求解器验证对齐的基于学习的预测与解释，并给出紧凑的报告模板和评估协议。

Conclusion: 求解后鲁棒性应是优化管线中缺失的关键层，也是评估学习驱动决策系统的新维度，应作为决策引擎的一等输出而非事后分析。

Abstract: Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) decision engines routinely output nominally optimal plans for high-stakes industrial systems. Yet deployment rarely matches solve-time assumptions: small perturbations in costs, demands, or resource availability can invalidate feasibility or trigger discontinuous shifts to qualitatively different solutions. We argue that this post-solve robustness gap is a missing layer in today's optimization pipelines and a missing evaluation dimension for learning-enabled decision systems. Rather than replacing robust optimization or stochastic programming, the proposed layer audits a solved incumbent and returns solver-backed evidence about how far that solution can be trusted. We formalize two central objects: (i) an $ε$-near-optimal feasible neighborhood in parameter space, capturing when an incumbent remains feasible and near-optimal under perturbations, and (ii) solution smoothness in decision space, capturing whether nearby alternatives with small combinatorial edits remain competitive. We then synthesize the most relevant partial answers from sensitivity and stability analysis, robust optimization, neighborhood search, adversarial testing, and learning-based enhancements, and articulate an agenda for a unified post-solve robustness layer. Concretely, we call for certified inner approximations around the incumbent, probabilistic robustness estimation with calibrated uncertainty, adversarial robustness margins, and learning-based prediction and explanation aligned with solver-backed verification. We conclude with a compact reporting template and evaluation protocol that would make robustness a first-class output of decision engines.

</details>


### [246] [Emergent Collaborative Deliberation in Multi-Model AI Systems: A BFT-Derived Protocol for Epistemic Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00005)
*VD Doske*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Consilium协议是一种基于拜占庭容错架构的多模型AI deliberation协议，将模型间分歧视为认知信号而非错误。通过为语言模型分配认知角色（persona）并引入样本内/样本外验证框架，实验表明：认知角色而非底层模型决定认知行为（0.0002美元廉价模型与10.69美元前沿模型输出质量相当）；RLHF训练在特定领域产生认知盲区（争议政策话题挑战度比科学话题低12.3个百分点，AI安全话题存在11.6%的不对称偏差）；协议本身无方向性偏差；样本外证据检索验证239个主张（100%成功率），发现167个训练数据无法发现的盲点。总成本217美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI deliberation方法将模型间分歧视为错误或噪声，缺乏系统性框架来利用分歧作为认知信号。同时，RLHF对齐训练可能引入未预期的认知偏差，亟需一种结构化的方法来检测和利用这些偏差，而非简单掩盖。

Method: 基于拜占庭容错（BFT）架构设计Consilium协议，核心包括：(1) 为LLM分配认知角色（cognitive persona），将模型能力与推理角色解耦；(2) 引入金融领域的样本内/样本外（In-Sample/Out-of-Sample）验证框架，区分训练数据共识与真实结论；(3) 在32个主题10个领域共1,478场讨论会话中，使用随机化模型×角色分配进行实验。

Result: (1) 认知角色主导认知行为，廉价模型（$0.0002）可与前沿模型（$10.69）产出相当分析结果；(2) RLHF在争议政策话题上挑战度降低12.3pp，AI安全话题呈现11.6%不对称偏差；(3) 协议自身无方向性偏差（移民Δ=2.3%，可再生能源Δ=1.2%）；(4) 样本外检索验证239个主张（100%成功率），发现167个盲点；(5) 重复性标准差±2.2%，总成本217美元。

Conclusion: Consilium协议提供了一种低成本、可重复、可验证的AI deliberation框架，能够系统性检测和利用LLM的认知偏差。实验证实认知角色与底层模型分离这一核心设计有效，RLHF对齐训练确实引入领域特异性认知盲区。开源MIT协议许可促进独立验证。

Abstract: We present the Consilium Protocol, a Byzantine Fault Tolerance-derived architecture for structured multi-model AI deliberation that treats inter-model disagreement as epistemic signal rather than error. The protocol assigns engineered cognitive personas to language models -- separating what a model is from how it reasons -- and introduces an In-Sample/Out-of-Sample validation framework adapted from quantitative finance to distinguish training-data consensus from empirically grounded conclusions. Across 1,478 deliberation sessions spanning 32 topics in 10 domain categories, we demonstrate that (1) the cognitive persona, not the underlying model, determines epistemic behavior: free edge-inference models costing 0.0002 USD per batch produced comparable analytical output to frontier models costing 10.69 USD; (2) RLHF alignment training creates measurable, domain-specific epistemic blind spots -- contested policy topics exhibit 12.3 percentage points less adversarial challenge than settled science topics, and AI safety topics show asymmetric bias ($Δ$=11.6%) where models challenge claims that AI is dangerous far more vigorously than claims that AI risk is overstated; (3) the protocol exhibits no directional bias of its own (immigration $Δ$=2.3%, renewables $Δ$=1.2%); and (4) out-of-sample evidence retrieval validated 239 claims with 100% evidence retrieval and surfaced 167 blind-spot discoveries invisible to training-data deliberation. Run-to-run reproducibility across randomized model$\times$persona assignments averages $\pm$2.2% standard deviation. Total cost for the complete battery including all overhead: 217 USD. We release the protocol specification under MIT license to enable independent verification.

</details>


### [247] [Deliberative Curation: A Protocol for Multi-Agent Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00007)
*Steven Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对AI代理在共享知识生态中的集体知识管理问题，提出了一种结合知识制品生命周期、声誉加权审议投票和分级制裁三层治理的审议策展协议，实验表明该协议在逆境下韧性显著优于多数投票机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理正从孤立工具演变为共享知识生态中的协作参与者，亟需有效的集体知识策展治理机制。传统人类平台治理机制（如威慑制裁、群体智慧依赖的独立性假设、协商共识）因代理无状态性、模型同质性和谄媚行为而无法直接迁移。

Method: 提出三层治理的审议策展协议：(1) 知识制品生命周期形式化为标记转换系统；(2) 声誉加权审议投票，结合Beta声誉和EigenTrust放大；(3) 针对无状态代理的分级制裁，包括区分故障与对抗行为的故障代理处理。通过100个代理的七类行为原型和两种逆境场景的基于代理的模拟评估。

Result: 协议在良性条件下精度略有牺牲，但在逆境下韧性显著提升：中度逆境下0.826 vs 0.791（多数投票），p<0.001；高压逆境下0.807 vs 0.740，p<0.001。协议性能衰减速度约为多数投票的1/3。消融分析表明提交-揭示投票隐藏机制是最有效的单个组件（精度提升8.2-8.6pp，p<0.001）。分级制裁在模拟中未被触发。

Conclusion: 提出的审议策展协议在集体知识策展中实现了精度与韧性的有效平衡，特别在逆境条件下优势显著。提交-揭示投票隐藏机制是关键设计要素，而分级制裁的有效性仍需进一步实证验证。

Abstract: As AI agents transition from isolated tools to collaborative participants in shared knowledge ecosystems, governing collective knowledge curation becomes a critical challenge. Human platform governance mechanisms do not transfer directly: agent statelessness undermines deterrence-based sanctions, model homogeneity violates independence assumptions underlying crowd wisdom, and sycophancy collapses deliberative consensus.
  We propose a deliberative curation protocol combining three governance layers: (1) a knowledge artifact lifecycle formalized as a labeled transition system; (2) reputation-weighted deliberative voting integrating Beta Reputation with EigenTrust amplification; and (3) graduated sanctions adapted for stateless agents, including broken agent handling distinguishing malfunction from adversarial behavior.
  We evaluate the protocol through agent-based simulation with 100 agents across seven behavioral archetypes under two adversity scenarios (30 seeds, paired t-tests). The protocol trades modest precision under benign conditions for substantially better resilience under adversity: 0.826 vs 0.791 for majority vote under moderate adversity (p<0.001), widening to 0.807 vs 0.740 under stress (p<0.001). The protocol degrades roughly three times more slowly than majority vote. Ablation analysis identifies commit-reveal vote concealment as the most impactful single component (8.2-8.6pp precision improvement, p<0.001), outperforming reputation weighting and deliberation combined. Graduated sanctions were not exercised in simulation and remain empirically unvalidated.

</details>


### [248] [Agents on a Tree: Pathwise Coordination for Multi-Objective Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00008)
*Jia Zhang,Tengfei Ma,Tianle Li,Daojian Zeng,Xieping Gao,Xiangxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ATOM多智能体框架，将分子优化建模为树状搜索，每个节点对应原子操作并配备专用智能体，通过路径协调实现多目标分子优化，在多个基准上取得更好的帕累托覆盖和超体积指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖单一策略或固定标量化，难以表示多样化权衡和探索多个有前景的设计轨迹，需要能处理长期依赖和冲突目标的分子优化方法。

Method: 提出ATOM框架，将分子优化构建为树状搜索：每个节点对应原子操作并部署针对特定目标或决策上下文的专用智能体；智能体沿树的不同路径协调而非全局共识；维护全局记忆以平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在包含活性、可合成性、ADMET相关属性的多目标基准上，ATOM在帕累托覆盖和超体积指标上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 路径式多智能体协调能有效处理分子设计中的长期依赖关系，是分子多目标优化的有效范式。

Abstract: Multi-objective molecular optimization requires searching vast chemical spaces under conflicting objectives, where early design decisions strongly constrain downstream outcomes. Existing methods typically rely on a single policy or fixed scalarization, which limits their ability to represent diverse trade-offs and to explore multiple promising design trajectories. We propose ATOM, a multi-agent framework that formulates molecular optimization as a tree-structured search. Each node corresponds to an atomic operation and hosts an agent specialized for a particular objective or decision context. Agents coordinate along different paths of the tree rather than enforcing a global consensus, enabling the method to maintain and compare alternative molecular evolution trajectories. A global memory of past optimization behaviors further supports balanced exploration and exploitation across objectives. This tree-structured interaction enables reasoning over long-horizon dependencies inherent in molecular design. Experiments on challenging multi-objective benchmarks involving activity, synthesizability, and ADMET-related properties show that ATOM consistently achieves improved Pareto coverage and hypervolume over strong baselines. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of pathwise multi-agent coordination for molecular optimization. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ATOM-41CE.

</details>


### [249] [Optimal Transport-based Permutation-Invariant Bayesian Optimization of Offshore Wind Farm Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00009)
*Antonio Candelieri,Laurens Bliek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输理论的排列不变贝叶斯优化方法（PIBO），用于解决布局优化问题（如海上风电场布局），该方法利用目标函数的排列对称性，在提高优化效果的同时将计算时间减少约一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准贝叶斯优化无法利用目标问题中存在的对称性（如布局优化中点的顺序不影响目标函数值），导致计算效率低下。

Method: 基于最优传输理论，提出排列不变贝叶斯优化（PIBO）方法，通过引入排列不变性来利用布局问题的对称结构。

Result: 与标准贝叶斯优化相比，PIBO能够提供更好的海上风电场布局方案，同时将计算时间减少约一半。

Conclusion: PIBO有效利用问题对称性，在布局优化场景中优于标准BO方法，具有显著的性能提升和计算效率改进。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely and successfully adopted for solving optimization problems having an expensive-to-evaluate, black-box, and non-convex objective function. However, the vanilla BO algorithm is not able to exploit possible symmetries characterizing the target problem. An intuitive case is given by optimal location problems, whose decision variables refer to a finite set of points within a continuous space, with the order of points not affecting the value of the objective function. We refer to this setting as optimization over layouts to distinguish from optimization over point-clouds where, instead, the order of points counts. As an instance of optimization over layouts we consider a real-life industrial-relevant application, that is the optimization of the layout of an offshore wind farm: given identical wind turbines, switching any pair of them has not any effect on the annual energy production. Based on Optimal Transport theory, we propose a Permutation-Invariant BO approach, namely PIBO, proved to provide better wind farm layouts when compared to the vanilla BO approach while cutting computation time roughly in half.

</details>


### [250] [MindGames Arena Generalization Track: In2AI Solution with Delayed Per-Step Reward Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00017)
*Aliaksei Korshuk,Alexander Buyantuev,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多智能体战略交互场景的延迟逐步奖励归因与资格门控方法，结合异步回滚生成、课程式对手采样和多层分层批构建，使8B参数的开源模型在MindGames Arena基准测试中击败了GPT-5等更大规模的专有系统，夺得NeurIPS 2025双赛道冠军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习假设每一步都能分配奖励，但在多智能体战略交互中，动作质量依赖于未发生的未来事件、违反规则的行为或其他玩家的决策，导致奖励在时间和智能体维度上高度纠缠，现有方法无法有效处理这一问题。

Method: 提出延迟逐步奖励归因与资格门控：仅在回合结束时计算奖励，根据任务特定语义将奖励回溯到原始步骤，并排除缺乏有效依赖信息的步骤。结合vLLM连续批处理实现异步回滚生成、课程式对手采样和多层分层批构建。

Result: 在NeurIPS 2025 MindGames Arena基准测试中，单个8B参数开源模型在头对头对战中匹配或超越GPT-5等更大规模专有系统，夺得Open（无限制）和Efficient（≤8B参数）双赛道第一名。

Conclusion: 所提出的训练框架能够实现多智能体环境下稳定且样本高效的RL训练，证明了适度规模的开源模型通过合适的训练方法可以在复杂多智能体交互中超越大规模专有系统。

Abstract: Training language model agents for multi-agent strategic interaction presents a core difficulty: the quality of any action may depend on future events that never materialize, on moves that violate game rules, or on decisions made by other players. Standard reinforcement learning assumes that rewards can be assigned at each step, but this assumption fails in settings where outcomes are entangled across time and agents. We introduce delayed per-step reward attribution with eligibility gating, an episode lifecycle and postprocessing pipeline that computes rewards only at episode end, propagates them back to originating steps according to task-specific semantics, and excludes steps that lack valid dependent information from training. Together with asynchronous rollout generation via vLLM's continuous batching, curriculum-based opponent sampling, and multi-level stratified batch construction, this approach enables stable, sample-efficient RL training in multi-agent environments. We evaluate on the MindGames Arena benchmark at NeurIPS 2025, where a single 8-billion-parameter open-source model trained with our method matched or surpassed substantially larger proprietary systems, including GPT-5, in head-to-head play and took first place in both the Open (unrestricted) and Efficient (<=8B parameters) tracks.

</details>


### [251] [Universal Quantum Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00045)
*Sungyong Chung,Alireza Talebpour*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通用量子变压器（UQT），一种基于多量子比特系统物理属性的量子原生计算架构，通过在紧凑的5量子比特基底上利用参数化几何相位嵌入和SU(2)波干涉，实现了对循环模算术(Z₁₁)和非阿贝尔代数(S₄置换群)的精确确定性泛化，超越了经典注意力网络在收敛时的随机不稳定性。这一被称为"结晶化"的现象比著名的"grokking"现象更进一步，同时理论上绕过了经典自注意力的二次瓶颈，对数级压缩表示维度，并在IBM量子计算机上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典连续空间神经网络难以锁定精确的数学对称性（如模算术和非交换代数），为逼近这些离散逻辑规则依赖大量参数缩放，导致随机不稳定性和延迟泛化现象（grokking）。作者旨在构建一种量子原生计算架构，利用多量子比特系统的物理属性作为通用归纳偏置，实现精确的数学和代数推理。

Method: 提出通用量子变压器（UQT），完全依赖参数化几何相位嵌入和SU(2)波干涉，而非翻译经典神经机制。量子注意力电路运行于紧凑的5量子比特基底上，利用量子拓扑作为物理归纳偏置。

Result: UQT在循环模算术(Z₁₁)和非阿贝尔代数(S₄置换群)两类形式上实现了精确的确定性泛化，而经典注意力网络在收敛时表现出随机不稳定性。该架构理论上绕过了经典自注意力的二次瓶颈，对数级压缩表示维度，消除了经典网络的过度参数化，并在IBM量子计算机上验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 参数化量子拓扑被确立为精确人工智能的通用优越物理基底，提出的"结晶化"现象超越了经典的grokking现象，为量子原生机器学习提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: Classical continuous-space neural networks fundamentally struggle to lock into exact mathematical symmetries, such as modular arithmetic and non-commutative algebra. To approximate these discrete logical rules, they often rely on massive parameter scaling, resulting in stochastic instability even after delayed generalization phenomena known as grokking. Here, we introduce the Universal Quantum Transformer (UQT), a fundamentally novel, quantum-native computing architecture that uses the physical properties of multi-qubit systems as a universal inductive bias for exact mathematical and algebraic reasoning. Rather than translating classical neural mechanisms, our framework relies entirely on parameterized geometric phase embedding and $SU(2)$ wave-interference. We demonstrate that the quantum attention circuit, operating on a highly compact 5-qubit substrate, perfectly learns two highly distinct formal classes: cyclic modular arithmetic ($\mathbb{Z}_{11}$) and non-Abelian algebra (the $S_4$ permutation group). While classical attention-based networks exhibit stochastic instability at convergence, the UQT achieves mathematically exact, deterministic generalization. We refer to this phenomenon as crystallization: a step beyond the well-known phenomenon of grokking. Crucially, this framework yields massive computational and memory advantages by theoretically bypassing the quadratic bottleneck of classical self-attention, and by logarithmically compressing the required representation dimension to eliminate the massive over-parameterization inherent to classical networks. Finally, we deploy this architecture on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) hardware, proving its viability on current IBM Quantum computers. These results establish parameterized quantum topology as a universally superior physical substrate for exact artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [252] [Grokers: Bottom-Up Inductive Comprehension and Write-Time Intelligence over Typed Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00050)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Grokers是一种通过自底向上归纳遍历依赖子图来构建持久、结构化知识图谱理解的架构。它通过在写入时进行智能处理（使用Groker智能体分析节点、提取属性、归纳组合理解），使得后续所有查询在语义不变时无需LM调用，KV缓存命中率接近100%，且无LM调用解决交互的比例单调递增。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG方法每次查询都需要完整理解成本，效率低下。作者希望构建一种架构，将理解成本转移到写入阶段，使得后续查询可以零成本复用已有理解成果。

Method: 提出Grokers架构，包含：(1) Groker智能体在写入时自底向上遍历依赖子图分析节点，通过受控LM调用提取结构化属性并归纳组合；(2) 事务性维护的反规范化索引保证上下文块在语义变化前字节一致，实现KV缓存复用；(3) 受控知识库增长协议保证无LM调用解决交互的比例单调递增；(4) 确定性同义词缓存协议替代基于嵌入的语义搜索，LM回退率在有限词汇域趋近于零。

Result: 三个形式化定理：字节同一性定理（事务性索引保证字节一致，KV缓存命中率接近100%）、累积单调性定理（无LM调用解决交互比例随交互次数非递减）、双遍历序定理（自顶向下生成与自底向上理解分别是DAG上各自任务的唯一正确遍历序，二者组合形成完整的生成-理解循环）。确定性同义词缓存的LM回退率在有限词汇域趋近于零。

Conclusion: Grokers通过将理解成本转移到写入阶段，实现了高效的持久化知识图谱理解架构，形式化保证了缓存效率和单调递增的无LM调用比例，为知识密集型应用提供了RAG的替代方案。

Abstract: We present Grokers, an architecture for building persistent, structured comprehension of typed knowledge graphs through bottom-up inductive traversal of dependency subgraphs. Unlike retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), which pays full comprehension cost at every query, Grokers pushes intelligence to write time: autonomous Groker agents analyze nodes in a typed stream graph, extract structured attributes via governed language model (LM) calls, and inductively compose that understanding upward through dependency relations, writing enriched typed attributes that serve all future queries at zero additional LM cost. We prove three formal properties: (1) the Byte-Identity Theorem, establishing that context blocks assembled from a transactionally-maintained denormalization index are byte-identical across LM turns between semantic changes, enabling KV-cache hit rates approaching 100%; (2) the Accumulation Monotonicity Theorem, establishing that the fraction of interactions resolved without LM calls is non-decreasing in the number of completed interactions under a governed wisdom library growth protocol; and (3) the Dual-Traversal Ordering Theorem, establishing that top-down generation and bottom-up comprehension are the unique correct traversal orderings for their respective tasks over a dependency DAG, and that their composition closes into a complete generation-comprehension cycle. We further present a deterministic alternative to embedding-based semantic search, with a synonym caching protocol whose LM fallback rate converges to zero for finite-vocabulary domains. A reference implementation is provided in the open-source Qbix / Safebox / Safebots stack.

</details>


### [253] [Product-Aware Deep Autoencoders for Robust Process Monitoring in Multi-Product Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00052)
*MD Shafikul Islam,Jordan Carden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现工业4.0环境下，传统"产品无关"的全局异常检测模型在多产品等级生产中存在"盲区"漏洞，容易遗漏针对特定产品模式的隐蔽异常或网络物理攻击。为此提出一种"产品感知自编码器"方法，将学习域限制在特定产品等级的分布上。在扩展的田纳西-伊斯曼过程基准测试中验证表明，产品感知框架在标准检测指标上与全局基线相当，但显著提升了鲁棒性：在模拟攻击压力测试中，全局模型在77.8%的场景下未能检测到偏差，而产品感知系统实现了100%检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工业异常检测普遍采用全局模型，但在多产品柔性制造环境下，全局模型为了兼容多种运行模式会扩大决策边界，形成"盲区"，使针对特定产品模式的隐蔽异常或攻击无法被发现。

Method: 提出Product-Aware Autoencoder，将学习域约束到特定产品等级的分布上，而非全局聚合数据。在扩展的田纳西-伊斯曼过程(TEP)基准上，与Global Agnostic基线进行对比验证，并设计了模拟攻击的压力测试场景。

Result: 产品感知框架在标准检测指标上与全局基线表现相当，但显著提升了针对产品等级特定运行模式的鲁棒性。在压力测试中，全局模型77.8%场景检测失败，产品感知系统达到100%检测准确率。

Conclusion: 在柔性制造环境中，通用型异常检测器存在不可忽视的安全风险，应转向模式感知的诊断架构。

Abstract: As Industry 4.0 accelerates the integration of Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) in manufacturing, robust anomaly detection has become critical for ensuring process safety and security. Current data-driven approaches typically employ "product-agnostic" or global models trained on the aggregate of all normal operating data. However, modern industrial facilities frequently operate under diverse product grades. While computationally simple, these global models inherently expand their decision boundaries to accommodate the variance of multiple modes, creating a "blind spot" where subtle anomalies or targeted cyber-physical attacks may be masked by the wide acceptance region of the model. In this work, we first demonstrate that the vulnerability described above is present in global-agnostic models operating across multiple product grades. We then present a Product-Aware Autoencoder as a principled mitigation that restricts the learning domain to grade-specific distributions. While this approach reduces the identified blind-spot risk, we do not claim it as the optimal mitigation among all possible alternatives. We rigorously validate this approach against a Global Agnostic baseline using the Extended Tennessee Eastman Process (TEP) benchmark. Our empirical results indicate that the Product-Aware framework performs comparably to the global baseline on standard detection metrics, while offering improved robustness to product-grade-specific operating modes.
